### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/customers.py`

*267 lines · 1 function*

> 4. Customer CRM - everybody who pays you, and what they are worth.
>
> The Customer ID generated here is the first of the four join keys. A
> property belongs to a customer, a job happens at a property, and an
> invoice bills a customer for jobs. Get this ID wrong and the chain
> comes apart three tabs later with nothing to say why.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Customer CRM').

````python
"""4. Customer CRM - everybody who pays you, and what they are worth.

The Customer ID generated here is the first of the four join keys. A
property belongs to a customer, a job happens at a property, and an
invoice bills a customer for jobs. Get this ID wrong and the chain
comes apart three tabs later with nothing to say why.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, count_by, id_col,
                     is_active_customer, loss_cf, margin, money_cf,
                     owing_cf, rank_desc, rating_cf, scale_cf, status_cf,
                     sum_by, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Customer CRM"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    rows = cfg.row_count("customers")
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Customer CRM",
        subtitle="Everyone who pays you, what they have spent, and who "
                 "has gone quiet. One row per customer, never per visit.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary,
        rows=rows,
        samples=sample("customers", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "Add the customer here first. The Customer ID appears "
            "automatically and becomes available in every drop-down "
            "that needs it - properties, quotes, jobs, invoices. "
            "A household with two addresses is ONE customer with two "
            "properties, not two customers."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Lifetime value counts completed jobs only, so a quote "
            "nobody accepted never inflates what somebody looks worth.",
            "'Dormant' is worked out, not typed: it is how long since "
            "their last completed visit against the window on Settings.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("name", "CustomerPrefix", digits=cfg.digits("customer"),
                   header="Customer ID",
                   note="Generated. This is the join key - every property, "
                        "job and invoice points back at it."),
            Col("name", "Customer name", 24, "text",
                note="A household, a company or a managing agent. Start "
                     "typing here and the row comes alive."),
            Col("contact", "Main contact", 18, "text",
                note="Who you actually speak to."),
            Col("phone", "Phone", 15, "text"),
            Col("email", "Email", 26, "text"),
            Col("address", "Billing address", 30, "wrap",
                note="Where the invoice goes. The place you mow is a "
                     "Property, on the next tab."),
            Col("town", "Town / city", 16, "text"),
            Col("postcode", "Zip / postcode", 12, "text"),
            Col("type", "Customer type", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("CustomerTypeList"),
                note="Commercial work pays later and in bigger lumps. "
                     "Worth being able to split the two."),
            Col("source", "How they found you", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("LeadSourceList"),
                note="Fill this in for a year and you will stop paying "
                     "for the adverts that do not work."),
            Col("first_job", "Customer since", 13, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("terms", "Payment terms", 17, "text",
                validate=list_from("PaymentTermsList"),
                note="Drives the due date on every invoice to them."),
            Col("method", "Pays by", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("PaymentMethodList")),
            Col("status", "Status", 13, "text",
                validate=list_from("CustomerStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("rating", "You rate them", 11, "rating",
                validate={"validate": "integer", "criteria": "between",
                          "minimum": 1, "maximum": 5,
                          "error_title": "1 to 5",
                          "error_message": "How good are they to work "
                                           "for? 5 is a dream job, 1 is "
                                           "one you dread."},
                cf=rating_cf(),
                note="Pays on time, dog is friendly, gate is unlocked. "
                     "Your own judgement, for your own planning."),
            Col("po_required", "Needs a PO", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Does this customer require a purchase "
                               "order number before you can invoice?"),
                note="Commercial accounts often do, and an invoice "
                     "without one does not get paid."),
            Col("tax_exempt", "Tax exempt", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno()),
            Col("marketing_ok", "Marketing OK", 11, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Have they agreed to seasonal offers and "
                               "reminders?"),
                note="Ask once, record it, and respect it."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 34, "wrap",
                note="The things that are not a field: 'ring the bell, "
                     "do not use the side gate', 'always wants the "
                     "clippings left'."),

            # ---- what they are actually worth ------------------------
            Col("properties", "Properties", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Property Database",
                                              "customer", '{id}{r}')),
                note="Automatic. How many addresses you look after for "
                     "them."),
            Col("jobs_done", "Visits done", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                              "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_done", "1")),
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("jobs_open", "Visits booked", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                              "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_open", "1"))),
            Col("lifetime", "Lifetime revenue", 15, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Job & Work Orders", "charge",
                                            "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_done", "1")),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="Completed work only. This is the number that tells "
                     "you who you cannot afford to lose."),
            Col("revenue_year", "This year", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by(
                    "Job & Work Orders", "charge", "customer", '{id}{r}',
                    "is_done", "1",
                    "date_num", '">="&YearStart',
                    "date_num", '"<="&YearEnd')),
                cf=money_cf()),
            Col("cost_total", "Cost to serve", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Job & Work Orders", "cost",
                                            "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_done", "1"))),
            Col("profit", "Profit", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", 'ROUND({lifetime}{r}-'
                                     '{cost_total}{r},2)'),
                cf=loss_cf(),
                note="What they have actually left you with after the "
                     "crew, the materials and the drive."),
            Col("margin_pct", "Margin", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        f'{margin("{profit}{r}", "{lifetime}{r}")})',
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("avg_job", "Average visit", 13, "money",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({jobs_done}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({lifetime}{r}/{jobs_done}{r},2)))'),
            Col("contracts", "Contracts", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Recurring Contracts",
                                              "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_live", "1")),
                note="Live recurring agreements. A customer with one is "
                     "worth far more than one without."),
            Col("contract_value", "Contract / yr", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Recurring Contracts",
                                            "annual_value", "customer",
                                            '{id}{r}', "is_live", "1")),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="Booked recurring income. The number a buyer of "
                     "your business would look at first."),

            # ---- money owed ------------------------------------------
            Col("invoiced", "Invoiced", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Invoice & Payments", "total",
                                            "customer", '{id}{r}'))),
            Col("received", "Received", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Invoice & Payments", "paid",
                                            "customer", '{id}{r}'))),
            Col("owing", "Owing", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("name", 'ROUND({invoiced}{r}-'
                                     '{received}{r},2)'),
                cf=owing_cf()),
            Col("overdue", "Overdue", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Invoice & Payments",
                                            "outstanding", "customer",
                                            '{id}{r}', "is_overdue", "1")),
                cf=owing_cf(),
                note="Past the due date and still unpaid. Chase this "
                     "column before you chase anything else."),
            Col("oldest_due", "Days late", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", 'IF({overdue}{r}<=0,0,'
                                     'MAX(0,SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                                     '([[Invoice & Payments.customer]]='
                                     '{id}{r})*'
                                     '[[Invoice & Payments.days_late]]))))'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 30,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The worst one, not the average. A single 90 day "
                     "debt is a different conversation."),

            # ---- how they are getting on -----------------------------
            Col("last_job_num", "Last visit #", 11, "number",
                formula=zero("name", 'SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                                     '([[Job & Work Orders.customer]]='
                                     '{id}{r})*'
                                     '([[Job & Work Orders.is_done]]=1)*'
                                     '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]]))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("last_job", "Last visit", 12, "date",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({last_job_num}{r}<=0,"",'
                        '{last_job_num}{r}))',
                note="Automatic, from the job register."),
            Col("days_since", "Days since", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", 'IF({last_job_num}{r}<=0,0,'
                                     'TodayDate-{last_job_num}{r})'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=DormantDays", "fmt": "bad"}]),
            Col("rating_given", "Their rating", 11, "number1",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(AVERAGEIFS('
                        '[[Customer Feedback.score]],'
                        '[[Customer Feedback.customer]],{id}{r},'
                        '[[Customer Feedback.score]],">0"),""))',
                cf=rating_cf(),
                note="What they think of you, averaged from the feedback "
                     "tab. The other direction to the column above."),
            Col("complaints", "Complaints", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Customer Feedback",
                                              "customer", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_problem", "1")),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 2,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("is_active", "Active", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("name", f'IF({is_active_customer("{status}{r}")},'
                                     '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            rank_desc("lifetime", guard='{name}{r}=""',
                      note="1 = your most valuable customer. The "
                           "dashboard reads this to list them."),
            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("name", [
                    ('{overdue}{r}>0',
                     "Overdue - invoice past its due date, chase it"),
                    ('{complaints}{r}>=2',
                     "Urgent - more than one complaint logged, call them"),
                    ('AND({status}{r}="Lead",{jobs_done}{r}=0)',
                     "Follow up - a lead with no work done yet"),
                    ('{properties}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no property on file, so nothing can be booked"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{days_since}{r}>=DormantDays,'
                     '{jobs_open}{r}=0)',
                     "Dormant - no visit for a long time and nothing "
                     "booked"),
                    ('AND({contracts}{r}=0,{jobs_done}{r}>=4)',
                     "Action - a regular customer with no contract, ask "
                     "for one"),
                    ('AND({lifetime}{r}>0,{margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<MinMargin)',
                     "Thin margin - this customer earns less than your "
                     "floor"),
                    ('{owing}{r}>0', "Due - invoiced and not yet paid"),
                    ('{email}{r}=""', "Add - no email address on file"),
                    ('{terms}{r}=""', "Add - payment terms not set"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/properties.py`

*284 lines · 1 function*

> 5. Property Database - the ground you actually work on.
>
> A customer is a person who pays; a property is a place with a gate
> code, a slope, a dog and a lawn of a particular size. The two are
> separate because one customer can own six of them, and because every
> price in this workbook is driven by what is written here: lawn area,
> terrain, access and how far it is from the yard.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Property Database').

````python
"""5. Property Database - the ground you actually work on.

A customer is a person who pays; a property is a place with a gate
code, a slope, a dog and a lawn of a particular size. The two are
separate because one customer can own six of them, and because every
price in this workbook is driven by what is written here: lawn area,
terrain, access and how far it is from the yard.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, customer_col,
                     id_col,
                     lookup_customer, money_cf,
                     rank_desc, scale_cf, status_cf, sum_by, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Property Database"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Property Database",
        subtitle="Every address you maintain: the size of the lawn, how "
                 "to get in, what is in the way, and how far it is.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary,
        rows=cfg.row_count("properties"),
        samples=sample("properties", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "Pick the customer first, then describe the site. Lawn area "
            "is the single most useful number in the workbook - the "
            "quote calculator, the time estimate and the material "
            "quantities all come off it. Pace it out once and you never "
            "have to guess at that address again."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Measure the area you actually cut, not the plot size. A "
            "quarter acre plot with a house, a drive and a patio on it "
            "is rarely more than 6,000 sq ft of grass.",
            "Gate codes and alarm codes are sensitive. If this file is "
            "shared or stored in the cloud, leave those boxes empty and "
            "keep them somewhere your crew can reach and nobody else "
            "can.",
            "Difficulty multiplier is how this sheet earns its keep: a "
            "steep, tight, dog-occupied garden takes longer than a flat "
            "open one and should be priced accordingly.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("address", "PropertyPrefix",
                   digits=cfg.digits("property"), header="Property ID",
                   note="Generated. Jobs, quotes and contracts point at "
                        "this ID, not at the address text."),
            customer_col(note="Who pays for work at this address. Add "
                              "them on the Customer CRM tab first."),
            Col("customer_name", "Customer name", 22, "text",
                formula=lookup_customer("name"),
                note="Automatic. 'Not in Customer CRM' means the ID is "
                     "wrong."),
            Col("nickname", "Site name", 18, "text",
                note="What the crew calls it: 'the big one on Mill "
                     "Lane', 'Harper rear'. Optional but they will "
                     "thank you."),
            Col("address", "Address", 30, "wrap",
                note="Start typing here. The Property ID appears as "
                     "soon as you do."),
            Col("town", "Town / city", 16, "text"),
            Col("postcode", "Zip / postcode", 12, "text"),
            Col("type", "Property type", 18, "text",
                validate=list_from("PropertyTypeList")),
            Col("lawn_area", "Lawn area (sq ft)", 14, "int",
                validate=amount(
                    0, 2000000,
                    "Roughly how much grass do you cut here, in square "
                    "feet? An average suburban back lawn is 2,000 to "
                    "6,000."),
                note="The number everything is priced from. Measure once, "
                     "properly, and the quote calculator does the rest."),
            Col("beds_area", "Beds / borders (sq ft)", 15, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 2000000),
                note="Planted area. Drives mulch and weeding quantities."),
            Col("hedge_length", "Hedge (linear ft)", 13, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 100000)),
            Col("terrain", "Terrain", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("TerrainList"),
                note="Flat and open cuts faster than steep and awkward. "
                     "This feeds the difficulty multiplier."),
            Col("access", "Access", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("AccessList"),
                note="How the mower gets in. A narrow side gate means "
                     "the ride-on stays on the trailer."),
            Col("gate_code", "Gate / alarm code", 14, "text",
                note="Leave empty if this file is shared. Recording a "
                     "code here is a choice, not a requirement."),
            Col("dog", "Dog on site", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Is there a dog that may be loose in the "
                               "garden?"),
                note="The crew needs to know before they open the gate."),
            Col("pets_note", "Site hazards", 26, "wrap",
                note="Ponds, septic lids, irrigation heads, a bad step, "
                     "the neighbour's cat. Anything that costs money if "
                     "it gets hit."),
            Col("parking", "Parking", 18, "text",
                note="Where the truck and trailer actually fit."),
            Col("water", "Water on site", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno()),
            Col("power", "Power on site", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno()),
            Col("disposal", "Clippings", 16, "text",
                note="Leave on site, bag them, or take them away. Taking "
                     "them away is a cost - price it."),
            Col("miles", "Miles from yard", 12, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 2000,
                                      "One way distance from your yard "
                                      "or depot."),
                note="One way. The quote calculator charges the round "
                     "trip at the rate on Settings."),
            Col("drive_minutes", "Drive (mins)", 11, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 600),
                note="One way, in normal traffic. Blank uses the default "
                     "on Settings."),
            Col("difficulty", "Difficulty", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'ROUND(1'
                             '+IF({terrain}{r}="Steep slope",0.25,'
                             'IF({terrain}{r}="Gentle slope",0.1,'
                             'IF({terrain}{r}="Terraced",0.2,'
                             'IF({terrain}{r}="Rough / uneven",0.15,'
                             'IF({terrain}{r}="Wet / boggy",0.15,0)))))'
                             '+IF({access}{r}="Narrow side gate",0.15,'
                             'IF({access}{r}="Rear access only",0.1,'
                             'IF({access}{r}="Locked gate - code needed",'
                             '0.05,IF({access}{r}="Steps only",0.2,0))))'
                             '+IF({obstacles}{r}>=8,0.15,'
                             'IF({obstacles}{r}>=4,0.08,0))'
                             '+IF({dog}{r}="Yes",0.05,0),2)'),
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Worked out from terrain, access, obstacles and the "
                     "dog. 1.00 is an easy flat lawn; 1.50 takes half as "
                     "long again. The quote calculator multiplies by it."),
            Col("obstacles", "Trees / obstacles", 12, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 500,
                                      "Roughly how many things do you "
                                      "have to mow around?"),
                note="Trees, beds, play equipment, washing poles. Every "
                     "one of them is a turn."),
            Col("mowing_minutes", "Mow time (mins)", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'ROUND(MAX(10,{lawn_area}{r}/1000*'
                             'MowMinutesPer1000)*{difficulty}{r},0)'),
                note="An estimate from area and difficulty, for planning. "
                     "Actual times come from the timesheets."),
            Col("status", "Status", 13, "text",
                validate=list_from("PropertyStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("start_date", "Maintained since", 14, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("photo_before", "Photo - before", 22, "link",
                note="Paste a link to the photo in your cloud drive. The "
                     "workbook stores the link, not the picture."),
            Col("photo_after", "Photo - after", 22, "link",
                note="The pair of them is the best sales tool you own."),
            Col("map_link", "Map link", 22, "link",
                note="A link to this address in your map app, for the "
                     "crew. The workbook does not navigate."),
            Col("notes", "Site notes", 34, "wrap",
                note="'Cut the verge too', 'nobody home on Fridays', "
                     "'bins out Tuesday so park on the road'."),

            # ---- what happens here -----------------------------------
            Col("jobs_done", "Visits done", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("address", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                 "property", '{id}{r}',
                                                 "is_done", "1")),
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("jobs_open", "Visits booked", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("address", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                 "property", '{id}{r}',
                                                 "is_open", "1"))),
            Col("revenue", "Revenue", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("address", sum_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                               "charge", "property",
                                               '{id}{r}', "is_done", "1")),
                cf=money_cf()),
            Col("cost", "Cost", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("address", sum_by("Job & Work Orders", "cost",
                                               "property", '{id}{r}',
                                               "is_done", "1"))),
            Col("profit", "Profit", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'ROUND({revenue}{r}-{cost}{r},2)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "good"}]),
            Col("hours_actual", "Hours booked", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("address", sum_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                               "hours_actual", "property",
                                               '{id}{r}', "is_done", "1")),
                note="From the timesheets, via the jobs. Compare with "
                     "the mow time estimate - if they disagree, the "
                     "estimate is wrong."),
            Col("per_hour", "Earned per hour", 13, "money",
                formula='=IF({address}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_actual}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({revenue}{r}/'
                        '{hours_actual}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The honest test of a price. Below your charge-out "
                     "rate means this address is subsidised by the rest."),
            Col("contracts", "On contract", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("address", count_by("Recurring Contracts",
                                                 "property", '{id}{r}',
                                                 "is_live", "1"))),
            Col("last_num", "Last visit #", 11, "number",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.property]]={id}{r})*'
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.is_done]]=1)*'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]]))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("last_visit", "Last visit", 12, "date",
                formula='=IF({address}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({last_num}{r}<=0,"",{last_num}{r}))'),
            Col("next_num", "Next visit #", 11, "number",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'IF(COUNTIFS('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.property]],{id}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.is_open]],1)=0,0,'
                             'SUMPRODUCT(MIN(IF('
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.property]]={id}{r})*'
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.is_open]]=1)=1,'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]],99999))))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("next_visit", "Next visit", 12, "date",
                formula='=IF({address}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({next_num}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'IF({next_num}{r}>=99999,"",'
                        '{next_num}{r})))',
                note="The earliest booked visit still outstanding."),
            Col("days_since", "Days since", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'IF({last_num}{r}<=0,0,'
                             'TodayDate-{last_num}{r})'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=DormantDays", "fmt": "bad"}]),
            Col("is_active", "Active", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("address",
                             'IF({status}{r}="Active",1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            rank_desc("revenue", guard='{address}{r}=""'),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("address", [
                    ('{customer_name}{r}="Not in Customer CRM"',
                     "Check - the Customer ID does not exist"),
                    ('{lawn_area}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no lawn area, so nothing here can be priced"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{jobs_open}{r}=0,'
                     '{contracts}{r}=0)',
                     "Schedule - active site with nothing booked and no "
                     "contract"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{days_since}{r}>=DormantDays,'
                     '{jobs_done}{r}>0)',
                     "Dormant - a long time since the last visit here"),
                    ('AND({revenue}{r}>0,{profit}{r}<0)',
                     "Loss - this address costs more than it brings in"),
                    ('AND({hours_actual}{r}>0,{per_hour}{r}<>"",'
                     '{per_hour}{r}<ChargeRate)',
                     "Thin margin - earning under your hourly rate here"),
                    ('{miles}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no distance set, so travel is not being "
                     "charged"),
                    ('AND({jobs_done}{r}=0,{jobs_open}{r}=0)',
                     "Action - no work recorded at this property yet"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/services.py`

*256 lines · 1 function*

> 6. Service Catalog - your price list, and what each job really costs.
>
> Thirty services come filled in with a rate, a unit and a realistic
> time. Change every one of them: these are plausible numbers for a
> two-person crew in a mid-cost area, not a recommendation. What the
> sheet is really for is the column on the right - the margin each
> service earns once the crew, the materials and the overhead are paid.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Service Catalog').

````python
"""6. Service Catalog - your price list, and what each job really costs.

Thirty services come filled in with a rate, a unit and a realistic
time. Change every one of them: these are plausible numbers for a
two-person crew in a mid-cost area, not a recommendation. What the
sheet is really for is the column on the right - the margin each
service earns once the crew, the materials and the overhead are paid.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, loss_cf, margin,
                     money_cf, rank_desc, scale_cf, status_cf, sum_by,
                     yesno, zero)

NAME = "Service Catalog"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Service Catalog",
        subtitle="What you sell, what you charge for it, how long it "
                 "takes and what it leaves you with.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary,
        rows=cfg.row_count("services"),
        samples=sample("services", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "The service name is the join key - the quote calculator, "
            "the job register and the contracts all pick from this "
            "list, so renaming a service here renames it everywhere "
            "that has not been written yet, but NOT on rows already "
            "recorded. Add a new line instead of renaming an old one "
            "once you are trading."),
        extra_notes=[
            "'Unit' decides how the quote calculator reads the rate. "
            "Per 1,000 sq ft multiplies by the lawn area; per visit, "
            "per hour and per job do not.",
            "Minutes per unit is your own measured time, and it is the "
            "number that makes a quote honest. If the catalogue says "
            "35 minutes and the timesheets say 50, change the "
            "catalogue.",
            "Margin here is before overhead; the quote calculator adds "
            "overhead per crew hour on top. A service showing 55% here "
            "is nearer 35% once the yard, the insurance and the "
            "paperwork are paid for.",
        ],
        columns=[
            Col("name", "Service", 30, "text",
                note="The name your customers see on the quote. This is "
                     "the join key - keep it short and keep it stable."),
            Col("category", "Category", 18, "text",
                validate=list_from("ServiceCategoryList")),
            Col("description", "What it includes", 40, "wrap",
                note="Worth writing properly: this is what stops the "
                     "'I thought that included the hedges' conversation."),
            Col("unit", "Priced per", 17, "text",
                validate=list_from("PricingUnitList"),
                note="How the rate is read. 'Per 1,000 sq ft' multiplies "
                     "by the lawn area; 'Per visit' does not."),
            Col("rate", "Your rate", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 100000),
                note="What you charge for one unit. Every price in the "
                     "workbook starts here."),
            Col("minutes", "Minutes per unit", 13, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 10000),
                note="Your own measured time for one unit of this "
                     "service, for one person."),
            Col("crew_size", "Crew needed", 11, "int",
                validate=amount(1, 20),
                note="How many people it takes. Two people for 30 "
                     "minutes is one crew hour, not half of one."),
            Col("materials_cost", "Materials per unit", 14, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 100000),
                note="Fertiliser, mulch, seed, bags. Leave at 0 for "
                     "pure labour services like mowing."),
            Col("equipment", "Main equipment", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("EquipmentCategoryList"),
                note="What has to be on the trailer. Useful when a "
                     "machine goes in for repair and you need to know "
                     "which jobs are affected."),
            Col("min_charge", "Minimum charge", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 100000),
                note="The least you will turn out for. Blank uses the "
                     "minimum visit charge on Settings."),
            Col("season", "Main season", 15, "text",
                validate=list_from("SeasonList"),
                note="When this sells. Drives the seasonal planner."),
            Col("frequency", "Usual frequency", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("FrequencyList"),
                note="How often a customer typically buys it."),
            Col("taxable", "Taxable", 9, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Is this service subject to sales tax or "
                               "VAT where you trade?"),
                note="Rules differ by state and country, and mowing is "
                     "not always treated the same as landscaping. Ask "
                     "your accountant, then set this."),
            Col("skill", "Skill needed", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewRoleList"),
                note="Who on the crew can actually do it. Pesticide work "
                     "needs the licence."),
            Col("licence_needed", "Licence needed", 12, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Does this service require a pesticide or "
                               "trade licence?"),
                note="If Yes, only licensed crew should be assigned."),
            Col("status", "Status", 12, "text",
                validate=list_from("ServiceStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 30, "wrap"),

            # ---- the economics ---------------------------------------
            Col("hours_per_unit", "Crew hours", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({minutes}{r}*MAX(1,{crew_size}{r})'
                             '/60,2)'),
                note="Minutes times the number of people, in hours. This "
                     "is what the labour actually costs you."),
            Col("labour_cost", "Labour cost", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({hours_per_unit}{r}*LabourRate,2)'),
                note="At the crew rate on Settings. Set a real loaded "
                     "rate there - wage plus tax plus insurance."),
            Col("overhead_cost", "Overhead", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({hours_per_unit}{r}*OverheadRate,2)'),
                note="The yard, the insurance and the office, charged "
                     "by the crew hour."),
            Col("total_cost", "Cost per unit", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({labour_cost}{r}+{materials_cost}{r}'
                             '+{overhead_cost}{r},2)'),
                note="Everything except the drive, which depends on the "
                     "property."),
            Col("profit_unit", "Profit per unit", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({rate}{r}-{total_cost}{r},2)'),
                cf=loss_cf()),
            Col("margin_pct", "Margin", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        f'{margin("{profit_unit}{r}", "{rate}{r}")})',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=MinMargin", "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Green at or above your target, red below your "
                     "floor. Both are set on Settings."),
            Col("per_hour", "Earns per hour", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_per_unit}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({rate}{r}/'
                        '{hours_per_unit}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The one number to compare services on. A service "
                     "earning below your charge-out rate is underpriced, "
                     "however good the margin looks."),
            Col("suggested", "Suggested rate", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({total_cost}{r}/'
                             'MAX(0.05,1-TargetMargin),2)'),
                note="What this rate would have to be to hit your target "
                     "margin. A suggestion, not a price - the market has "
                     "a vote."),
            Col("gap", "Rate gap", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({rate}{r}-{suggested}{r},2)'),
                cf=loss_cf(),
                note="Negative means you are charging less than your own "
                     "target says you should."),

            # ---- how it is selling -----------------------------------
            Col("times_sold", "Visits done", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                              "service", '{name}{r}',
                                              "is_done", "1")),
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("revenue", "Revenue", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Job & Work Orders", "charge",
                                            "service", '{name}{r}',
                                            "is_done", "1")),
                cf=money_cf()),
            Col("quoted", "Times quoted", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Quote Calculator",
                                              "service", '{name}{r}'))),
            Col("won", "Quotes won", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Quote Calculator",
                                              "service", '{name}{r}',
                                              "is_won", "1"))),
            Col("win_rate", "Win rate", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({quoted}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({won}{r}/{quoted}{r},4)))',
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Winning everything usually means the price is too "
                     "low, not that the selling is good."),
            Col("avg_actual", "Actual hours avg", 13, "number1",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(AVERAGEIFS('
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.hours_actual]],'
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.service]],{name}{r},'
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.is_done]],1,'
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.hours_actual]],">0"),""))',
                note="What this service really takes, from the "
                     "timesheets. The reality check on the estimate."),
            Col("contracts", "On contract", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Recurring Contracts",
                                              "service", '{name}{r}',
                                              "is_live", "1"))),
            Col("is_active", "Active", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("name", 'IF({status}{r}="Active",1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            rank_desc("revenue", guard='{name}{r}=""'),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 44, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("name", [
                    ('{rate}{r}=0', "Add - no rate set for this service"),
                    ('{minutes}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no time set, so it cannot be scheduled "
                     "honestly"),
                    ('{profit_unit}{r}<0',
                     "Loss - this service costs more than it charges"),
                    ('AND({margin_pct}{r}<>"",{margin_pct}{r}<MinMargin)',
                     "Urgent - margin is below your floor, reprice it"),
                    ('AND({avg_actual}{r}<>"",{hours_per_unit}{r}>0,'
                     '{avg_actual}{r}>{hours_per_unit}{r}*1.25)',
                     "Check - it really takes longer than the catalogue "
                     "says"),
                    ('AND({quoted}{r}>=5,{win_rate}{r}<>"",'
                     '{win_rate}{r}<0.2)',
                     "Check - rarely accepted, the price may be too "
                     "high"),
                    ('AND({quoted}{r}>=5,{win_rate}{r}<>"",'
                     '{win_rate}{r}>0.9)',
                     "Action - you win nearly every one, test a higher "
                     "price"),
                    ('AND({margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<TargetMargin)',
                     "Thin margin - under your target, review the rate"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{times_sold}{r}=0,'
                     '{quoted}{r}=0)',
                     "Action - never sold or quoted, is it worth "
                     "offering?"),
                    ('{description}{r}=""',
                     "Add - no description, so the quote is vague"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

---

## 6. The quote, and the work order it becomes

Workflow two. The quote calculator starts from the lawn size on the property record and the minutes in the catalogue, adds labour at your cost rate, materials at your markup, equipment, two-way travel and overhead per crew hour, then works the price back off the margin you asked for and refuses to go under your minimum visit. Accept it and the Job & Work Orders register carries the same price through to the invoice, costing itself twice on the way: what you estimated, and what the timesheets and expenses say it really took.

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/quotes.py`

*391 lines · 1 function*

> 7. Quote Calculator - the tab people buy this workbook for.
>
> Pick a property and a service and the price builds itself: lawn size
> off the property, labour off the catalogue time, materials, equipment,
> the round trip to site, overhead by the hour, then the margin you
> asked for. Every line of that build is visible, because a quote you
> cannot explain is a quote you cannot defend.
>
> Mark a quote Accepted and the work order columns light up - that is
> the hand-off into the Job & Work Orders tab.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Quote Calculator').

````python
"""7. Quote Calculator - the tab people buy this workbook for.

Pick a property and a service and the price builds itself: lawn size
off the property, labour off the catalogue time, materials, equipment,
the round trip to site, overhead by the hour, then the margin you
asked for. Every line of that build is visible, because a quote you
cannot explain is a quote you cannot defend.

Mark a quote Accepted and the work order columns light up - that is
the hand-off into the Job & Work Orders tab.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, customer_col,
                     date_num_col, id_col, is_live_quote, is_won_quote,
                     lookup_customer, lookup_property, lookup_property_num,
                     lookup_service, lookup_service_num, loss_cf, margin,
                     money_cf, month_col, pick, property_col,
                     service_col, service_tax, status_cf, zero)

NAME = "Quote Calculator"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Quote Calculator",
        subtitle="Lawn size, labour, materials, equipment, travel and "
                 "the margin you want - priced in one row.",
        tab_color=Palette.accent,
        rows=cfg.row_count("quotes"),
        samples=sample("quotes", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "Pick the customer, the property and the service. The lawn "
            "area and the drive come from the Property Database, the "
            "time and the materials from the Service Catalog, and the "
            "rates from Settings. Then look at the margin column and "
            "decide whether you are happy. Override any white cell - "
            "the shaded ones work themselves out."),
        extra_notes=[
            "The quote is built cost-up, not price-down: your cost is "
            "worked out first and the margin is added to it. That is "
            "why it can tell you when a price you had in mind is one "
            "you cannot afford.",
            "Travel is charged on the round trip, both ways, because "
            "that is how long the truck is away from anything else.",
            "Accepted quotes are the ones the job register expects to "
            "see. Set the status, write the Job ID you create against "
            "it, and the two tabs stay tied together.",
            "A quote expires on its own after the number of days set "
            "on Settings. An expired quote is a reason to call, not a "
            "reason to delete the row.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", "QuotePrefix", digits=cfg.digits("quote"),
                   header="Quote ID",
                   note="Generated. Put this number on the paperwork "
                        "you send out."),
            Col("date", "Quote date", 12, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here. Everything else on the row unlocks "
                     "once there is a date."),
            customer_col(),
            Col("customer_name", "Customer name", 22, "text",
                formula=lookup_customer("name")),
            property_col(),
            Col("address", "Address", 28, "text",
                formula=lookup_property("address")),
            service_col(),
            Col("description", "What is included", 34, "wrap",
                formula='=IF({service}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX([[Service Catalog.description]],'
                        'MATCH({service}{r},'
                        '[[Service Catalog.name]],0))&"",""))',
                note="Pulled from the catalogue so the quote says the "
                     "same thing every time. Overwrite it on the "
                     "printable if one job is different."),
            Col("frequency", "Frequency", 15, "text",
                validate=pick("FrequencyList",
                              "One-off, or how often it repeats if this "
                              "is a maintenance quote."),
                note="A recurring quote is worth its annual value, not "
                     "its visit value. The column on the right shows "
                     "both."),

            # ---- what the site tells us ------------------------------
            Col("area", "Lawn area", 11, "int",
                formula=lookup_property_num("lawn_area"),
                note="From the Property Database. Measure it there, "
                     "once."),
            Col("difficulty", "Difficulty", 10, "number1",
                formula='=IF({property}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX([[Property Database.difficulty]],'
                        'MATCH({property}{r},'
                        '[[Property Database.id]],0))*1,1))',
                note="Slope, access, obstacles and the dog, from the "
                     "property record."),
            Col("miles", "Miles each way", 12, "number1",
                formula=lookup_property_num("miles")),

            # ---- the build -------------------------------------------
            Col("cat_minutes", "Catalogue mins", 12, "int",
                formula=lookup_service_num("minutes"),
                hidden=True),
            Col("cat_unit", "Unit", 14, "text",
                formula=lookup_service("unit", missing=""),
                hidden=True),
            Col("cat_crew", "Catalogue crew", 11, "int",
                formula=lookup_service_num("crew_size"),
                hidden=True),
            Col("units", "Units", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per 1,000 sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r})/1000,'
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per 100 sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r})/100,'
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r}),1)))'),
                note="How many units of the catalogue price this site "
                     "needs. For a per-visit service it is simply 1."),
            Col("est_minutes", "Est. minutes", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({cat_minutes}{r}*{units}{r}*'
                             'MAX(1,{difficulty}{r}),0)'),
                note="Catalogue time, scaled for the size of this lawn "
                     "and how awkward it is."),
            Col("crew_size", "Crew", 8, "int",
                validate=amount(1, 20, "How many people are going?",
                                decimal=False),
                note="Override the catalogue if you are sending a "
                     "different number of people."),
            Col("crew_used", "Crew used", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IF({crew_size}{r}>0,{crew_size}{r},'
                             'MAX(1,{cat_crew}{r}))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("site_hours", "Hours on site", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({est_minutes}{r}/60/'
                             'MAX(1,{crew_used}{r}),2)'),
                note="How long the visit takes with that many people on "
                     "it. Two people halve the clock, not the cost."),
            Col("drive_hours", "Drive hours", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND(2*IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Property Database.drive_minutes]],'
                             'MATCH({property}{r},'
                             '[[Property Database.id]],0))*1,'
                             'DriveMinutes)/60*{crew_used}{r},2)'),
                note="Round trip, times the crew, because they are all "
                     "in the truck and all being paid."),
            Col("labour_hours", "Crew hours", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({site_hours}{r}*{crew_used}{r}+'
                             '{drive_hours}{r},2)'),
                note="Total paid hours this quote has to cover."),
            Col("labour_cost", "Labour cost", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({labour_hours}{r}*LabourRate,2)'),
                note="At your loaded crew rate from Settings - wages "
                     "plus tax plus insurance, not the take-home."),
            Col("materials_cost", "Materials", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "Leave blank to use the catalogue "
                                "figure for this service."),
                note="Your cost, not the customer's price. The markup "
                     "is added below."),
            Col("materials_used", "Materials used", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IF({materials_cost}{r}>0,'
                             '{materials_cost}{r},'
                             'ROUND(IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Service Catalog.materials_cost]],'
                             'MATCH({service}{r},'
                             '[[Service Catalog.name]],0))*1,0)*'
                             '{units}{r},2))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("materials_charge", "Materials charged", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({materials_used}{r}*'
                             'MaterialMarkup,2)'),
                note="Cost times the markup on Settings. Handling, "
                     "collecting and carrying are real work."),
            Col("equipment_cost", "Equipment", 10, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "Hire, blades, fuel for the day, or a "
                                "share of what the machine costs to "
                                "own."),
                note="Anything this job needs that the hourly rate does "
                     "not already cover."),
            Col("travel_cost", "Travel", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND(2*{miles}{r}*TravelRate,2)'),
                note="Round trip at the mileage rate on Settings. Fuel, "
                     "tyres and the share of the truck."),
            Col("disposal_cost", "Disposal", 10, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "Tip fees, green waste, skip hire."),
                note="Taking it away is never free. Charge it."),
            Col("overhead_cost", "Overhead", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({labour_hours}{r}*OverheadRate,2)'),
                note="The yard, the van insurance, the phone and the "
                     "evenings doing paperwork, per crew hour."),
            Col("total_cost", "Cost to you", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({labour_cost}{r}+'
                             '{materials_used}{r}+{equipment_cost}{r}+'
                             '{travel_cost}{r}+{disposal_cost}{r}+'
                             '{overhead_cost}{r},2)'),
                note="What this visit actually costs you, before a "
                     "penny of profit."),

            # ---- the price -------------------------------------------
            Col("target_margin", "Margin wanted", 11, "percent0",
                validate=amount(0, 0.95,
                                "The profit margin you want on this "
                                "job. Blank uses the target on "
                                "Settings."),
                note="Your number, job by job. A difficult customer or "
                     "a tight season is a reason to change it."),
            Col("margin_used", "Margin used", 10, "percent0",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IF({target_margin}{r}>0,'
                             '{target_margin}{r},TargetMargin)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("calc_price", "Calculated price", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND(MAX(MinimumVisit,'
                             '({total_cost}{r}-{materials_used}{r}+'
                             '{materials_charge}{r})/'
                             'MAX(0.05,1-{margin_used}{r})),2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="Cost plus the margin you asked for, with the "
                     "minimum visit charge as a floor. This is the "
                     "suggestion."),
            Col("discount", "Discount", 10, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "Any reduction you are offering - a "
                                "neighbour deal, a first-visit offer."),
                note="Record it rather than quietly lowering the price, "
                     "so you can see what discounting costs you."),
            Col("quoted_price", "Your price", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "What you are actually quoting. Blank "
                                "uses the calculated price."),
                note="Override whenever you like. The margin column "
                     "tells you immediately what the override did."),
            Col("price_used", "Price", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND(IF({quoted_price}{r}>0,'
                             '{quoted_price}{r},{calc_price}{r})-'
                             '{discount}{r},2)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("tax", "Tax", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("service", service_tax('{price_used}{r}')),
                note="Only if you have switched tax on, on Settings. "
                     "Whether mowing is taxable where you trade is a "
                     "question for your accountant."),
            Col("total", "Total quoted", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({price_used}{r}+{tax}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What goes on the paperwork."),
            Col("profit", "Profit", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({price_used}{r}-{total_cost}{r},2)'),
                cf=loss_cf()),
            Col("margin_pct", "Margin", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({service}{r}="","",'
                        f'{margin("{profit}{r}", "{price_used}{r}")})',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=MinMargin", "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The number to look at before you press send."),
            Col("per_hour", "Earns per hour", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({service}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({labour_hours}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({price_used}{r}/'
                        '{labour_hours}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("visits_year", "Visits / year", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IFERROR(INDEX(VisitsPerYear,'
                             'MATCH({frequency}{r},FrequencyList,0))*1,'
                             '0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("annual_value", "Annual value", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({total}{r}*{visits_year}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What a recurring quote is really worth over a "
                     "year. A one-off shows zero - that is the point."),

            # ---- where it went ---------------------------------------
            Col("status", "Status", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("QuoteStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf(),
                note="Accepted is the one that matters - it is the "
                     "signal to raise a work order."),
            Col("sent_date", "Sent", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("decision_date", "Answered", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("valid_until", "Valid until", 11, "date",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        '{date}{r}+QuoteValidDays)',
                note="Automatic, from the date and the validity period "
                     "on Settings."),
            Col("days_out", "Days waiting", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({sent_date}{r}="",0,'
                             'IF({decision_date}{r}<>"",'
                             '{decision_date}{r}-{sent_date}{r},'
                             'TodayDate-{sent_date}{r}))'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 14,
                     "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("job", "Job ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("JobIDList",
                              "Pick the work order this quote turned "
                              "into."),
                note="Once you have raised the work order, write its "
                     "Job ID here. That is the thread from quote to "
                     "job to invoice."),
            Col("contract", "Contract ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("ContractIDList",
                              "Pick the agreement this quote became."),
                note="If the quote turned into a recurring agreement, "
                     "put the Contract ID here instead."),
            Col("lost_reason", "If lost, why", 24, "text",
                note="Price, timing, went elsewhere, never replied. Six "
                     "months of this column is worth more than any "
                     "advice about pricing."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 30, "wrap"),

            Col("is_won", "Won", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date", f'IF({is_won_quote("{status}{r}")},'
                                     '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_live", "Live", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date", f'IF({is_live_quote("{status}{r}")},'
                                     '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_expired", "Expired", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({is_live}{r}=1,'
                             '{valid_until}{r}<TodayDate),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('AND({is_won}{r}=1,{job}{r}="",{contract}{r}="")',
                     "Action - accepted but no work order raised yet"),
                    ('{margin_pct}{r}<0',
                     "Loss - this price is below what the job costs"),
                    ('AND({margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<MinMargin,{is_live}{r}=1)',
                     "Urgent - margin under your floor, reprice before "
                     "sending"),
                    ('{is_expired}{r}=1',
                     "Expired - past its validity date, call them"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{sent_date}{r}="")',
                     "Send - quote prepared but never sent"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{days_out}{r}>=ChaseQuoteDays)',
                     "Chase - no answer since you sent it"),
                    ('AND({status}{r}="Declined",{lost_reason}{r}="")',
                     "Add - lost, and no reason recorded"),
                    ('{area}{r}=0',
                     "Check - no lawn area on the property record"),
                    ('AND({margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<TargetMargin)',
                     "Thin margin - under target, but above your floor"),
                    ('{is_live}{r}=1', "Quoted - waiting on an answer"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/jobs.py`

*475 lines · 1 function*

> 8. Job & Work Orders - one row per visit, and the hub of the workbook.
>
> Everything meets here. The customer and property say where; the
> service says what; the timesheets say what the labour really cost;
> the expenses say what was spent on site; the invoice says whether you
> were paid for any of it.
>
> The estimated-versus-actual pair of columns is the whole argument for
> keeping records at all. Estimated cost is what the quote assumed.
> Actual cost is what the crew and the receipts say happened. The gap
> between them is where a landscaping business quietly loses money.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Job & Work Orders').

````python
"""8. Job & Work Orders - one row per visit, and the hub of the workbook.

Everything meets here. The customer and property say where; the
service says what; the timesheets say what the labour really cost;
the expenses say what was spent on site; the invoice says whether you
were paid for any of it.

The estimated-versus-actual pair of columns is the whole argument for
keeping records at all. Estimated cost is what the quote assumed.
Actual cost is what the crew and the receipts say happened. The gap
between them is where a landscaping business quietly loses money.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from, time_check
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, customer_col,
                     date_num_col, id_col, is_catchup_job, is_done_job,
                     is_open_job, lookup_customer, lookup_property,
                     lookup_property_num, lookup_service,
                     lookup_service_num, loss_cf, margin, money_cf,
                     month_col, owing_cf, pick, property_col, scale_cf,
                     service_col, service_tax, status_cf, sum_by,
                     week_start_col, weekday_col, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Job & Work Orders"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Job & Work Orders",
        subtitle="Every visit, booked or done: who, where, what, how "
                 "long it took and what it was really worth.",
        tab_color=Palette.deep,
        rows=cfg.row_count("jobs"),
        samples=sample("jobs", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "One row per visit. Book it with a date, a property and a "
            "service; mark it Completed when it is done and fill in "
            "the actual hours. Everything else - the estimate, the "
            "costs, the profit, the invoice status - comes from the "
            "other tabs automatically."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Estimated cost comes from the catalogue time and the "
            "property's difficulty. Actual cost comes from the "
            "timesheets and the expense tracker. Watch the variance "
            "column: a service that is always 20% over is a service "
            "that is underpriced, not a crew that is slow.",
            "'Rained off' and 'Needs revisit' are not failures, they "
            "are work you still owe. They stay on the catch-up list on "
            "the dashboard until they are rescheduled.",
            "Leave the Invoice ID blank until you raise the invoice. "
            "The 'Unbilled' alert finds completed work nobody has "
            "charged for - on most jobs that is the single most "
            "profitable column in this workbook.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", "JobPrefix", digits=cfg.digits("job"),
                   header="Job ID",
                   note="Generated. This is what the timesheets, the "
                        "expenses and the invoice all point at."),
            Col("date", "Date", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="The day of the visit. Start here."),
            Col("weekday", "Day", 8, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'TEXT({date}{r},"ddd"))',
                hidden=True),
            customer_col(),
            Col("customer_name", "Customer", 20, "text",
                formula=lookup_customer("name")),
            property_col(),
            Col("address", "Address", 28, "text",
                formula=lookup_property("address")),
            Col("town", "Town", 14, "text",
                formula=lookup_property("town", missing=""),
                note="Used by the route planner to group a day's stops "
                     "sensibly."),
            service_col(),
            Col("crew", "Crew", 12, "text",
                validate=pick("CrewNameList",
                              "Which crew is doing it? Set the crews up "
                              "on the Settings tab."),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Unassigned", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The route planner prints one sheet per crew per "
                     "day from this column."),
            Col("status", "Status", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("JobStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf(),
                note="Completed is what makes a job count as revenue. "
                     "Nothing is earned until it is done."),
            Col("priority", "Priority", 15, "text",
                validate=list_from("PriorityList"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "1 - Urgent", "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "2 - High", "fmt": "bad"}]),
            Col("contract", "Contract ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("ContractIDList",
                              "Pick the agreement this visit belongs "
                              "to."),
                note="If this visit came from a recurring agreement, "
                     "its Contract ID. Blank for one-off work."),
            Col("quote", "Quote ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("QuoteIDList",
                              "Pick the quote this work was priced "
                              "from."),
                note="If it came from an accepted quote, its Quote ID. "
                     "That is the audit trail from price to paid."),

            # ---- the plan --------------------------------------------
            Col("start_time", "Start", 9, "time",
                validate=time_check(),
                note="Planned arrival. The route planner sorts the day "
                     "by this."),
            Col("area", "Lawn area", 10, "int",
                formula=lookup_property_num("lawn_area"),
                hidden=True),
            Col("difficulty", "Difficulty", 9, "number1",
                formula='=IF({property}{r}="",1,'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX([[Property Database.difficulty]],'
                        'MATCH({property}{r},'
                        '[[Property Database.id]],0))*1,1))',
                hidden=True),
            Col("cat_minutes", "Catalogue mins", 11, "int",
                formula=lookup_service_num("minutes"),
                hidden=True),
            Col("cat_unit", "Unit", 12, "text",
                formula=lookup_service("unit", missing=""),
                hidden=True),
            Col("units", "Units", 8, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per 1,000 sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r})/1000,'
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per 100 sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r})/100,'
                             'IF({cat_unit}{r}="Per sq ft",'
                             'MAX(1,{area}{r}),1)))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("crew_size", "Crew size", 9, "int",
                validate=amount(1, 20, "How many people are going?",
                                decimal=False),
                note="Blank uses the default crew size on Settings."),
            Col("crew_used", "Crew used", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({crew_size}{r}>0,{crew_size}{r},'
                             'CrewSize)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("hours_est", "Hours (est)", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({cat_minutes}{r}*{units}{r}*'
                             'MAX(1,{difficulty}{r})/60,2)'),
                note="Crew hours the catalogue expects, adjusted for "
                     "this site."),

            # ---- what happened ---------------------------------------
            Col("hours_logged", "Hours (timesheet)", 13, "number1",
                formula=zero("date", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "hours", "job", '{id}{r}')),
                hidden=True),
            Col("hours_typed", "Hours (actual)", 12, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 200,
                                "Total crew hours on site. Leave blank "
                                "if you are using the timesheets."),
                note="Type it here, or let the timesheets fill it in. "
                     "The timesheets win if both are present."),
            Col("hours_actual", "Hours used", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({hours_logged}{r}>0,{hours_logged}{r},'
                             '{hours_typed}{r})'),
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Timesheet hours if there are any, otherwise what "
                     "you typed."),
            Col("hours_var", "Hours vs est", 11, "number1",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_actual}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({hours_actual}{r}-'
                        '{hours_est}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0.5,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": -0.25,
                     "fmt": "good"}],
                note="Positive means it took longer than the catalogue "
                     "said. A pattern here is a pricing problem."),
            Col("weather", "Weather", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("WeatherList"),
                note="Why the day went the way it did. Rain is the "
                     "reason most schedules slip."),
            Col("completed_by", "Done by", 14, "text",
                note="Who actually did it, if it was not the crew you "
                     "booked."),
            Col("checklist_done", "Checklist done", 12, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Has the crew worked through the job "
                               "completion checklist?"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "No", "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Gates shut, clippings cleared, tools counted. The "
                     "checklist itself is on the Seasonal Planner tab."),
            Col("photo_before", "Photo - before", 18, "link"),
            Col("photo_after", "Photo - after", 18, "link",
                note="Links to your cloud drive, not pictures in the "
                     "file. Keeps the workbook fast and the evidence "
                     "safe."),
            Col("issues", "Problems on site", 30, "wrap",
                note="Broken sprinkler, locked gate, a dog out. Write "
                     "it down the same day or it is gone."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 30, "wrap"),
````
