````python
            # ---- money in --------------------------------------------
            Col("price_quoted", "Agreed price", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "What the customer agreed to pay. Blank "
                                "uses the contract or catalogue price."),
                note="Blank falls back to the contract price, then the "
                     "catalogue."),
            Col("contract_price", "Contract price", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({contract}{r}="",0,'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Recurring Contracts.visit_price]],'
                             'MATCH({contract}{r},'
                             '[[Recurring Contracts.id]],0))*1,0))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("cat_price", "Catalogue price", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND(IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Service Catalog.rate]],'
                             'MATCH({service}{r},'
                             '[[Service Catalog.name]],0))*1,0)*'
                             '{units}{r},2)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("charge", "Charge", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(IF({price_quoted}{r}>0,'
                             '{price_quoted}{r},'
                             'IF({contract_price}{r}>0,'
                             '{contract_price}{r},{cat_price}{r})),2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What this visit earns, before tax. Completed "
                     "visits with a charge are your revenue."),
            Col("extras", "Extras charged", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "Anything beyond the agreed price - an "
                                "extra bag of mulch, a hedge they asked "
                                "for on the day."),
                note="The work your crew does for nothing unless "
                     "somebody writes it down."),
            Col("tax", "Tax", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             service_tax('{charge}{r}+{extras}{r}')),
                hidden=True),
            Col("total_charge", "Total", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({charge}{r}+{extras}{r}+'
                             '{tax}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf()),

            # ---- money out: estimated, then actual -------------------
            Col("cost_est", "Cost (est)", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("service",
                             'ROUND({hours_est}{r}*(LabourRate+'
                             'OverheadRate)+'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Service Catalog.materials_cost]],'
                             'MATCH({service}{r},'
                             '[[Service Catalog.name]],0))*1,0)*'
                             '{units}{r}+'
                             '2*IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Property Database.miles]],'
                             'MATCH({property}{r},'
                             '[[Property Database.id]],0))*1,0)*'
                             'TravelRate,2)'),
                note="What the quote assumed: catalogue time at your "
                     "rates, plus materials, plus the drive."),
            Col("labour_cost", "Labour (actual)", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "cost", "job", '{id}{r}')),
                note="From the timesheets, at each person's own rate."),
            Col("labour_fallback", "Labour fallback", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({labour_cost}{r}>0,0,'
                             'ROUND({hours_actual}{r}*LabourRate,2))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("expense_cost", "Site costs", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date", sum_by("Expense Tracker", "amount",
                                            "job", '{id}{r}')),
                note="Materials, tip fees and anything else booked to "
                     "this Job ID on the expense tracker."),
            Col("overhead_cost", "Overhead", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({hours_actual}{r}*OverheadRate,2)'),
                note="Charged on the hours actually worked, not the "
                     "hours hoped for."),
            Col("travel_cost", "Travel", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(2*IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Property Database.miles]],'
                             'MATCH({property}{r},'
                             '[[Property Database.id]],0))*1,0)*'
                             'TravelRate,2)')),
            Col("cost", "Cost (actual)", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({labour_cost}{r}+'
                             '{labour_fallback}{r}+{expense_cost}{r}+'
                             '{overhead_cost}{r}+{travel_cost}{r},2)'),
                note="Everything this visit really cost you."),
            Col("cost_var", "Cost vs est", 11, "money",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({is_done}{r}=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({cost}{r}-{cost_est}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "good"}],
                note="Red means it cost more than you estimated. This "
                     "column, averaged over a season, is the most "
                     "valuable thing in the workbook."),
            Col("profit", "Profit", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({is_done}{r}=0,0,'
                             'ROUND({charge}{r}+{extras}{r}-'
                             '{cost}{r},2))'),
                cf=loss_cf(),
                note="Completed visits only, and excluding tax - tax is "
                     "never yours."),
            Col("margin_pct", "Margin", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({is_done}{r}=0,"",'
                        f'{margin("{profit}{r}", "({charge}{r}+{extras}{r})")}))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=MinMargin", "fmt": "bad"}]),
            Col("per_hour", "Earned per hour", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_actual}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND(({charge}{r}+{extras}{r})/'
                        '{hours_actual}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "warn"}]),

            # ---- getting paid ----------------------------------------
            Col("invoice", "Invoice ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("InvoiceIDList",
                              "Pick the invoice that bills this "
                              "visit."),
                note="Fill in when you raise the invoice. Blank on a "
                     "completed job means unbilled work."),
            Col("invoiced", "Invoiced", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date", sum_by("Invoice & Payments", "total",
                                            "job", '{id}{r}')),
                hidden=True),
            Col("paid_state", "Payment", 14, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({invoice}{r}="",'
                        'IF({is_done}{r}=1,"Not invoiced",""),'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX('
                        '[[Invoice & Payments.status]],'
                        'MATCH({invoice}{r},'
                        '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0))&"",'
                        '"Check - invoice not found")))',
                cf=status_cf(),
                note="Read straight off the invoice register, so it can "
                     "never disagree with it."),
            Col("outstanding", "Still owed", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({invoice}{r}="",0,'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Invoice & Payments.outstanding]],'
                             'MATCH({invoice}{r},'
                             '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0))*1,0))'),
                cf=owing_cf(),
                hidden=True),

            # ---- the hidden machinery --------------------------------
            Col("is_done", "Done", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date", f'IF({is_done_job("{status}{r}")},'
                                     '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_open", "Open", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date", f'IF({is_open_job("{status}{r}")},'
                                     '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_catchup", "Catch up", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             f'IF({is_catchup_job("{status}{r}")},1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_today", "Today", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({date}{r}=TodayDate,'
                             '{is_open}{r}=1),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_week", "This week", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({is_open}{r}=1,'
                             '{date}{r}>=TodayDate,'
                             '{date}{r}<=TodayDate+UpcomingDays),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_unbilled", "Unbilled", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({is_done}{r}=1,{invoice}{r}="",'
                             '{charge}{r}>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("today_rank", "Today rank", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({is_today}{r}=0,0,'
                             'COUNTIFS({is_today_first}:{is_today}{r},'
                             '1))'),
                hidden=True,
                note="1, 2, 3 ... down today's jobs, so the dashboard "
                     "can list them without an array formula."),
            Col("route_key", "Route key", 14, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'TEXT({date}{r},"yyyy-mm-dd")&"|"&'
                        '{crew}{r})',
                hidden=True,
                note="Date and crew together - how the route planner "
                     "finds one crew's day in one lookup."),
            Col("route_seq", "Stop", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'COUNTIFS({route_key_first}:{route_key}{r},'
                             '{route_key}{r})'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("route_match", "Route match", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({date}{r}=RouteDate,'
                             '{crew}{r}=RouteCrew),{route_seq}{r},0)'),
                hidden=True,
                note="Which line of the printable work order this job "
                     "is, if it is on the chosen day at all."),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            weekday_col("date", "dow", header="DOW"),
            week_start_col("date"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('AND({is_open}{r}=1,{date}{r}<TodayDate)',
                     "Overdue - booked in the past and never closed off"),
                    ('AND({is_done}{r}=1,{charge}{r}=0)',
                     "Urgent - completed with no charge, is it free?"),
                    ('{is_catchup}{r}=1',
                     "Reschedule - rained off or needs a revisit"),
                    ('AND({is_unbilled}{r}=1,'
                     '{date}{r}<TodayDate-OverdueDays)',
                     "Unbilled - done and never invoiced"),
                    ('AND({is_done}{r}=1,{profit}{r}<0)',
                     "Loss - this visit cost more than it charged"),
                    ('{is_today}{r}=1', "Today - on the list for today"),
                    ('AND({is_open}{r}=1,{crew}{r}="Unassigned")',
                     "Action - booked with nobody assigned to it"),
                    ('AND({is_done}{r}=1,{hours_actual}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - completed with no hours, so profit is "
                     "guesswork"),
                    ('AND({is_done}{r}=1,{checklist_done}{r}="No")',
                     "Check - completion checklist not signed off"),
                    ('AND({is_done}{r}=1,{margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<MinMargin)',
                     "Thin margin - under your floor on this visit"),
                    ('{is_week}{r}=1', "This week - coming up"),
                    ('{is_open}{r}=1', "Upcoming - booked"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/route.py`

*483 lines · 8 functions*

> `Route Planner` - one crew, one day, one sheet to take with them.
>
> Pick a date and a crew at the top and the day builds itself from the
> Job & Work Orders tab: every stop, in the order it is booked, with the
> address, the gate code, the dog warning, the service, the time it
> should take and what to charge. Print it and hand it over.
>
> What this page is not, and the listing says so too: it is not GPS
> navigation and it does not optimise anything. It will not tell you
> that visiting Mill Lane before Oak Avenue saves eleven minutes. It
> organises the stops you have chosen, in the order you have chosen
> them, onto one piece of paper - which is the thing most crews are
> actually missing.
>
> The stop order is the order the jobs were entered for that date, and
> you change it by changing the start times on the job rows.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `_stop(field, line)` | Field from the nth job on the chosen crew's chosen day |
| `_stop_num(field, line)` |  |
| `build(book, fmts, cfg, reg)` |  |
| `_pull(sheet, field, key, picker, numeric)` | One field off the record the picker names |
| `_doc_line(s, fmts, row, label, formula, style)` | `Label | value` inside a printed document |
| `_money_line(s, fmts, row, label, formula, strong)` | A right-aligned money line on a document |
| `_printable_quote(s, fmts, cfg, row)` | A quote on one page, built from the row you name |
| `_printable_invoice(s, fmts, cfg, row)` | An invoice on one page, built from the row you name |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Route Planner'), `LAST_COL` (10), `STOPS` (14).

````python
"""`Route Planner` - one crew, one day, one sheet to take with them.

Pick a date and a crew at the top and the day builds itself from the
Job & Work Orders tab: every stop, in the order it is booked, with the
address, the gate code, the dog warning, the service, the time it
should take and what to charge. Print it and hand it over.

What this page is not, and the listing says so too: it is not GPS
navigation and it does not optimise anything. It will not tell you
that visiting Mill Lane before Oak Avenue saves eleven minutes. It
organises the stops you have chosen, in the order you have chosen
them, onto one piece of paper - which is the thing most crews are
actually missing.

The stop order is the order the jobs were entered for that date, and
you change it by changing the start times on the job rows.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import CREW_NAMES, Config, Palette
from ..layout import Registry
from ..writer import SheetWriter, date_check

NAME = "Route Planner"
LAST_COL = 10
STOPS = 14


def _stop(field: str, line: int) -> str:
    """Field from the nth job on the chosen crew's chosen day."""
    return (f'IFERROR(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.{field}]],'
            f'MATCH({line},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0)),"")')


def _stop_num(field: str, line: int) -> str:
    return (f'IFERROR(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.{field}]],'
            f'MATCH({line},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0))*1,"")')


def build(book, fmts, cfg: Config, reg: Registry) -> SheetWriter:
    s = SheetWriter(book, fmts, cfg, reg, NAME,
                    tab_color=Palette.primary_light)

    s.banner("Route Planner",
             "Pick a day and a crew. The stops, the gate codes and "
             "the times fill themselves in, ready to print.",
             LAST_COL)
    s.set_col(0, 0, 5)
    s.set_col(1, 1, 13)
    s.set_col(2, 2, 24)
    s.set_col(3, 3, 30)
    s.set_col(4, 4, 22)
    s.set_col(5, 5, 9)
    s.set_col(6, 6, 9)
    s.set_col(7, 7, 11)
    s.set_col(8, 8, 16)
    s.set_col(9, LAST_COL, 26)

    row = 4
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "This page organises stops onto a printable sheet. It is not "
        "satellite navigation and it does not work out the shortest "
        "route - you decide the order by setting start times on the "
        "Job & Work Orders tab, and this prints what you decided.",
        LAST_COL, height=34)
    row += 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------- the picker --
    row = s.section(row, "Choose the day", LAST_COL)
    row = s.field(row, "Date", cfg.today, style="date",
                  name="RouteDate", help_col=4,
                  help_text="Type any date. The stops below change "
                            "immediately.",
                  validate=date_check())
    row = s.field(row, "Crew", CREW_NAMES[0], name="RouteCrew",
                  help_col=4,
                  validate={"validate": "list",
                            "source": "=CrewNameList",
                            "input_message": "Which crew's day is "
                                             "this?"},
                  help_text="Each crew gets its own sheet. Print one "
                            "per truck.")
    row += 1

    # ---------------------------------------------------- the day's shape --
    row = s.section(row, "The day at a glance", LAST_COL)
    s.kpi(row, 0, "Stops",
          '=COUNTIFS(JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,RouteCrew,'
          'JobIsOpen,1)', "int", note="Jobs booked, still open")
    s.kpi(row, 2, "Work hours",
          '=SUMIFS(JobHoursEst,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
          'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)', "number1",
          note="Crew hours on site, estimated")
    s.kpi(row, 4, "Driving",
          '=ROUND(SUMIFS(JobDateNum,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
          'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)*0+COUNTIFS(JobDateNum,RouteDate,'
          'JobCrew,RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)*DriveMinutes/60,1)',
          "number1", note="Rough, at the default hop on Settings")
    s.kpi(row, 6, "Worth",
          '=SUMIFS(JobCharge,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,RouteCrew,'
          'JobIsOpen,1)', "money", note="Value of the round")
    row = s.kpi(row, 8, "Per crew hour",
                '=IFERROR(SUMIFS(JobCharge,JobDateNum,RouteDate,'
                'JobCrew,RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)/'
                'SUMIFS(JobHoursEst,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
                'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1),"")', "money", span=2,
                note="Against your charge-out rate")
    row += 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------- print head --
    row += 1
    s.merge(row, 0, 5, None, fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.formula(row, 0, '=BusinessName&" - crew work order"',
              fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.merge(row, 6, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    s.formula(row, 6,
              '=TEXT(RouteDate,"dddd d mmmm yyyy")&"   "&RouteCrew',
              fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    row += 1
    s.merge(row, 0, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.formula(
        row, 0,
        '="Jobs: "&COUNTIFS(JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,RouteCrew,'
        'JobIsOpen,1)&"    Estimated hours: "&'
        'TEXT(SUMIFS(JobHoursEst,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
        'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1),"0.0")&'
        '"    Office: "&BusinessPhone',
        fmts.chrome("note"))
    row += 2

    # ------------------------------------------------------- the stops --
    s.table_head(row, 0,
                 ["#", "Start", "Customer", "Address", "Service",
                  "Mins", "Crew", "Charge", "Access / code",
                  "Notes and hazards"],
                 widths=[5, 13, 24, 30, 22, 9, 9, 11, 16, 26])
    head = row
    for n in range(1, STOPS + 1):
        line = head + n
        s.write(line, 0, n, fmts.chrome("line_number"))
        s.formula(line, 1,
                  '=IFERROR(IF(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.start_time]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0))="",'
                  '"",INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.start_time]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0))),'
                  '"")', fmts.calc("time"))
        s.formula(line, 2, "=" + _stop("customer_name", n),
                  fmts.calc("text"))
        s.formula(line, 3, "=" + _stop("address", n),
                  fmts.calc("wrap"))
        s.formula(line, 4, "=" + _stop("service", n),
                  fmts.calc("text"))
        s.formula(line, 5,
                  '=IFERROR(ROUND(INDEX('
                  '[[Job & Work Orders.hours_est]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0))*60,'
                  '0),"")', fmts.calc("int"))
        s.formula(line, 6, "=" + _stop_num("crew_used", n),
                  fmts.calc("int"))
        s.formula(line, 7, "=" + _stop_num("charge", n),
                  fmts.calc("money"))
        s.formula(line, 8,
                  '=IFERROR(INDEX([[Property Database.gate_code]],'
                  'MATCH(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.property]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0)),'
                  '[[Property Database.id]],0))&"","")',
                  fmts.calc("text"))
        s.formula(line, 9,
                  '=IFERROR(IF(INDEX('
                  '[[Property Database.dog]],'
                  'MATCH(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.property]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0)),'
                  '[[Property Database.id]],0))="Yes","DOG ON SITE. ",'
                  '"")&INDEX([[Property Database.pets_note]],'
                  'MATCH(INDEX([[Job & Work Orders.property]],'
                  f'MATCH({n},[[Job & Work Orders.route_match]],0)),'
                  '[[Property Database.id]],0))&"","")',
                  fmts.calc("wrap"))
        s.row_height(line, 26)
    s.cf(f"J{head + 2}:J{head + 1 + STOPS}",
         {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing", "value": "DOG",
          "format": fmts.cf("bad")})
    row = head + STOPS + 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------- the totals --
    s.write(row, 0, None, fmts.chrome("total_label"))
    s.merge(row, 0, 4, "Day total", fmts.chrome("total_label"))
    s.formula(row, 5,
              '=ROUND(SUMIFS(JobHoursEst,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
              'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)*60,0)',
              fmts.chrome("total_value"))
    s.write(row, 6, None, fmts.chrome("total_value"))
    s.formula(row, 7,
              '=SUMIFS(JobCharge,JobDateNum,RouteDate,JobCrew,'
              'RouteCrew,JobIsOpen,1)', fmts.chrome("total_value"))
    s.merge(row, 8, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("total_value"))
    row += 2

    # ------------------------------------------- what the crew writes on --
    row = s.section(row, "For the crew to fill in", LAST_COL)
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Print this page and the crew writes the real times and "
        "anything that went wrong in these boxes. Those numbers go "
        "back into the Timesheets and the job rows that evening, "
        "and that is what makes the profit figures true rather than "
        "hopeful.",
        LAST_COL, height=34)
    row += 1
    s.table_head(row, 0,
                 ["#", "Arrived", "Left", "Actual mins", "Done?",
                  "Problems on site", "Extra work done", "Materials "
                  "used", "Photo taken", "Signature"],
                 widths=[5, 13, 13, 12, 9, 26, 22, 18, 12, 20])
    head = row
    for n in range(1, STOPS + 1):
        line = head + n
        s.write(line, 0, n, fmts.chrome("line_number"))
        for column in range(1, LAST_COL + 1):
            s.write(line, column, None, fmts.chrome("form_line"))
        s.row_height(line, 24)
    row = head + STOPS + 2

    # --------------------------------------------------- the checklist --
    row = s.section(row, "Before you leave each property", LAST_COL)
    row = s.bullets(row, [
        "Gates shut and latched - the single most expensive thing a "
        "crew forgets.",
        "Clippings off the paths, the drive and the road.",
        "Beds and borders edged where the job includes them.",
        "Nothing left behind: strimmer line, fuel cans, hand tools, "
        "the blower.",
        "Any damage photographed and reported the same day, not the "
        "next week.",
        "Customer spoken to if they are in, and told what was done.",
        "Actual time written on this sheet before you drive off.",
    ], LAST_COL)
    row += 1

    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Stops are listed in the order the jobs appear for that date "
        "and crew. To change the order, change the start times on "
        "the Job & Work Orders tab. Stops beyond fourteen in a day "
        "are not printed - if you are regularly over fourteen, split "
        "the round between two crews.",
        LAST_COL, height=34)
    row += 2

    row = _printable_quote(s, fmts, cfg, row)
    row = _printable_invoice(s, fmts, cfg, row)

    s.finish(freeze_row=4, gridlines=False, fit_width=True)
    s.protect()
    return s


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# the two documents a customer actually sees
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _pull(sheet: str, field: str, key: str, picker: str,
          numeric: bool = False) -> str:
    """One field off the record the picker names."""
    tail = "*1" if numeric else '&""'
    return (f'=IF({picker}="","",IFERROR(INDEX([[{sheet}.{field}]],'
            f'MATCH({picker},[[{sheet}.{key}]],0)){tail},'
            f'{0 if numeric else chr(34) * 2}))')


def _doc_line(s: SheetWriter, fmts, row: int, label: str,
              formula: str, style: str = "text") -> int:
    """`Label | value` inside a printed document."""
    s.merge(row, 1, 2, label, fmts.chrome("form_label"))
    s.merge(row, 3, 6, None, fmts.calc(style))
    s.formula(row, 3, formula, fmts.calc(style))
    return row + 1


def _money_line(s: SheetWriter, fmts, row: int, label: str,
                formula: str, strong: bool = False) -> int:
    """A right-aligned money line on a document."""
    s.merge(row, 4, 6, label,
            fmts.chrome("total_label" if strong else "label_right"))
    s.formula(row, 7, formula,
              fmts.chrome("total_value") if strong
              else fmts.calc("money"))
    return row + 1


def _printable_quote(s: SheetWriter, fmts, cfg: Config, row: int) -> int:
    """A quote on one page, built from the row you name."""
    quote = "Quote Calculator"
    row = s.section(row, "Printable quote - pick one and print it",
                    LAST_COL)
    row = s.field(
        row, "Quote to print",
        "QTE-0001" if cfg.include_sample_data else None,
        name="PrintQuote", help_col=4,
        validate={"validate": "list", "source": "=QuoteIDList",
                  "input_message": "Pick the quote you want on "
                                   "paper."},
        help_text="Every figure below comes from that row of the "
                  "Quote Calculator.")
    row += 1

    s.merge(row, 0, 5, None, fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.formula(row, 0, '=BusinessName&" - quote"',
              fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.merge(row, 6, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    s.formula(row, 6, '="Quote "&PrintQuote&"   "&'
                      'TEXT(TodayDate,"d mmmm yyyy")',
              fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    row += 1
    s.merge(row, 0, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.formula(row, 0,
              '=BusinessAddress&", "&BusinessCity&"    "&'
              'BusinessPhone&"    "&BusinessEmail&'
              'IF(BusinessLicence="","","    Licence "&'
              'BusinessLicence)', fmts.chrome("note"))
    row += 2

    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "Quote for",
                    _pull(quote, "customer_name", "id", "PrintQuote"))
    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "Property",
                    _pull(quote, "address", "id", "PrintQuote"))
    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "Work quoted",
                    _pull(quote, "service", "id", "PrintQuote"))
    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "What is included",
                    _pull(quote, "description", "id", "PrintQuote"),
                    style="wrap")
    s.row_height(row - 1, 30)
    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "How often",
                    _pull(quote, "frequency", "id", "PrintQuote"))
    row = _doc_line(
        row=row, s=s, fmts=fmts, label="Time on site",
        formula='=IF(PrintQuote="","",IFERROR(TEXT(INDEX('
                '[[Quote Calculator.site_hours]],MATCH(PrintQuote,'
                '[[Quote Calculator.id]],0)),"0.0")&" hours with "&'
                'INDEX([[Quote Calculator.crew_size]],MATCH('
                'PrintQuote,[[Quote Calculator.id]],0))&" on site",'
                '""))')
    row += 1

    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Price for the visit",
                      _pull(quote, "price_used", "id", "PrintQuote",
                            numeric=True))
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Discount",
                      _pull(quote, "discount", "id", "PrintQuote",
                            numeric=True))
    s.merge(row, 4, 6, None, fmts.chrome("label_right"))
    s.formula(row, 4, '=IF(TaxOnServices="Yes",TaxName&" at "&'
                      'TEXT(TaxRate,"0.0%"),TaxName)',
              fmts.chrome("label_right"))
    s.formula(row, 7, _pull(quote, "tax", "id", "PrintQuote",
                            numeric=True), fmts.calc("money"))
    row += 1
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Total",
                      _pull(quote, "total", "id", "PrintQuote",
                            numeric=True), strong=True)
    row = _money_line(
        s, fmts, row, "A year of these visits",
        _pull(quote, "annual_value", "id", "PrintQuote", numeric=True))
    row += 1

    s.merge(row, 0, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.formula(row, 0,
              '="This quote stands until "&IF(PrintQuote="","",'
              'IFERROR(TEXT(INDEX([[Quote Calculator.valid_until]],'
              'MATCH(PrintQuote,[[Quote Calculator.id]],0)),'
              '"d mmmm yyyy"),""))&". Prices assume reasonable '
              'access and normal growth; anything we find that '
              'changes the work, we will tell you before we do it."',
              fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.row_height(row, 28)
    row += 2
    return row


def _printable_invoice(s: SheetWriter, fmts, cfg: Config,
                       row: int) -> int:
    """An invoice on one page, built from the row you name."""
    inv = "Invoice & Payments"
    row = s.section(row, "Printable invoice - pick one and print it",
                    LAST_COL)
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "This is the document the Invoice & Payments tab refers to. "
        "Pick an invoice, print this block - File, Print, Print "
        "Selection after highlighting it - and record what the "
        "customer pays back on that tab. The workbook cannot send "
        "an email or take a card payment.",
        LAST_COL, height=34)
    row = s.field(
        row, "Invoice to print",
        "INV-00001" if cfg.include_sample_data else None,
        name="PrintInvoice", help_col=4,
        validate={"validate": "list", "source": "=InvoiceIDList",
                  "input_message": "Pick the invoice you want on "
                                   "paper."},
        help_text="Marked on the invoice row as the print line.")
    row += 1

    s.merge(row, 0, 5, None, fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.formula(row, 0, '=BusinessName&" - invoice"',
              fmts.chrome("print_title"))
    s.merge(row, 6, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    s.formula(row, 6,
              '="Invoice "&IF(PrintInvoice="","",IFERROR(IF(INDEX('
              '[[Invoice & Payments.invoice_number]],MATCH('
              'PrintInvoice,[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0))="",'
              'PrintInvoice,INDEX([[Invoice & Payments.'
              'invoice_number]],MATCH(PrintInvoice,'
              '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0))),PrintInvoice))',
              fmts.chrome("print_sub"))
    row += 1
    s.merge(row, 0, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.formula(row, 0,
              '=BusinessAddress&", "&BusinessCity&"    "&'
              'BusinessPhone&"    "&BusinessEmail',
              fmts.chrome("note"))
    row += 2

    row = _doc_line(s, fmts, row, "Bill to",
                    _pull(inv, "customer_name", "id", "PrintInvoice"))
    row = _doc_line(
        s, fmts, row, "Work done",
        _pull(inv, "description", "id", "PrintInvoice"), style="wrap")
    s.row_height(row - 1, 30)
    row = _doc_line(
        s, fmts, row, "Period covered",
        _pull(inv, "period", "id", "PrintInvoice"))
    row = _doc_line(
        s, fmts, row, "Invoice date",
        '=IF(PrintInvoice="","",IFERROR(TEXT(INDEX('
        '[[Invoice & Payments.date]],MATCH(PrintInvoice,'
        '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0)),"d mmmm yyyy"),""))')
    row = _doc_line(
        s, fmts, row, "Payment due",
        '=IF(PrintInvoice="","",IFERROR(TEXT(INDEX('
        '[[Invoice & Payments.due_date]],MATCH(PrintInvoice,'
        '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0)),"d mmmm yyyy")&"   ("&'
        'INDEX([[Invoice & Payments.terms]],MATCH(PrintInvoice,'
        '[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0))&")",""))')
    row += 1

    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Net",
                      _pull(inv, "net", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True))
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Discount",
                      _pull(inv, "discount", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True))
    s.merge(row, 4, 6, None, fmts.chrome("label_right"))
    s.formula(row, 4, '=IF(TaxOnServices="Yes",TaxName&" at "&'
                      'TEXT(TaxRate,"0.0%"),TaxName)',
              fmts.chrome("label_right"))
    s.formula(row, 7, _pull(inv, "tax", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True), fmts.calc("money"))
    row += 1
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Total",
                      _pull(inv, "total", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True), strong=True)
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Already paid",
                      _pull(inv, "paid", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True))
    row = _money_line(s, fmts, row, "Now due",
                      _pull(inv, "outstanding", "id", "PrintInvoice",
                            numeric=True), strong=True)
    row += 1

    s.merge(row, 0, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.formula(row, 0,
              '="Please pay "&CurrencySign&TEXT(IF(PrintInvoice="",'
              '0,IFERROR(INDEX([[Invoice & Payments.outstanding]],'
              'MATCH(PrintInvoice,[[Invoice & Payments.id]],0)),0)),'
              '"#,##0.00")&" to "&BusinessName&" by the due date '
              'above. Questions about this invoice: "&BusinessPhone&'
              '". Thank you for your custom."',
              fmts.chrome("note"))
    s.row_height(row, 28)
    row += 2
    return row
````

---

## 7. The recurring round

Workflow three, and the half of this product a one-off job sheet does not have. A contract carries a frequency, a visit price and a renewal date; it works out its own next visit from the last one, tells you when nothing is booked, values itself over a year and warns before it lapses. The seasonal planner turns the year into four blocks of work with the maintenance and inspection checklists the crew actually uses.

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/contracts.py`

*280 lines · 1 function*

> 10. Recurring Contracts - the income you can count on.
>
> A contract says: this service, at this property, every so many days,
> for this much. From that the sheet works out the next visit date, how
> many visits are left this season, what the agreement is worth over a
> year, and when it needs renewing.
>
> The scheduler is deliberately a calculator, not a robot. It tells you
> the next date a visit is due; you still create the job. A workbook
> that silently invented rows would be a workbook you could not trust.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Recurring Contracts').

````python
"""10. Recurring Contracts - the income you can count on.

A contract says: this service, at this property, every so many days,
for this much. From that the sheet works out the next visit date, how
many visits are left this season, what the agreement is worth over a
year, and when it needs renewing.

The scheduler is deliberately a calculator, not a robot. It tells you
the next date a visit is due; you still create the job. A workbook
that silently invented rows would be a workbook you could not trust.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, customer_col,
                     date_num_col, id_col, is_live_contract,
                     lookup_customer, lookup_property, money_cf,
                     property_col, scale_cf, service_col, status_cf,
                     sum_by, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Recurring Contracts"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Recurring Contracts",
        subtitle="Standing agreements: what, where, how often, how "
                 "much, and when the next visit is due.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary_mid,
        rows=cfg.row_count("contracts"),
        samples=sample("contracts", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "Set up one row per standing agreement. The next due date "
            "is worked out from the last completed visit and the "
            "frequency, so it moves on by itself as the work gets "
            "done. When a visit is due, create it on the Job & Work "
            "Orders tab and put this Contract ID on it."),
        extra_notes=[
            "This is a scheduling calculator, not an automatic "
            "booking system. It tells you what is due; you decide "
            "what actually gets booked, because weather, holidays and "
            "a broken mower all have a vote.",
            "Annual value is the honest measure of a contract. Twelve "
            "visits at $45 is a $540 customer, and that is the number "
            "to think about before offering a discount.",
            "Renewal reminders fire on the number of days set on "
            "Settings. Renewing a good contract is far cheaper than "
            "finding a new customer.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("start_date", "ContractPrefix",
                   digits=cfg.digits("contract"), header="Contract ID",
                   note="Generated. Put it on every job that comes from "
                        "this agreement."),
            customer_col(),
            Col("customer_name", "Customer", 20, "text",
                formula=lookup_customer("name")),
            property_col(),
            Col("address", "Address", 26, "text",
                formula=lookup_property("address")),
            service_col(),
            Col("frequency", "Frequency", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("FrequencyList"),
                note="How often the visit repeats. Drives both the next "
                     "due date and the annual value."),
            Col("day_pref", "Preferred day", 12, "text",
                validate=list_from("WeekdayList"),
                note="'Always Tuesdays' keeps a round tight and the "
                     "customer happy."),
            Col("start_date", "Starts", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here. The row comes alive with a date."),
            Col("end_date", "Ends", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Leave blank for a rolling agreement. Fill it in "
                     "for a fixed season."),
            Col("season_only", "Season only", 11, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Does this pause out of season?"),
                note="Mowing stops in winter; snow clearing does not. "
                     "The season months are on Settings."),
            Col("visit_price", "Price per visit", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "What the customer pays for each "
                                "visit under this agreement."),
                note="The job register uses this automatically for "
                     "visits carrying this Contract ID."),
            Col("billing", "Billed", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("PaymentTermsList"),
                note="How this one is invoiced - per visit, monthly in "
                     "advance, however you agreed it."),
            Col("po_number", "PO number", 14, "text",
                note="Commercial accounts often will not pay without "
                     "one."),
            Col("status", "Status", 13, "text",
                validate=list_from("ContractStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("renewal_date", "Review on", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="When you will revisit the price. Put it in the "
                     "diary before the season starts, not during it."),
            Col("price_review", "Last price change", 13, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Two seasons without a change, with fuel and wages "
                     "where they are, is a quiet pay cut."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 30, "wrap"),

            # ---- what it is worth ------------------------------------
            Col("visits_year", "Visits / year", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IFERROR(INDEX(VisitsPerYear,'
                             'MATCH({frequency}{r},FrequencyList,0))*1,'
                             '0)'),
                note="From the frequency list on Settings."),
            Col("gap_days", "Days between", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IFERROR(INDEX(DaysBetween,'
                             'MATCH({frequency}{r},FrequencyList,0))*1,'
                             '0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("annual_value", "Annual value", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'ROUND({visit_price}{r}*'
                             '{visits_year}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="Price per visit times visits per year. The number "
                     "that says how much this customer matters."),
            Col("monthly_value", "Per month", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'ROUND({annual_value}{r}/12,2)'),
                note="The same money spread evenly - useful when a "
                     "customer wants a flat monthly bill."),
            Col("visits_done", "Visits done", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                    "contract", '{id}{r}',
                                                    "is_done", "1")),
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("visits_booked", "Visits booked", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date", count_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                    "contract",
                                                    '{id}{r}',
                                                    "is_open", "1"))),
            Col("revenue", "Revenue to date", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("start_date", sum_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                  "charge", "contract",
                                                  '{id}{r}',
                                                  "is_done", "1")),
                cf=money_cf()),
            Col("cost_total", "Cost to date", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("start_date", sum_by("Job & Work Orders",
                                                  "cost", "contract",
                                                  '{id}{r}',
                                                  "is_done", "1"))),
            Col("profit", "Profit to date", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'ROUND({revenue}{r}-{cost_total}{r},2)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "good"}]),
            Col("margin_pct", "Margin", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({start_date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({revenue}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({profit}{r}/{revenue}{r},4)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=TargetMargin", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=MinMargin", "fmt": "bad"}],
                note="A contract below your floor is one to renegotiate "
                     "at review, not to cancel in a temper."),

            # ---- when the next one is due ----------------------------
            Col("last_num", "Last visit #", 11, "number",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.contract]]={id}{r})*'
                             '([[Job & Work Orders.is_done]]=1)*'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]]))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("last_visit", "Last visit", 11, "date",
                formula='=IF({start_date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({last_num}{r}<=0,"",{last_num}{r}))'),
            Col("next_num", "Next due #", 10, "number",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IF({is_live}{r}=0,0,'
                             'IF({gap_days}{r}<=0,0,'
                             'IF({last_num}{r}<=0,{start_date}{r},'
                             '{last_num}{r}+{gap_days}{r})))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("next_due", "Next due", 11, "date",
                formula='=IF({start_date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({next_num}{r}<=0,"",'
                        '{next_num}{r}))',
                cf=[{"type": "formula",
                     "criteria": '=AND(ISNUMBER({next_due}{r}),'
                                 '{next_due}{r}<TodayDate)',
                     "fmt": "bad"}],
                note="Last completed visit plus the gap, or the start "
                     "date if nothing has happened yet."),
            Col("days_to_next", "Days to next", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IF({next_num}{r}<=0,0,'
                             '{next_num}{r}-TodayDate)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=", "value": 7,
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Negative means the visit is already late."),
            Col("booked_next", "Next booked", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'COUNTIFS([[Job & Work Orders.contract]],'
                             '{id}{r},[[Job & Work Orders.is_open]],1)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("days_to_renew", "Days to review", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IF({renewal_date}{r}="",0,'
                             '{renewal_date}{r}-TodayDate)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=",
                     "value": "=RenewalWarnDays", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("days_to_end", "Days to end", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IF({end_date}{r}="",9999,'
                             '{end_date}{r}-TodayDate)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_live", "Live", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             f'IF(AND({is_live_contract("{status}{r}")},'
                             '({end_date}{r}="")+'
                             '({end_date}{r}>=TodayDate)>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_due", "Due", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("start_date",
                             'IF(AND({is_live}{r}=1,{next_num}{r}>0,'
                             '{next_num}{r}<=TodayDate+UpcomingDays,'
                             '{booked_next}{r}=0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            date_num_col("next_due", "next_date_num", header="Next #"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("start_date", [
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{days_to_next}{r}<0,'
                     '{booked_next}{r}=0)',
                     "Overdue - a visit is past due and nothing is "
                     "booked"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{visit_price}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no price agreed, so the visits earn nothing"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{days_to_renew}{r}<0,'
                     '{renewal_date}{r}<>"")',
                     "Renew - the review date has passed"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{renewal_date}{r}<>"",'
                     '{days_to_renew}{r}<=RenewalWarnDays)',
                     "Renew - review date coming up, agree the new "
                     "price"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{end_date}{r}<>"",'
                     '{days_to_end}{r}<=RenewalWarnDays,'
                     '{days_to_end}{r}>=0)',
                     "Renew - this agreement ends soon"),
                    ('AND({revenue}{r}>0,{profit}{r}<0)',
                     "Loss - this contract is costing you money"),
                    ('{is_due}{r}=1',
                     "Schedule - next visit due, create the work order"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{margin_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{margin_pct}{r}<MinMargin)',
                     "Thin margin - raise it at the next review"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{price_review}{r}<>"",'
                     'TodayDate-{price_review}{r}>730)',
                     "Check - the price has not changed in two years"),
                    ('AND({is_live}{r}=1,{renewal_date}{r}="")',
                     "Add - no review date set"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/seasonal.py`

*318 lines · 1 function*

> `Seasonal Planner` - the year, four blocks at a time.
>
> Lawn care is the same twelve months over and over, and almost
> everything that goes wrong is something that should have been booked
> six weeks earlier. This page is the list: what to sell, what to
> service and what to order, season by season, with the numbers from
> your own records sitting next to each block so you can see what that
> season has actually been worth.
>
> The two checklists at the bottom are the ones crews are most often
> missing: a maintenance visit checklist, and a property inspection to
> run before quoting a site you have never seen.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `build(book, fmts, cfg, reg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Seasonal Planner'), `LAST_COL` (10), `TASK_ROWS` (7), `SEASON_PLAN` (4 items), `MAINTENANCE_CHECKLIST` (12 items), `INSPECTION_CHECKLIST` (12 items).

````python
"""`Seasonal Planner` - the year, four blocks at a time.

Lawn care is the same twelve months over and over, and almost
everything that goes wrong is something that should have been booked
six weeks earlier. This page is the list: what to sell, what to
service and what to order, season by season, with the numbers from
your own records sitting next to each block so you can see what that
season has actually been worth.

The two checklists at the bottom are the ones crews are most often
missing: a maintenance visit checklist, and a property inspection to
run before quoting a site you have never seen.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Config, Palette
from ..layout import Registry
from ..writer import SheetWriter

NAME = "Seasonal Planner"
LAST_COL = 10
TASK_ROWS = 7

#: season -> (months, what to sell, what to prepare)
SEASON_PLAN = [
    ("Spring", "March to May", 3, 5, [
        ("Spring clean-ups", "The biggest selling window of the year. "
                             "Quote these in February, not April."),
        ("First cuts and edging", "Set the round order now and it "
                                  "holds for the whole season."),
        ("Fertiliser and pre-emergent", "Timing beats product. Book "
                                        "it by soil temperature, not "
                                        "by the calendar."),
        ("Mulch and bed refresh", "High margin, and it sells itself "
                                  "once the first warm weekend "
                                  "arrives."),
        ("Aeration and overseeding", "Cool season grass only. Ask "
                                     "before you quote it."),
        ("Irrigation start-up", "Check every head before you turn "
                                "the water on, not after."),
        ("Contract renewals", "Agree this season's price before the "
                              "first cut, never halfway through."),
    ]),
    ("Summer", "June to August", 6, 8, [
        ("Weekly and fortnightly cutting", "The core of the round. "
                                           "Protect it."),
        ("Hedge trimming", "Check for nesting birds first - in many "
                           "places that is the law, not a courtesy."),
        ("Weed control", "Licensed work in most places. Check who on "
                         "the crew can legally do it."),
        ("Irrigation checks", "A dry August is when customers notice "
                              "a head that has not worked since "
                              "May."),
        ("Watering advice", "Free, takes two minutes, and saves the "
                            "lawn you will be blamed for."),
        ("Holiday cover", "Book crew holidays around the round, and "
                          "tell customers before they notice."),
        ("Mid-season equipment service", "The one everybody skips. "
                                         "It is also the one that "
                                         "stops August breakdowns."),
    ]),
    ("Autumn / Fall", "September to November", 9, 11, [
        ("Leaf clearance", "Sell it as a series, not a single "
                           "visit. Three visits beats one argument."),
        ("Autumn feed", "Sets up next spring. Easy to sell to "
                        "anybody who liked their lawn this year."),
        ("Final cuts", "Agree the last cut date in writing so the "
                       "invoice is never a surprise."),
        ("Gutter and drain clearing", "Easy add-on while you are "
                                      "already there with a ladder."),
        ("Irrigation winterising", "Miss it and you will be quoted "
                                   "for the burst pipe."),
        ("Snow contracts", "Sign these in October. Nobody signs a "
                           "snow contract during a blizzard at a "
                           "sensible price."),
        ("Next year's pricing", "Review every contract against this "
                                "year's actual hours."),
    ]),
    ("Winter", "December to February", 12, 2, [
        ("Snow and ice clearing", "If you offer it, agree the "
                                  "trigger depth and the rate "
                                  "before the first fall."),
        ("Pruning and dormant work", "Trees and shrubs are easier to "
                                     "read with no leaves on them."),
        ("Hardscaping and fencing", "Fills the quiet weeks and keeps "
                                    "the crew together."),
        ("Full equipment service", "Every machine, in the workshop, "
                                   "before March. Blades, belts, "
                                   "filters, plugs."),
        ("Book next season", "Ring every customer. The round you "
                             "confirm in January is the round you "
                             "have in April."),
        ("Marketing and quotes", "Spring enquiries start in "
                                 "February. Be ready in January."),
        ("Accounts and insurance", "Renewals, licences, tax. Do it "
                                   "now; you will not have time in "
                                   "May."),
    ]),
]

MAINTENANCE_CHECKLIST = [
    "Walk the property before starting - look for toys, hoses, pet "
    "mess, stones and anything that will become a projectile.",
    "Check the mowing height is right for the season and the grass "
    "type. Scalping in July is how you lose a customer.",
    "Blades sharp and clean. A blunt blade tears the grass and the "
    "lawn goes brown three days later, long after you have left.",
    "Mow in a different direction from last visit where you can - it "
    "stops ruts and keeps the grass standing up.",
    "Edge and strim everything the job includes, every visit. Missed "
    "edges are the most common complaint in this trade.",
    "Blow all hard surfaces clear: paths, drive, patio, and the road "
    "outside.",
    "Clippings dealt with the way the customer agreed - left, bagged "
    "or taken away.",
    "Beds and borders checked for weeds if the job includes them.",
    "Gates closed and latched. Every single time.",
    "Tools counted back onto the trailer before you drive off.",
    "Damage, hazards and anything odd photographed and reported the "
    "same day.",
    "Actual time written down before the next job starts.",
]

INSPECTION_CHECKLIST = [
    "Measure the lawn properly - pace it or use a map tool. Guessing "
    "the area is guessing the price.",
    "Note the slope and the surface. Steep, wet or rough ground can "
    "add half as much again to the time.",
    "Check access: gate width, steps, locked gates, who holds a key, "
    "whether the ride-on can get in at all.",
    "Count the obstacles - trees, beds, play equipment, washing "
    "poles. Every one is a turn and a slow-down.",
    "Look for hazards: ponds, septic lids, irrigation heads, "
    "retaining walls, a bad step, overhead cables.",
    "Ask about dogs, and whether they are out during the day.",
    "Find out where the truck and trailer will park, and whether "
    "there is a time limit.",
    "Check for water and power on site if the work needs them.",
    "Agree what happens to the clippings before you quote, not "
    "after.",
    "Ask what they were unhappy about with the last company. It is "
    "the most useful question you will ask all day.",
    "Photograph the site - before photos are worth money later.",
    "Write the drive time and mileage from your yard onto the "
    "property record.",
]


def build(book, fmts, cfg: Config, reg: Registry) -> SheetWriter:
    s = SheetWriter(book, fmts, cfg, reg, NAME,
                    tab_color=Palette.primary_light)

    s.banner("Seasonal Planner",
             "What to sell, service and order in each season - with "
             "what that season has actually earned you.",
             LAST_COL)
    s.set_col(0, 0, 4)
    s.set_col(1, 1, 30)
    s.set_col(2, 2, 52)
    s.set_col(3, 3, 13)
    s.set_col(4, 4, 13)
    s.set_col(5, LAST_COL, 14)

    row = 4
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Everything here is a prompt, not a rule. Grass types, "
        "climates and local regulations differ enormously - what "
        "follows is the shape of a temperate season, and you should "
        "edit the tasks to match the ground you actually work on. "
        "The figures on the right are your own, from your own job "
        "records.",
        LAST_COL, height=44)
    row += 1

    # --------------------------------------------------- where we are --
    row = s.section(row, "Right now", LAST_COL)
    s.kpi(row, 0, "Season",
          '=IF(MONTH(TodayDate)<=2,"Winter",'
          'IF(MONTH(TodayDate)<=5,"Spring",'
          'IF(MONTH(TodayDate)<=8,"Summer",'
          'IF(MONTH(TodayDate)<=11,"Autumn / Fall","Winter"))))',
          "text", note="Worked out from today's date")
    s.kpi(row, 2, "In season?",
          '=IF(AND(MONTH(TodayDate)>=SeasonStart,'
          'MONTH(TodayDate)<=SeasonEnd),"Yes - main season",'
          '"No - out of season")', "text",
          note="Against the months on Settings")
    s.kpi(row, 4, "Jobs this month",
          '=COUNTIFS(JobMonth,ThisMonthKey,JobIsDone,1)', "int",
          note="Completed so far")
    s.kpi(row, 6, "Revenue this month",
          '=SUMIFS(JobCharge,JobMonth,ThisMonthKey,JobIsDone,1)',
          "money")
    row = s.kpi(row, 8, "Contracts live",
                "=SUM(ConIsLive)", "int", span=2,
                note="Standing agreements in force")
    row += 1

    # ----------------------------------------------- the four seasons --
    for name, months, first_month, last_month in [
            (p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3]) for p in SEASON_PLAN]:
        plan = dict((p[0], p[4]) for p in SEASON_PLAN)[name]
        row = s.section(row, f"{name}  -  {months}", LAST_COL)

        # the season's own numbers, from the job register
        if first_month <= last_month:
            in_season = (f'(MONTH(JobDateNum)>={first_month})*'
                         f'(MONTH(JobDateNum)<={last_month})')
        else:                      # winter wraps the year end
            in_season = (f'((MONTH(JobDateNum)>={first_month})+'
                         f'(MONTH(JobDateNum)<={last_month}))')
        s.merge(row, 1, 2, "What this season has earned you, all "
                           "years", fmts.chrome("label_bold"))
        s.formula(row, 3,
                  f'=SUMPRODUCT({in_season}*JobIsDone*JobCharge)',
                  fmts.calc("money"))
        s.formula(row, 4,
                  f'=SUMPRODUCT({in_season}*JobIsDone)',
                  fmts.calc("int"))
        s.merge(row, 5, LAST_COL, None, fmts.chrome("note"))
        s.write(row, 5, "Revenue and completed visits in these "
                        "months, from your own records",
                fmts.chrome("note"))
        row += 1

        s.table_head(row, 1, ["Task", "Why it matters", "Start by",
                              "Done?", "Who", "Notes"],
                     widths=[30, 52, 13, 13, 14, 20])
        head = row
        for index, (task, why) in enumerate(plan[:TASK_ROWS]):
            line = head + 1 + index
            s.write(line, 1, task, fmts.chrome("label_bold"))
            s.write(line, 2, why, fmts.chrome("note"))
            s.write(line, 3, None, fmts.input("date"))
            s.write(line, 4, None, fmts.input("flag"))
            s.write(line, 5, None, fmts.input("text"))
            s.merge(line, 6, LAST_COL, None, fmts.input("text"))
            s.ws.data_validation(
                line, 4, line, 4,
                {"validate": "list", "source": ["Yes", "No"]})
            s.row_height(line, 26)
        row = head + len(plan[:TASK_ROWS]) + 2

    # -------------------------------------------- maintenance checklist --
    row = s.section(row, "Lawn maintenance checklist - every visit",
                    LAST_COL)
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Print this and put it in the truck. Most complaints in this "
        "trade are not about the quality of the cut - they are about "
        "an open gate, a missed edge or clippings on the drive.",
        LAST_COL, height=30)
    row += 1
    s.table_head(row, 1, ["Check", "Done"], widths=[70, 10])
    head = row
    for index, item in enumerate(MAINTENANCE_CHECKLIST):
        line = head + 1 + index
        s.merge(line, 1, 5, item, fmts.chrome("day_item"))
        s.write(line, 6, None, fmts.chrome("form_box"))
        s.row_height(line, 24)
    row = head + len(MAINTENANCE_CHECKLIST) + 2

    # --------------------------------------------- inspection checklist --
    row = s.section(row, "Property inspection checklist - before you "
                         "quote", LAST_COL)
    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Twelve things to establish on a site you have never worked. "
        "Every one of them is a number or a fact that belongs on the "
        "Property Database, and every one of them changes the price.",
        LAST_COL, height=30)
    row += 1
    s.table_head(row, 1, ["Check", "Noted"], widths=[70, 10])
    head = row
    for index, item in enumerate(INSPECTION_CHECKLIST):
        line = head + 1 + index
        s.merge(line, 1, 5, item, fmts.chrome("day_item"))
        s.write(line, 6, None, fmts.chrome("form_box"))
        s.row_height(line, 24)
    row = head + len(INSPECTION_CHECKLIST) + 2

    # ------------------------------------------------ seasonal reminders --
    row = s.section(row, "Reminders that pay for themselves", LAST_COL)
    row = s.bullets(row, [
        "Renew contracts before the season starts, never during it. "
        "A price agreed in February is a conversation; the same "
        "price raised in June is an argument.",
        "Service every machine in the quiet months. A mower that "
        "fails in July costs a week of the best weather of the "
        "year.",
        "Order bulk materials out of season. Mulch and fertiliser "
        "are cheapest when nobody wants them.",
        "Ring dormant customers at the start of spring. The "
        "Customer CRM tab marks them for you after the number of "
        "days you set on Settings.",
        "Check licence and insurance expiry dates in the winter. The "
        "Crew Database warns you sixty days out.",
        "Take before and after photos through the season. In "
        "February they are the only marketing material you will "
        "have.",
    ], LAST_COL)
    row += 1

    row = s.note(
        row,
        "Local regulation beats anything written here. Pesticide "
        "and fertiliser application, hedge cutting during nesting "
        "season, green waste disposal and water use are all "
        "controlled differently from one place to the next, and some "
        "of them carry real penalties. Check what applies where you "
        "work before you sell it.",
        LAST_COL, height=48)

    s.finish(freeze_row=4, gridlines=False, fit_width=True)
    s.protect()
    return s
````

---

## 8. Crew, schedule and payroll hours

Workflow four. The crew database holds the pay rate, the licence expiry and what each person has earned you; the schedule compares planned hours against capacity day by day; the timesheets split overtime out on a weekly running total, cost every hour at that person's rate, and hand the job its real labour cost. Gross figures for pricing, not a payroll filing - the workbook says so where you can read it.

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/crew.py`

*228 lines · 1 function*

> 11. Crew Database - the people, their rates and their paperwork.
>
> The hourly cost recorded here is what the whole workbook uses to work
> out what a job cost. It should be the loaded cost - wage plus payroll
> tax plus insurance plus the share of the truck they ride in - not the
> figure on their payslip. If you put the payslip number in, every job
> in the workbook will look more profitable than it is.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Crew Database').

````python
"""11. Crew Database - the people, their rates and their paperwork.

The hourly cost recorded here is what the whole workbook uses to work
out what a job cost. It should be the loaded cost - wage plus payroll
tax plus insurance plus the share of the truck they ride in - not the
figure on their payslip. If you put the payslip number in, every job
in the workbook will look more profitable than it is.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import ACTIVE_CREW_STATUSES, Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, id_col,
                     in_list, money_cf, rank_desc, rating_cf, scale_cf,
                     status_cf, sum_by, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Crew Database"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Crew Database",
        subtitle="Who works for you, what they cost per hour, what "
                 "they are qualified to do and when it expires.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary_mid,
        rows=cfg.row_count("crew"),
        samples=sample("crew", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "Add each person once. The Employee ID appears "
            "automatically and feeds the timesheets, where hours are "
            "costed at that person's own rate. Expiry dates for "
            "licences and certificates are tracked here and warn you "
            "before they lapse."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Hourly cost should be the loaded cost, not the wage. A "
            "$20 wage usually costs the business $26 to $30 once tax, "
            "insurance and downtime are counted. Guessing low here "
            "makes every job in the workbook look better than it is.",
            "Keep people who have left, with status 'Left'. Their "
            "hours are part of last season's costs, and deleting the "
            "row leaves those timesheets pointing at nobody.",
            "This is a record of hours and rates for your own "
            "planning. It is not a payroll system and it does not "
            "calculate tax withholding - your payroll provider or "
            "accountant does that.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("name", "CrewPrefix", digits=cfg.digits("crew"),
                   header="Employee ID",
                   note="Generated. The timesheets point at this."),
            Col("name", "Name", 22, "text",
                note="Start here."),
            Col("role", "Role", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewRoleList")),
            Col("crew", "Usual crew", 12, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewNameList"),
                note="Which truck they normally ride with."),
            Col("employment", "Employment", 16, "text",
                validate=list_from("EmploymentTypeList"),
                note="Seasonal and subcontract people cost differently "
                     "and are worth separating."),
            Col("phone", "Phone", 15, "text"),
            Col("email", "Email", 24, "text"),
            Col("emergency", "Emergency contact", 22, "text",
                note="Name and number. The one field you hope never to "
                     "use, and must have when you do."),
            Col("start_date", "Started", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("end_date", "Left", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Fill in rather than deleting the row."),
            Col("status", "Status", 12, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("pay_rate", "Pay rate / hr", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000, "What you pay them per hour."),
                note="The wage itself, for reference and for payroll."),
            Col("cost_rate", "Cost to you / hr", 13, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000,
                                "Wage plus payroll tax plus insurance "
                                "plus overheads attached to this "
                                "person."),
                note="The real number. This is what costs every job in "
                     "the workbook. Blank falls back to the crew rate "
                     "on Settings."),
            Col("overtime_rate", "Overtime x", 10, "number1",
                validate=amount(1, 5,
                                "Multiplier for hours over the weekly "
                                "threshold."),
                note="Blank uses the multiplier on Settings."),
            Col("licence", "Licence / cert", 20, "text",
                note="Pesticide applicator, chainsaw ticket, CDL. The "
                     "ones that stop work if they lapse."),
            Col("licence_expiry", "Expires", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                cf=[{"type": "formula",
                     "criteria": '=AND(ISNUMBER({licence_expiry}{r}),'
                                 '{licence_expiry}{r}<TodayDate)',
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"}],
                note="Warned about well before the date, and in red "
                     "after it."),
            Col("insurance_expiry", "Insurance to", 12, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("licence_days", "Days to expiry", 12, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({licence_expiry}{r}="",9999,'
                             '{licence_expiry}{r}-TodayDate)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("can_spray", "Can spray", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Are they licensed to apply pesticide or "
                               "fertiliser?"),
                note="Jobs flagged as needing a licence should only go "
                     "to people marked Yes here."),
            Col("can_drive", "Can drive", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno()),
            Col("equipment_ok", "Machinery trained", 14, "wrap",
                note="Which machines they are signed off on. A free "
                     "text note, because every yard is different."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 28, "wrap"),

            # ---- what they actually do -------------------------------
            Col("hours_total", "Hours logged", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "hours", "employee",
                                            '{id}{r}')),
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("hours_year", "Hours this year", 13, "number1",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by(
                    "Timesheets & Payroll", "hours", "employee",
                    '{id}{r}',
                    "date_num", '">="&YearStart',
                    "date_num", '"<="&YearEnd'))),
            Col("hours_billable", "Billable hours", 13, "number1",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "hours", "employee",
                                            '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_billable", "1")),
                note="Hours booked to a job, as opposed to travel, "
                     "yard time and machine repairs."),
            Col("billable_pct", "Billable %", 10, "percent",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_total}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({hours_billable}{r}/'
                        '{hours_total}{r},4)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 0.75,
                     "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0.5,
                     "fmt": "bad"}],
                note="The number that explains a bad month better than "
                     "anything else. Below 60% means the day is being "
                     "eaten by driving and faffing."),
            Col("cost_total", "Labour cost", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "cost", "employee",
                                            '{id}{r}'))),
            Col("revenue", "Revenue worked on", 15, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "job_value", "employee",
                                            '{id}{r}')),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="Their share of what the jobs they worked on were "
                     "worth. A rough measure, but a fair one."),
            Col("value_per_hour", "Value per hour", 13, "money",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_billable}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({revenue}{r}/'
                        '{hours_billable}{r},2)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<",
                     "value": "=ChargeRate", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("jobs_done", "Jobs worked", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                              "employee", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_billable", "1"))),
            Col("rating", "Quality score", 12, "number1",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(AVERAGEIFS('
                        '[[Customer Feedback.score]],'
                        '[[Customer Feedback.crew]],{crew}{r},'
                        '[[Customer Feedback.score]],">0"),""))',
                cf=rating_cf(),
                note="Feedback for the crew they ride with, since "
                     "customers rate the visit, not the individual."),
            Col("overtime_hours", "Overtime hrs", 11, "number1",
````
