````python
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Timesheets & Payroll",
                                            "overtime_hours", "employee",
                                            '{id}{r}')),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("is_active", "Active", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             f'IF({in_list("{status}{r}", ACTIVE_CREW_STATUSES)},'
                             '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            rank_desc("revenue", guard='{name}{r}=""'),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 44, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("name", [
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{licence}{r}<>"",'
                     '{licence_days}{r}<0)',
                     "Expired - licence has lapsed, they cannot do "
                     "that work"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{cost_rate}{r}=0,'
                     '{pay_rate}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no rate set, so their hours cost nothing"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{licence}{r}<>"",'
                     '{licence_days}{r}<=60)',
                     "Renew - licence expires within two months"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{emergency}{r}="")',
                     "Add - no emergency contact on file"),
                    ('{overtime_hours}{r}>0',
                     "Check - overtime logged, is the round too big?"),
                    ('AND({hours_total}{r}>0,{billable_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{billable_pct}{r}<0.5)',
                     "Check - under half their hours are billable"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{hours_total}{r}=0)',
                     "Action - no hours ever logged for this person"),
                    ('AND({is_active}{r}=1,{cost_rate}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no loaded cost rate, jobs use the default"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/schedule.py`

*208 lines · 1 function*

> 12. Crew Schedule - who is working, and who is already busy.
>
> This is the people side of the week, as opposed to the Route Planner
> which is the stops side. One row per person per day: on, off, holiday
> or sick, and how many hours they are down for.
>
> The clash column is the point of the tab. It compares the hours
> assigned to a person on a day against the jobs actually booked for
> their crew that day, so a day that has been promised to two places at
> once shows up before the morning it goes wrong.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Crew Schedule').

````python
"""12. Crew Schedule - who is working, and who is already busy.

This is the people side of the week, as opposed to the Route Planner
which is the stops side. One row per person per day: on, off, holiday
or sick, and how many hours they are down for.

The clash column is the point of the tab. It compares the hours
assigned to a person on a day against the jobs actually booked for
their crew that day, so a day that has been promised to two places at
once shows up before the morning it goes wrong.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from, time_check
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, date_num_col, id_col,
                     lookup_crew, month_col, pick,
                     scale_cf, week_start_col, weekday_col,
                     yesno, zero)

NAME = "Crew Schedule"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Crew Schedule",
        subtitle="Who is available, who is off, and whether the day "
                 "you have planned actually fits in the hours.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary_mid,
        rows=cfg.row_count("schedule"),
        samples=sample("schedule", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "One row per person per working day. Put the date, pick "
            "the person, and say whether they are working. The "
            "'Planned hours' column adds up the jobs already booked "
            "for their crew that day, so you can see at a glance "
            "whether the day is full, empty or impossible."),
        extra_notes=[
            "You do not need a row for every person every day. Most "
            "businesses fill this in a week ahead for the days that "
            "are unusual - holidays, half days, somebody covering "
            "another crew - and leave the ordinary days blank.",
            "Planned hours counts every open job booked to that crew "
            "on that date, using the estimated hours from the "
            "catalogue. Capacity is their available hours. When "
            "planned is bigger than capacity, something will be late.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", '"SCH-"', digits=5, header="Line",
                   note="Just a line number, so two identical rows can "
                        "still be told apart."),
            Col("date", "Date", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here."),
            Col("weekday", "Day", 8, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'TEXT({date}{r},"ddd"))'),
            Col("employee", "Employee", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("CrewIDList",
                              "Pick the Employee ID from the Crew "
                              "Database."),
                note="Pick from the list, never type it."),
            Col("name", "Name", 20, "text",
                formula=lookup_crew("name")),
            Col("crew", "Crew", 12, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewNameList"),
                note="Which truck they are on that day. It is not "
                     "always their usual one."),
            Col("working", "Working", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Are they available for work on this "
                               "day?"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "No", "fmt": "muted"}]),
            Col("reason", "If not, why", 18, "text",
                validate=pick("AbsenceList",
                              "Holiday, sick, training, weather, or a "
                              "day off."),
                note="Six months of this column tells you what your "
                     "real available workforce is, as opposed to the "
                     "one on paper."),
            Col("start_time", "Start", 9, "time",
                validate=time_check()),
            Col("end_time", "End", 9, "time",
                validate=time_check()),
            Col("break_mins", "Break (mins)", 11, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 480, decimal=False)),
            Col("capacity", "Available hrs", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({working}{r}<>"Yes",0,'
                             'IF(AND({start_time}{r}<>"",'
                             '{end_time}{r}<>""),'
                             'ROUND(({end_time}{r}-{start_time}{r})*24-'
                             '{break_mins}{r}/60,2),WorkingHours))'),
                note="Worked out from the times if you gave them, "
                     "otherwise the standard working day on Settings."),
            Col("planned", "Planned hrs", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(SUMIFS('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.hours_est]],'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]],'
                             '{date}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.crew]],{crew}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.is_open]],1)/'
                             'MAX(1,CrewSize),2)'),
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Every open job booked to this crew on this date, "
                     "shared across the crew. Automatic."),
            Col("jobs_count", "Jobs booked", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'COUNTIFS('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.date_num]],{date}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.crew]],{crew}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.is_open]],1)')),
            Col("logged", "Hours logged", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'SUMIFS('
                             '[[Timesheets & Payroll.hours]],'
                             '[[Timesheets & Payroll.date_num]],'
                             '{date}{r},'
                             '[[Timesheets & Payroll.employee]],'
                             '{employee}{r})'),
                note="What the timesheets say actually happened. The "
                     "other half of the comparison."),
            Col("spare", "Spare hrs", 10, "number1",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'ROUND({capacity}{r}-{planned}{r},2))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": -0.01,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 2,
                     "fmt": "info"}],
                note="Negative means the day does not fit. Positive by "
                     "a lot means there is room to sell another job."),
            Col("use_pct", "Day used", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({capacity}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({planned}{r}/'
                        '{capacity}{r},4)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 1,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 0.8,
                     "fmt": "good"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0.5,
                     "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("cost_rate", "Cost / hr", 10, "money",
                formula='=IF({employee}{r}="","",'
                        'IF(IFERROR(INDEX('
                        '[[Crew Database.cost_rate]],'
                        'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                        '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0)>0,'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX([[Crew Database.cost_rate]],'
                        'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                        '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0),LabourRate))',
                hidden=True),
            Col("day_cost", "Cost of day", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({capacity}{r}*'
                             'IF({cost_rate}{r}="",LabourRate,'
                             '{cost_rate}{r}),2)'),
                note="What this person's day costs you whether or not "
                     "the work was sold. That is why empty days hurt."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 28, "wrap"),

            Col("is_future", "Future", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({date}{r}>=TodayDate,1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_over", "Over", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({working}{r}="Yes",'
                             '{spare}{r}<0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_idle", "Idle", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({working}{r}="Yes",'
                             '{is_future}{r}=1,{jobs_count}{r}=0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            weekday_col("date", "dow", header="DOW"),
            week_start_col("date"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 42, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('AND({working}{r}="Yes",{spare}{r}<-1)',
                     "Clash - more work booked than hours in the day"),
                    ('AND({working}{r}<>"Yes",{jobs_count}{r}>0)',
                     "Urgent - jobs booked for a crew that is off"),
                    ('{is_over}{r}=1',
                     "Over hours - the day is slightly over, check it"),
                    ('{is_idle}{r}=1',
                     "Schedule - available and nothing booked"),
                    ('AND({working}{r}="Yes",{use_pct}{r}<>"",'
                     '{use_pct}{r}<0.5,{is_future}{r}=1)',
                     "Action - less than half the day is sold"),
                    ('AND({working}{r}<>"Yes",{reason}{r}="")',
                     "Add - marked unavailable with no reason"),
                    ('AND({is_future}{r}=0,{logged}{r}=0,'
                     '{working}{r}="Yes")',
                     "Missing - a worked day with no timesheet"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/timesheets.py`

*243 lines · 1 function*

> 13. Timesheets & Payroll - the hours, and what they really cost.
>
> Every line is one person, one day, and usually one job. Hours booked
> against a Job ID flow straight into that job's actual cost, which is
> what makes the estimated-versus-actual comparison possible at all.
>
> Hours that are not against a job - driving, yard time, mending a
> mower, quoting - still cost money and are still recorded here. They
> are what the billable percentage measures, and that percentage
> explains more bad months than any other number in the workbook.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Timesheets & Payroll').

````python
"""13. Timesheets & Payroll - the hours, and what they really cost.

Every line is one person, one day, and usually one job. Hours booked
against a Job ID flow straight into that job's actual cost, which is
what makes the estimated-versus-actual comparison possible at all.

Hours that are not against a job - driving, yard time, mending a
mower, quoting - still cost money and are still recorded here. They
are what the billable percentage measures, and that percentage
explains more bad months than any other number in the workbook.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, time_check
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, date_num_col, id_col,
                     lookup_crew, money_cf,
                     month_col, pick, scale_cf, week_start_col,
                     weekday_col, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Timesheets & Payroll"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Timesheets & Payroll",
        subtitle="Hours by person, by day and by job - costed at each "
                 "person's own rate, and fed back into job profit.",
        tab_color=Palette.primary_mid,
        rows=cfg.row_count("timesheets"),
        samples=sample("timesheets", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "One line per person per job per day. Put the Job ID on "
            "it and the hours cost that job automatically. Leave the "
            "Job ID blank for travel, yard work or anything else that "
            "is not chargeable - it still costs you, and it still "
            "belongs here."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Hours can be entered either as a start and finish time "
            "or as a plain number of hours. If both are present the "
            "times win, because they are the harder thing to "
            "misremember.",
            "Overtime is worked out per person per week against the "
            "threshold on Settings, and costed at the multiplier. It "
            "is an indicator for your own planning, not a payroll "
            "calculation - overtime law varies and your payroll "
            "provider is the authority on it.",
            "This tab does not file taxes, produce payslips or "
            "report to any payroll service. It records what was "
            "worked so that your job costs are honest.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", '"TS-"', digits=5, header="Line"),
            Col("date", "Date", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here."),
            Col("employee", "Employee", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("CrewIDList",
                              "Pick the Employee ID from the Crew "
                              "Database."),
                note="Pick from the list. Their own cost rate is used."),
            Col("name", "Name", 20, "text",
                formula=lookup_crew("name")),
            Col("job", "Job ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("JobIDList",
                              "Pick the job these hours were worked "
                              "on."),
                note="The job these hours belong to. Leave blank for "
                     "non-chargeable time - that is not a mistake, it "
                     "is the point of the billable column."),
            Col("job_address", "Where", 26, "text",
                formula='=IF({job}{r}="","",'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX('
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.address]],'
                        'MATCH({job}{r},'
                        '[[Job & Work Orders.id]],0))&"",'
                        '"Check - Job ID not found"))',
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Check -", "fmt": "bad"}]),
            Col("task", "What they did", 22, "text",
                validate=pick("TaskTypeList",
                              "Mowing, driving, maintenance, quoting, "
                              "yard work."),
                note="Non-job time is worth classifying. It is where "
                     "the day goes."),
            Col("start_time", "Start", 9, "time",
                validate=time_check()),
            Col("end_time", "End", 9, "time",
                validate=time_check()),
            Col("break_mins", "Break", 8, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 480, decimal=False)),
            Col("hours_typed", "Hours", 9, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 24,
                                "Hours worked, if you are not using "
                                "start and finish times."),
                note="Use this or the times, whichever suits. Not "
                     "both."),
            Col("hours", "Hours used", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({start_time}{r}<>"",'
                             '{end_time}{r}<>""),'
                             'ROUND(MAX(0,({end_time}{r}-'
                             '{start_time}{r})*24-'
                             '{break_mins}{r}/60),2),'
                             '{hours_typed}{r})'),
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="From the times if they are there, otherwise the "
                     "typed figure."),
            Col("week_hours", "Week to date", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'SUMIFS({hours_first}:{hours_last},'
                             '{employee_first}:{employee_last},'
                             '{employee}{r},'
                             '{week_start_first}:{week_start_last},'
                             '{week_start}{r})'),
                hidden=True,
                note="This person's hours in the same week, used to "
                     "work out where overtime starts."),
            Col("week_before", "Week before line", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'SUMIFS({hours_first}:{hours}{r},'
                             '{employee_first}:{employee}{r},'
                             '{employee}{r},'
                             '{week_start_first}:{week_start}{r},'
                             '{week_start}{r})-{hours}{r}'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("overtime_hours", "Overtime", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(MAX(0,{week_before}{r}+'
                             '{hours}{r}-OvertimeAfter)-'
                             'MAX(0,{week_before}{r}-OvertimeAfter),2)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="The part of this line that falls past the weekly "
                     "threshold on Settings."),
            Col("normal_hours", "Normal hours", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({hours}{r}-'
                             '{overtime_hours}{r},2)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("rate", "Cost / hr", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Crew Database.cost_rate]],'
                             'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                             '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0)>0,'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Crew Database.cost_rate]],'
                             'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                             '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0),'
                             'LabourRate)'),
                note="Their loaded rate from the Crew Database, or the "
                     "default on Settings if it is blank."),
            Col("ot_multiplier", "Overtime x", 10, "number1",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Crew Database.overtime_rate]],'
                             'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                             '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0)>0,'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Crew Database.overtime_rate]],'
                             'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                             '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0),'
                             'OvertimeRate)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("cost", "Cost", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({normal_hours}{r}*{rate}{r}+'
                             '{overtime_hours}{r}*{rate}{r}*'
                             '{ot_multiplier}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What this line costs the business. Lines with a "
                     "Job ID land on that job's actual cost."),
            Col("pay", "Gross pay", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(({normal_hours}{r}+'
                             '{overtime_hours}{r}*'
                             '{ot_multiplier}{r})*'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Crew Database.pay_rate]],'
                             'MATCH({employee}{r},'
                             '[[Crew Database.id]],0))*1,0),2)'),
                note="At their wage rate, for your own reference. Not "
                     "a payslip, and no tax is calculated."),
            Col("mileage", "Miles driven", 11, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 2000),
                note="Personal vehicle miles to reimburse, if that is "
                     "how you work."),
            Col("approved", "Approved", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Have you checked and approved this "
                               "line?"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "No", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 26, "wrap"),

            # ---- the bits other tabs read ----------------------------
            Col("is_billable", "Billable", 8, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({job}{r}<>"",{hours}{r}>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("job_value", "Job value share", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({is_billable}{r}=0,0,'
                             'ROUND(IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.charge]],'
                             'MATCH({job}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.id]],0))*1,0)*'
                             '{hours}{r}/MAX({hours}{r},SUMIFS('
                             '{hours_first}:{hours_last},'
                             '{job_first}:{job_last},{job}{r})),2))'),
                hidden=True,
                note="This line's share of what the job was worth, "
                     "split by hours. Used for the crew scorecard."),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            weekday_col("date", "dow", header="DOW"),
            week_start_col("date"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 42, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('{job_address}{r}="Check - Job ID not found"',
                     "Check - the Job ID on this line does not exist"),
                    ('{hours}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no hours on this line"),
                    ('{hours}{r}>14',
                     "Check - more than fourteen hours in one day"),
                    ('{rate}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no cost rate for this person"),
                    ('{overtime_hours}{r}>0',
                     "Over hours - part of this line is overtime"),
                    ('AND({job}{r}="",{task}{r}="")',
                     "Add - non-job time with no explanation"),
                    ('{approved}{r}<>"Yes"',
                     "Check - not approved yet"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

---

## 9. Machines, trucks and materials

Workflow five. Equipment warns twice - on engine hours since the last service and on months since it, whichever lands first - and the dashboard picks that up. The vehicle tracker works out miles per gallon from the odometer readings rather than asking you for it. Materials stock is derived from the purchases and the usage logged against jobs, so it is never a number anybody types over, and it reorders itself when it drops to the trigger level.

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/equipment.py`

*256 lines · 1 function*

> 14. Equipment Inventory - the machines, and when they are due.
>
> Mowers measure their lives in engine hours, not calendar months, so
> this tab tracks both: hours since the last service against the
> interval you set, and days since the last service against the months
> you set. Whichever comes first wins, which is how the manual says it
> and how the dashboard alert works.
>
> A machine that fails in July costs a week of the season. The warning
> columns here exist so that it fails in the workshop in February
> instead.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Equipment Inventory').

````python
"""14. Equipment Inventory - the machines, and when they are due.

Mowers measure their lives in engine hours, not calendar months, so
this tab tracks both: hours since the last service against the
interval you set, and days since the last service against the months
you set. Whichever comes first wins, which is how the manual says it
and how the dashboard alert works.

A machine that fails in July costs a week of the season. The warning
columns here exist so that it fails in the workshop in February
instead.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, id_col,
                     is_usable_equipment, rank_desc, scale_cf,
                     status_cf, sum_by, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Equipment Inventory"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Equipment Inventory",
        subtitle="Every machine you own: what it cost, what it is "
                 "worth, when it was last serviced and what it owes "
                 "you.",
        tab_color=Palette.accent,
        rows=cfg.row_count("equipment"),
        samples=sample("equipment", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "List every machine worth more than a few days' hire. "
            "Record the engine hours when you service it and the "
            "workbook works out when the next one is due, by hours "
            "and by date, and warns you before either arrives."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Service intervals are set per machine because a "
            "commercial mower and a hedge trimmer do not live the "
            "same life. The defaults on Settings are used when a row "
            "leaves them blank.",
            "Cost per hour is the number that tells you whether to "
            "repair or replace. Once a machine's running cost per "
            "hour passes what hiring one would cost, the decision "
            "has already been made for you.",
            "Depreciation here is straight line and is for your own "
            "planning only. Your accountant will use a different "
            "method for tax, and theirs is the one that counts.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("name", "EquipmentPrefix",
                   digits=cfg.digits("equipment"), header="Equipment ID",
                   note="Generated. Useful on a sticker on the "
                        "machine itself."),
            Col("name", "Equipment", 26, "text",
                note="Start here. 'Exmark Lazer Z 60\"' beats 'big "
                     "mower' when there are three of them."),
            Col("category", "Category", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("EquipmentCategoryList")),
            Col("make", "Make", 15, "text"),
            Col("model", "Model", 16, "text"),
            Col("serial", "Serial number", 18, "text",
                note="The one the insurer and the police ask for. "
                     "Photograph the plate as well."),
            Col("purchase_date", "Bought", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("purchase_price", "Cost", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000)),
            Col("life_years", "Life (years)", 11, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 50, decimal=False),
                note="How long you expect to keep it working. Drives "
                     "the depreciation estimate."),
            Col("warranty_end", "Warranty to", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Worth checking before paying for a repair."),
            Col("status", "Status", 15, "text",
                validate=list_from("EquipmentStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("condition", "Condition", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("EquipmentConditionList"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Poor", "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Needs replacing", "fmt": "bold_bad"}]),
            Col("assigned", "Assigned to", 13, "text",
                validate=list_from("CrewNameList"),
                note="Which crew has it on the trailer."),
            Col("location", "Kept at", 16, "text",
                note="Yard, trailer two, Danny's garage."),

            # ---- servicing -------------------------------------------
            Col("hours_now", "Engine hours", 12, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 100000,
                                "The reading on the hour meter today."),
                note="Update it when you service the machine. It is "
                     "the only honest measure of a mower's life."),
            Col("last_service", "Last service", 12, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("last_service_hours", "Hours at service", 14, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 100000),
                note="The hour meter reading at the last service."),
            Col("service_hours", "Service every (hrs)", 15, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 10000, decimal=False),
                note="Blank uses the default interval on Settings."),
            Col("service_months", "Service every (months)", 16, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 120, decimal=False),
                note="Some machines are due by the calendar even if "
                     "they have barely run."),
            Col("hours_used", "Hours since", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'MAX(0,{hours_now}{r}-'
                             '{last_service_hours}{r})'),
                note="Engine hours since the last service."),
            Col("hours_interval", "Interval used", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({service_hours}{r}>0,'
                             '{service_hours}{r},ServiceHours)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("hours_left", "Hours to service", 14, "number1",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'ROUND({hours_interval}{r}-'
                        '{hours_used}{r},1))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=", "value": 10,
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Negative means the service is overdue by that "
                     "many engine hours."),
            Col("days_since_service", "Days since", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({last_service}{r}="",0,'
                             'TodayDate-{last_service}{r})'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("months_interval", "Months used", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({service_months}{r}>0,'
                             '{service_months}{r},ServiceMonths)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("next_service_date", "Service due", 12, "date",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({last_service}{r}="","",'
                        'EDATE({last_service}{r},'
                        '{months_interval}{r})))',
                cf=[{"type": "formula",
                     "criteria": '=AND(ISNUMBER({next_service_date}{r}),'
                                 '{next_service_date}{r}<TodayDate)',
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"}],
                note="By the calendar. The hours column may get there "
                     "first - whichever does, wins."),
            Col("days_to_service", "Days to service", 13, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({last_service}{r}="",0,'
                             'EDATE({last_service}{r},'
                             '{months_interval}{r})-TodayDate)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=",
                     "value": "=ServiceWarnDays", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("service_due", "Due now", 8, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF(({hours_left}{r}<=0)+'
                             '(AND({last_service}{r}<>"",'
                             '{days_to_service}{r}<=0))>0,1,0)'),
                hidden=True,
                note="Either measure being past due makes this 1. The "
                     "dashboard counts it."),
            Col("service_soon", "Due soon", 8, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF(AND({service_due}{r}=0,'
                             '({hours_left}{r}<=ServiceWarnHours)+'
                             '(AND({last_service}{r}<>"",'
                             '{days_to_service}{r}<='
                             'ServiceWarnDays))>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),

            # ---- what it costs ---------------------------------------
            Col("maintenance_cost", "Maintenance spend", 15, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Expense Tracker", "amount",
                                            "equipment", '{id}{r}')),
                note="Everything booked to this machine on the expense "
                     "tracker."),
            Col("maintenance_count", "Repairs", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Expense Tracker",
                                              "equipment", '{id}{r}'))),
            Col("age_years", "Age (years)", 10, "number1",
                formula='=IF({purchase_date}{r}="","",'
                        'ROUND((TodayDate-'
                        '{purchase_date}{r})/365.25,1))'),
            Col("depreciated", "Written down to", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({life_years}{r}<=0,0,'
                             'ROUND(MAX(0,{purchase_price}{r}*'
                             '(1-MIN(1,(TodayDate-'
                             '{purchase_date}{r})/365.25/'
                             '{life_years}{r}))),2))'),
                note="Straight line, for planning. Your accountant "
                     "will do it differently for tax."),
            Col("total_cost", "Owned cost", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({purchase_price}{r}+'
                             '{maintenance_cost}{r},2)')),
            Col("cost_per_hour", "Cost per hour", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({hours_now}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({total_cost}{r}/'
                        '{hours_now}{r},2)))',
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Purchase plus repairs, divided by the hours it "
                     "has run. Compare it with the hire rate."),
            Col("replace_flag", "Replace?", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Are you planning to replace this in "
                               "the next twelve months?"),
                note="Planning it beats discovering it in June."),
            Col("insured", "Insured", 9, "flag",
                validate=yesno()),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 28, "wrap"),

            Col("is_usable", "Usable", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             f'IF({is_usable_equipment("{status}{r}")},'
                             '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            rank_desc("maintenance_cost", guard='{name}{r}=""'),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("name", [
                    ('{status}{r}="Out of service"',
                     "Urgent - out of service, jobs needing it will "
                     "slip"),
                    ('AND({is_usable}{r}=1,{service_due}{r}=1)',
                     "Service - overdue, book it in now"),
                    ('{condition}{r}="Needs replacing"',
                     "Action - condition says it needs replacing"),
                    ('AND({is_usable}{r}=1,{service_soon}{r}=1)',
                     "Due - service coming up shortly"),
                    ('AND({purchase_price}{r}>0,'
                     '{maintenance_cost}{r}>{purchase_price}{r}*0.5)',
                     "Check - repairs have passed half what it cost"),
                    ('AND({hours_now}{r}>0,{last_service}{r}="")',
                     "Add - no service history recorded"),
                    ('AND({is_usable}{r}=1,{hours_now}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no engine hours, so servicing cannot be "
                     "tracked"),
                    ('{insured}{r}="No"',
                     "Check - not marked as insured"),
                    ('{replace_flag}{r}="Yes"',
                     "Plan - flagged for replacement this year"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/vehicles.py`

*197 lines · 1 function*

> 15. Vehicle & Fuel Tracker - every fill-up and every repair.
>
> One row per fill-up or per vehicle cost. Record the odometer each
> time and the workbook works out the miles between fills and the
> economy you are actually getting, which is the earliest warning you
> will get that something is wrong with a truck.
>
> Fuel entered here also lands on the expense reports, so it is
> counted once and only once.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Vehicle & Fuel Tracker').

````python
"""15. Vehicle & Fuel Tracker - every fill-up and every repair.

One row per fill-up or per vehicle cost. Record the odometer each
time and the workbook works out the miles between fills and the
economy you are actually getting, which is the earliest warning you
will get that something is wrong with a truck.

Fuel entered here also lands on the expense reports, so it is
counted once and only once.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, date_num_col, id_col,
                     money_cf, month_col, pick, scale_cf, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Vehicle & Fuel Tracker"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Vehicle & Fuel Tracker",
        subtitle="Fill-ups, repairs, tax and insurance - by vehicle, "
                 "with the economy you are really getting.",
        tab_color=Palette.accent,
        rows=cfg.row_count("vehicles"),
        samples=sample("vehicles", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "One line per fill-up or per vehicle bill. Put the "
            "odometer reading on every fuel line and the miles per "
            "gallon work themselves out from one fill to the next. "
            "Set up your vehicles in the list on the Settings tab "
            "first."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Economy is calculated between consecutive fill-ups for "
            "the same vehicle, so the first fill for each one shows "
            "nothing. That is correct, not a fault.",
            "A truck whose economy drops by a fifth is telling you "
            "something - tyres, a dragging brake, a trailer loaded "
            "wrong, or a driver with a heavy foot.",
            "Costs recorded here flow into the expense reports "
            "automatically. Do not enter the same fuel receipt on "
            "the Expense Tracker as well or it will be counted "
            "twice.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", '"VEH-"', digits=5, header="Line"),
            Col("date", "Date", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here."),
            Col("vehicle", "Vehicle", 16, "text",
                validate=pick("VehicleList",
                              "Pick the vehicle. The list is on the "
                              "Settings tab."),
                note="Pick from the list so one truck's history stays "
                     "in one place."),
            Col("type", "Cost type", 16, "text",
                validate=pick("VehicleCostList",
                              "Fuel, service, repair, tyres, "
                              "insurance, tax, cleaning."),
                note="Fuel lines get the economy treatment; the rest "
                     "are just costs."),
            Col("odometer", "Odometer", 11, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 2000000, decimal=False),
                note="The reading at the pump. Put it on every fuel "
                     "line and the economy columns do the rest."),
            Col("gallons", "Gallons / litres", 13, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 1000,
                                "How much fuel went in. Use whichever "
                                "unit you buy in, consistently."),
                note="Whatever unit you buy in - just be consistent, "
                     "because the economy figure follows it."),
            Col("amount", "Cost", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What you paid."),
            Col("unit_price", "Price per unit", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({gallons}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({amount}{r}/'
                        '{gallons}{r},3)))',
                note="Worth watching over a season. Fuel is usually "
                     "the third biggest cost in this trade."),
            Col("full_tank", "Filled up", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Was the tank filled right up? Economy "
                               "is only meaningful between full "
                               "tanks."),
                note="Part fills make the economy figure meaningless, "
                     "so they are skipped."),
            Col("driver", "Driver", 16, "text",
                note="Who was driving. Useful when one truck's economy "
                     "is oddly worse than the other's."),
            Col("supplier", "Where", 18, "text"),
            Col("reference", "Receipt / invoice", 16, "text"),
            Col("job", "Job ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("JobIDList",
                              "Pick the job, if this cost belongs to "
                              "one."),
                note="Only if this cost belongs to one job - a special "
                     "trip to the tip, for instance. Usually blank."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 26, "wrap"),

            # ---- the economy -----------------------------------------
            Col("is_fuel", "Fuel", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({type}{r}="Fuel",'
                             '{gallons}{r}>0,{odometer}{r}>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("prev_odo", "Previous odo", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({is_fuel}{r}=0,0,'
                             'SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                             '({vehicle_first}:{vehicle_last}='
                             '{vehicle}{r})*'
                             '({is_fuel_first}:{is_fuel_last}=1)*'
                             '({odometer_first}:{odometer_last}<'
                             '{odometer}{r})*'
                             '{odometer_first}:{odometer_last})))'),
                hidden=True,
                note="The highest lower odometer reading for the same "
                     "vehicle - where this tank started. Works out by "
                     "reading, not by row, so entering a receipt late "
                     "does not break it."),
            Col("miles", "Miles on tank", 12, "int",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({prev_odo}{r}<=0,"",'
                        '{odometer}{r}-{prev_odo}{r}))',
                note="Blank on the first fill for each vehicle, which "
                     "is as it should be."),
            Col("economy", "Miles per unit", 13, "number1",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({is_fuel}{r}=0,"",'
                        'IF({full_tank}{r}<>"Yes","",'
                        'IF({prev_odo}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND(({odometer}{r}-'
                        '{prev_odo}{r})/{gallons}{r},2)))))',
                cf=scale_cf(),
                note="Only on full tanks with a previous reading to "
                     "measure from."),
            Col("cost_per_mile", "Cost per mile", 12, "money",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({miles}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({miles}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({amount}{r}/'
                        '{miles}{r},3))))'),
            Col("vehicle_avg", "Vehicle average", 13, "number1",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({is_fuel}{r}=0,"",'
                        'IFERROR(AVERAGEIFS('
                        '{economy_first}:{economy_last},'
                        '{vehicle_first}:{vehicle_last},'
                        '{vehicle}{r},'
                        '{economy_first}:{economy_last},">0"),"")))',
                hidden=True),
            Col("economy_gap", "vs average", 10, "percent",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({economy}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({vehicle_avg}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({vehicle_avg}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({economy}{r}/'
                        '{vehicle_avg}{r}-1,4)))))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": -0.15,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0.1,
                     "fmt": "good"}],
                note="How this tank compares with that vehicle's "
                     "normal. A run of red is worth investigating."),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 42, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('AND({type}{r}="Fuel",{odometer}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no odometer reading, so economy cannot be "
                     "worked out"),
                    ('{amount}{r}=0', "Add - no cost on this line"),
                    ('AND({economy_gap}{r}<>"",'
                     '{economy_gap}{r}<-0.15)',
                     "Check - economy well below this vehicle's "
                     "average"),
                    ('AND({type}{r}="Fuel",{gallons}{r}=0)',
                     "Add - no quantity, so price per unit is blank"),
                    ('AND({prev_odo}{r}>0,{odometer}{r}<'
                     '{prev_odo}{r})',
                     "Check - odometer lower than a previous reading"),
                    ('{reference}{r}=""',
                     "Add - no receipt reference recorded"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/materials.py`

*238 lines · 1 function*

> 16. Materials Inventory - mulch, seed, fertiliser and fuel cans.
>
> Stock on hand is derived, never typed: opening stock, plus what the
> expense tracker says you bought, minus what the jobs say you used.
> Typing a stock figure over the top of a calculation is how a stock
> sheet stops being believed, so this one will not let you.
>
> The low-stock alert uses your own reorder point, and the three
> columns next to it tell you how long what you have will last at the
> rate you are getting through it.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Materials Inventory').

````python
"""16. Materials Inventory - mulch, seed, fertiliser and fuel cans.

Stock on hand is derived, never typed: opening stock, plus what the
expense tracker says you bought, minus what the jobs say you used.
Typing a stock figure over the top of a calculation is how a stock
sheet stops being believed, so this one will not let you.

The low-stock alert uses your own reorder point, and the three
columns next to it tell you how long what you have will last at the
rate you are getting through it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, count_by, id_col,
                     money_cf, rank_desc, scale_cf, status_cf, sum_by,
                     yesno, zero)

NAME = "Materials Inventory"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Materials Inventory",
        subtitle="What is in the yard, what it cost, and what you are "
                 "about to run out of.",
        tab_color=Palette.accent,
        rows=cfg.row_count("materials"),
        samples=sample("materials", cfg),
        help_text=(
            "Add each material once with its unit, its cost and the "
            "level at which you want to be warned. Record purchases "
            "on the Expense Tracker against the Material ID and usage "
            "on the same tab - stock on hand follows automatically."),
        extra_notes=[
            "Stock on hand is opening stock plus purchases minus "
            "usage. If it is wrong, one of those three is wrong - "
            "fix the entry, not the number, or you lose the ability "
            "to trust any of it.",
            "Weeks of cover is based on what you have actually used "
            "in the last ninety days. In March it will look alarming "
            "and in November reassuring; read it with the season in "
            "mind.",
            "Charge-out price is what the customer pays per unit. It "
            "should be above cost - handling, collecting and "
            "carrying are real work and the markup on Settings is "
            "there to cover them.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("name", "MaterialPrefix",
                   digits=cfg.digits("material"), header="Material ID",
                   note="Generated. Book purchases and usage against "
                        "it on the Expense Tracker."),
            Col("name", "Material", 26, "text",
                note="Start here. 'Hardwood mulch, bulk' is more use "
                     "than 'mulch'."),
            Col("category", "Category", 20, "text",
                validate=list_from("MaterialCategoryList")),
            Col("unit", "Unit", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("MaterialUnitList"),
                note="Bag, yard, gallon, roll. Be consistent - half "
                     "the value of this tab is in the unit."),
            Col("supplier", "Supplier", 20, "text"),
            Col("supplier_code", "Their code", 14, "text",
                note="The code to quote when you ring up to order."),
            Col("cost_unit", "Cost per unit", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000),
                note="What you pay. Drives job costs and the "
                     "suggested charge-out price."),
            Col("charge_unit", "Charge per unit", 13, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "What the customer pays per unit. "
                                "Blank uses cost times the markup on "
                                "Settings."),
                note="Blank falls back to cost times the markup."),
            Col("charge_used", "Charged", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({charge_unit}{r}>0,{charge_unit}{r},'
                             'ROUND({cost_unit}{r}*MaterialMarkup,2))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("markup_pct", "Markup", 9, "percent",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({cost_unit}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND({charge_used}{r}/'
                        '{cost_unit}{r}-1,4)))',
                cf=scale_cf()),
            Col("opening", "Opening stock", 12, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "How much you had when you started "
                                "using this workbook."),
                note="Set once, when you start. After that the "
                     "purchases and usage do the work."),
            Col("reorder_at", "Reorder at", 11, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000),
                note="The level at which you want a warning. Set it "
                     "to a week's use plus whatever your supplier's "
                     "lead time is."),
            Col("reorder_qty", "Order quantity", 12, "number1",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000),
                note="How much you usually buy at once."),
            Col("location", "Kept at", 16, "text"),
            Col("status", "Status", 13, "text",
                validate=list_from("MaterialStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf()),
            Col("hazardous", "Hazardous", 10, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Does this need a safety data sheet or "
                               "a licence to apply?"),
                note="Fertiliser, herbicide and fuel all have rules. "
                     "Know which ones apply to you."),
            Col("expiry", "Use by", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Seed and chemicals do go off."),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 26, "wrap"),

            # ---- stock, derived --------------------------------------
            Col("purchased", "Bought", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Expense Tracker", "quantity",
                                            "material", '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_purchase", "1")),
                note="From the Expense Tracker, automatically."),
            Col("used", "Used", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Expense Tracker", "quantity",
                                            "material", '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_usage", "1")),
                note="Booked out to jobs on the Expense Tracker."),
            Col("on_hand", "On hand", 11, "number1",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({opening}{r}+{purchased}{r}-'
                             '{used}{r},2)'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "formula",
                     "criteria": '=AND({name}{r}<>"",'
                                 '{on_hand}{r}<={reorder_at}{r})',
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Opening plus bought minus used. Calculated - you "
                     "cannot type over it, and that is deliberate."),
            Col("stock_value", "Stock value", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND(MAX(0,{on_hand}{r})*'
                             '{cost_unit}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What is sitting in the yard, at cost."),
            Col("used_90", "Used (90 days)", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'SUMIFS([[Expense Tracker.quantity]],'
                             '[[Expense Tracker.material]],{id}{r},'
                             '[[Expense Tracker.is_usage]],1,'
                             '[[Expense Tracker.date_num]],'
                             '">="&TodayDate-90)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("weekly_use", "Used per week", 12, "number1",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'ROUND({used_90}{r}/13,2)'),
                note="Averaged over the last ninety days."),
            Col("weeks_cover", "Weeks of cover", 13, "number1",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({weekly_use}{r}<=0,"",'
                        'ROUND(MAX(0,{on_hand}{r})/'
                        '{weekly_use}{r},1)))',
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=", "value": 1,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": "<=", "value": 3,
                     "fmt": "warn"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 20,
                     "fmt": "info"}],
                note="At the current rate. Blank means you have not "
                     "used any lately, so there is nothing to project."),
            Col("spend_total", "Spent to date", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("name", sum_by("Expense Tracker", "amount",
                                            "material", '{id}{r}',
                                            "is_purchase", "1")),
                cf=money_cf()),
            Col("times_bought", "Orders", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("name", count_by("Expense Tracker",
                                              "material", '{id}{r}',
                                              "is_purchase", "1"))),
            Col("last_buy_num", "Last bought #", 11, "number",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'SUMPRODUCT(MAX('
                             '([[Expense Tracker.material]]={id}{r})*'
                             '([[Expense Tracker.is_purchase]]=1)*'
                             '[[Expense Tracker.date_num]]))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("last_bought", "Last bought", 11, "date",
                formula='=IF({name}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({last_buy_num}{r}<=0,"",'
                        '{last_buy_num}{r}))'),
            Col("is_low", "Low", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF(AND({status}{r}="Active",'
                             '{on_hand}{r}<={reorder_at}{r}),1,0)'),
                hidden=True,
                note="What the dashboard's low-material count reads."),
            Col("order_value", "Cost to restock", 13, "money",
                formula=zero("name",
                             'IF({is_low}{r}=0,0,'
                             'ROUND(MAX({reorder_qty}{r},'
                             '{reorder_at}{r}-{on_hand}{r})*'
                             '{cost_unit}{r},2))'),
                note="What it would cost to bring the low ones back "
                     "up. The shopping list, in money."),
            rank_desc("spend_total", guard='{name}{r}=""'),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 44, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("name", [
                    ('{on_hand}{r}<0',
                     "Urgent - stock has gone negative, a purchase is "
                     "missing"),
                    ('AND({status}{r}="Active",{on_hand}{r}<=0)',
                     "Out of stock - none left in the yard"),
                    ('AND({expiry}{r}<>"",{expiry}{r}<TodayDate,'
                     '{on_hand}{r}>0)',
                     "Expired - past its use-by date"),
                    ('{is_low}{r}=1',
                     "Reorder - at or below your reorder point"),
                    ('AND({weeks_cover}{r}<>"",{weeks_cover}{r}<=1)',
                     "Order - less than a week of cover left"),
                    ('{cost_unit}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no cost per unit, so job costs are "
                     "understated"),
                    ('AND({expiry}{r}<>"",'
                     '{expiry}{r}<TodayDate+60,{on_hand}{r}>0)',
                     "Due - use-by date within two months"),
                    ('{reorder_at}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no reorder point, so you will not be "
                     "warned"),
                    ('AND({hazardous}{r}="Yes",{notes}{r}="")',
                     "Check - hazardous with no handling note"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

---

## 10. Getting paid, and what was left

Workflow six. Invoices age from the due date rather than the invoice date, take part payments, and chase themselves. The expense tracker separates a cost that belongs to one visit from an overhead that belongs to the month. Customer feedback scores the crew and reopens the complaint until somebody closes it. Profit analysis, the monthly report and the annual report then put fourteen months of revenue, labour, materials, fuel and margin into one picture.

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/invoices.py`

*284 lines · 1 function*

> 17. Invoice & Payments - what you billed and what actually arrived.
>
> Outstanding is the whole point: total minus paid, aged against the
> due date that comes from the customer's own payment terms. The
> overdue column feeds the dashboard, the customer record and the
> chase list, so there is exactly one place where a debt lives.
>
> An invoice can cover one job or a month of them. Put the Job ID on it
> when it is one job - that is what closes the loop from quote to job
> to money in the bank.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `spec(cfg)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NAME` ('Invoice & Payments').

````python
"""17. Invoice & Payments - what you billed and what actually arrived.

Outstanding is the whole point: total minus paid, aged against the
due date that comes from the customer's own payment terms. The
overdue column feeds the dashboard, the customer record and the
chase list, so there is exactly one place where a debt lives.

An invoice can cover one job or a month of them. Put the Job ID on it
when it is one job - that is what closes the loop from quote to job
to money in the bank.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from ..config import Palette, Config
from ..layout import Col, SheetSpec
from ..sample_data import get as sample
from ..writer import date_check, list_from
from .common import (alert_cf, alert_chain, amount, customer_col,
                     date_num_col, id_col, is_owed_invoice,
                     is_paid_invoice, lookup_customer, money_cf,
                     month_col, owing_cf, pick, service_tax,
                     status_cf, term_days, yesno, zero)

NAME = "Invoice & Payments"


def spec(cfg: Config) -> SheetSpec:
    return SheetSpec(
        name=NAME,
        title="Invoice & Payments",
        subtitle="Every invoice, what has been paid against it, and "
                 "exactly how late the rest of it is.",
        tab_color=Palette.deep,
        rows=cfg.row_count("invoices"),
        samples=sample("invoices", cfg),
        freeze_cols=3,
        help_text=(
            "One row per invoice. The due date comes from the "
            "customer's payment terms, the outstanding amount from "
            "what you have recorded as paid, and the overdue flag "
            "from the two together. Record part payments in the paid "
            "column as they arrive."),
        extra_notes=[
            "This tab records invoices; it does not send them and it "
            "does not take card payments. Use the printable invoice "
            "page to produce the document, then record what happens "
            "here.",
            "Age is counted from the due date, not the invoice date, "
            "so an invoice on 30-day terms is not 'thirty days late' "
            "the moment it is sent.",
            "Writing one off is a decision, not a deletion. Set the "
            "status to 'Written off' and the amount stops being "
            "chased but stays in the record, where it belongs.",
        ],
        columns=[
            id_col("date", "InvoicePrefix", digits=cfg.digits("invoice"),
                   header="Invoice ID",
                   note="Generated, sequential and unique. Put it on "
                        "the job it bills."),
            Col("invoice_number", "Your number", 13, "text",
                note="If your accountant needs a particular numbering "
                     "format, put it here. The ID above is what the "
                     "workbook joins on."),
            Col("date", "Invoice date", 12, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="Start here."),
            customer_col(),
            Col("customer_name", "Customer", 22, "text",
                formula=lookup_customer("name")),
            Col("job", "Job ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("JobIDList",
                              "Pick the job this invoice bills."),
                note="For a single-job invoice. Leave blank for a "
                     "monthly or multi-job bill and list the work in "
                     "the description."),
            Col("contract", "Contract ID", 11, "id",
                validate=pick("ContractIDList",
                              "Pick the agreement being billed."),
                note="If this is the regular bill for a standing "
                     "agreement."),
            Col("description", "What for", 34, "wrap",
                note="What the customer sees. 'Grass cutting, April - "
                     "4 visits' saves a phone call."),
            Col("period", "Period covered", 14, "text",
                note="For monthly bills: 'April 2026'."),
            Col("po_number", "PO number", 13, "text",
                note="Commercial customers who need one will not pay "
                     "without it."),

            # ---- the money -------------------------------------------
            Col("subtotal", "Subtotal", 12, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "The amount before tax. Leave blank "
                                "to use the job's charge."),
                note="Blank pulls the charge from the Job ID, if "
                     "there is one."),
            Col("job_charge", "From job", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({job}{r}="",0,'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.charge]],'
                             'MATCH({job}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.id]],0))*1,0)+'
                             'IFERROR(INDEX('
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.extras]],'
                             'MATCH({job}{r},'
                             '[[Job & Work Orders.id]],0))*1,0))'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("net", "Net", 11, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND(IF({subtotal}{r}>0,{subtotal}{r},'
                             '{job_charge}{r}),2)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("discount", "Discount", 10, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000)),
            Col("tax", "Tax", 10, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             service_tax('{net}{r}-{discount}{r}')),
                note="Only if tax is switched on for your business on "
                     "the Settings tab."),
            Col("total", "Total", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({net}{r}-{discount}{r}+'
                             '{tax}{r},2)'),
                cf=money_cf(),
                note="What you are asking for."),
            Col("paid", "Paid", 11, "money",
                validate=amount(0, 1000000,
                                "How much has arrived. Part payments "
                                "are fine - put the running total."),
                note="Update as money comes in. The rest follows."),
            Col("paid_date", "Paid on", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check(),
                note="The day the last payment landed."),
            Col("method", "Paid by", 15, "text",
                validate=list_from("PaymentMethodList")),
            Col("outstanding", "Outstanding", 12, "money",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'ROUND({total}{r}-{paid}{r},2)'),
                cf=owing_cf(),
                note="Total minus paid. This is the number the "
                     "dashboard and the customer record both read."),

            # ---- when it is due --------------------------------------
            Col("terms", "Terms", 16, "text",
                validate=pick("PaymentTermsList",
                              "Blank uses the terms on the customer's "
                              "record."),
                note="Blank falls back to the customer's own terms."),
            Col("terms_used", "Terms used", 16, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({terms}{r}<>"",{terms}{r},'
                        'IFERROR(INDEX([[Customer CRM.terms]],'
                        'MATCH({customer}{r},'
                        '[[Customer CRM.id]],0))&"","Due on receipt")))',
                hidden=True),
            Col("term_days", "Days allowed", 11, "int",
                formula=zero("date", term_days('{terms_used}{r}')),
                hidden=True),
            Col("due_date", "Due", 11, "date",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        '{date}{r}+MAX(0,{term_days}{r}))',
                cf=[{"type": "formula",
                     "criteria": '=AND(ISNUMBER({due_date}{r}),'
                                 '{due_date}{r}<TodayDate,'
                                 '{outstanding}{r}>0)',
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"}],
                note="Invoice date plus the days your terms allow."),
            Col("days_late", "Days late", 10, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({outstanding}{r}<=0.004,0,'
                             'MAX(0,TodayDate-'
                             '({date}{r}+MAX(0,{term_days}{r}))))'),
                cf=[{"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 60,
                     "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">=", "value": 30,
                     "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "cell", "criteria": ">", "value": 0,
                     "fmt": "warn"}],
                note="Counted from the due date. Zero once it is paid."),
            Col("age_band", "Age", 13, "text",
                formula='=IF({date}{r}="","",'
                        'IF({outstanding}{r}<=0.004,"Paid",'
                        'IF({days_late}{r}<=0,"Not due yet",'
                        'IF({days_late}{r}<=30,"1-30 days",'
                        'IF({days_late}{r}<=60,"31-60 days",'
                        'IF({days_late}{r}<=90,"61-90 days",'
                        '"Over 90 days"))))))',
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Over 90", "fmt": "bold_bad"},
                    {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "61-90", "fmt": "bad"},
                    {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "31-60", "fmt": "warn"},
                    {"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "Paid", "fmt": "good"}],
                note="The ageing bucket the monthly report groups on."),
            Col("status", "Status", 14, "text",
                validate=list_from("InvoiceStatusList"),
                cf=status_cf(),
                note="Set it as things happen. The jobs tab reads it "
                     "back, so there is only ever one version of the "
                     "truth."),
            Col("sent", "Sent", 8, "flag",
                validate=yesno("Has it actually gone to the customer?"),
                cf=[{"type": "text", "criteria": "containing",
                     "value": "No", "fmt": "warn"}]),
            Col("reminders", "Reminders sent", 12, "int",
                validate=amount(0, 50, decimal=False),
                note="How many times you have chased. Three and still "
                     "nothing is a different conversation."),
            Col("last_chase", "Last chased", 11, "date",
                validate=date_check()),
            Col("notes", "Notes", 26, "wrap"),

            # ---- flags other tabs read -------------------------------
            Col("is_owed", "Owed", 6, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             f'IF(AND({is_owed_invoice("{status}{r}")},'
                             '{outstanding}{r}>0.004),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_paid", "Paid flag", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             f'IF({is_paid_invoice("{status}{r}")},'
                             '1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("is_overdue", "Overdue", 7, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF(AND({is_owed}{r}=1,'
                             '{days_late}{r}>0),1,0)'),
                hidden=True),
            Col("late_rank", "Late rank", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({is_overdue}{r}=0,0,'
                             'COUNTIFS({days_late_first}:'
                             '{days_late_last},">"&{days_late}{r},'
                             '{is_overdue_first}:{is_overdue_last},1)+'
                             'COUNTIFS({days_late_first}:'
                             '{days_late}{r},{days_late}{r},'
                             '{is_overdue_first}:{is_overdue}{r},1))'),
                hidden=True,
                note="1 = the latest invoice you have. The dashboard "
                     "chase list reads this."),
            Col("print_rank", "Print line", 9, "int",
                formula=zero("date",
                             'IF({id}{r}=PrintInvoice,1,0)'),
                hidden=True,
                note="Marks the one invoice the printable page is "
                     "showing."),
            date_num_col("date", "date_num"),
            date_num_col("paid_date", "paid_num", header="Paid #"),
            month_col("date"),

            Col("alert", "What needs doing", 46, "wrap",
                formula=alert_chain("date", [
                    ('{days_late}{r}>=60',
                     "Urgent - more than sixty days late, decide what "
                     "to do about it"),
                    ('AND({is_overdue}{r}=1,{reminders}{r}=0)',
                     "Chase - overdue and never chased"),
                    ('{is_overdue}{r}=1',
                     "Overdue - past its due date and unpaid"),
                    ('AND({total}{r}>0,{sent}{r}<>"Yes")',
                     "Send - raised but never sent to the customer"),
                    ('{status}{r}="Disputed"',
                     "Urgent - disputed, sort it out before it ages"),
                    ('{total}{r}=0',
                     "Add - no amount on this invoice"),
                    ('AND({outstanding}{r}<-0.004)',
                     "Check - paid more than the invoice, refund or "
                     "credit due"),
                    ('AND({is_owed}{r}=1,{days_late}{r}=0)',
                     "Due - sent and waiting, not yet late"),
                    ('AND({paid}{r}>0,{paid_date}{r}="")',
                     "Add - payment recorded with no date"),
                    ('AND({outstanding}{r}<=0.004,'
                     '{status}{r}<>"Paid",{total}{r}>0)',
                     "Check - fully paid but the status says "
                     "otherwise"),
                ]),
                cf=alert_cf()),
        ],
    )
````

### `landscaping_spreadsheet/sheets/expenses.py`

*208 lines · 1 function*

> 18. Expense Tracker - everything that goes out, and what it belongs to.
>
> Two kinds of line live here and the difference matters. A line with a
> Job ID is a cost of doing that job, and it lands on that job's actual
> cost and its profit. A line without one is a cost of being in
> business - insurance, the accountant, the yard rent - and it belongs
> to the month, not to any visit.
>
