````python
#: Every link below is a formula in row 5 of a register.  Together
#: they are the product: break one and a buyer gets a blank cell
#: instead of an answer, with nothing to tell them why.
#:
#: The SKU is the join key.  Every separately stocked variant has its
#: own SKU, sixteen registers reach back to the product database
#: through it, and stock itself is derived from four transaction
#: registers rather than typed anywhere.  Most of what follows is
#: those two decisions being checked in both directions.
#:
#: Every entry was read off the real formula, not guessed.  A guessed
#: chain check is worse than none: it fails on a correct product and
#: teaches you to ignore the tool.
CHAIN_CHECKS = [
    # ---- the customer, the hub every other register joins to -----------

    ("Customer CRM", "jobs_done", ("sheet", "Job & Work Orders")),

def _name_target(wb, name):
    """The one cell a defined name points at, or None."""
    defn = wb.defined_names.get(name)
    if defn is None:
        for ws in wb.worksheets:
            if name in ws.defined_names:
                defn = ws.defined_names[name]
                break
    if defn is None:
        return None
    try:
        sheet, ref = next(defn.destinations)
    except (StopIteration, Exception):          # noqa: B902
        return None
    if sheet not in wb.sheetnames or ":" in ref:
        return None
    return wb[sheet][ref.replace("$", "")]


def check_pages(wb, problems: list, notes: list) -> None:
    """The summary pages, checked through their defined names."""
    live = 0
    for name, what in PAGE_FORMULAS:
        cell = _name_target(wb, name)
        if cell is None:
            problems.append(f"{name} is missing - {what}")
            continue
        value = cell.value
        if not (isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("=")):
            problems.append(f"{name} no longer holds a formula - {what}")
            continue
        if cell.protection and not cell.protection.locked:
            problems.append(f"{name} is a formula but is unlocked - {what}")
            continue
        live += 1
    typed = 0
    for name, what in PAGE_INPUTS:
        cell = _name_target(wb, name)
        if cell is None:
            problems.append(f"{name} is missing - {what}")
            continue
        if cell.protection and cell.protection.locked:
            problems.append(f"{name} is locked, so nobody can change it "
                            f"- {what}")
            continue
        typed += 1
    notes.append(f"summary pages: {live} live formulas, "
                 f"{typed} typeable inputs verified")


def _specs():
    """Every register specification, keyed by sheet name."""
    from landscaping_spreadsheet.build import REGISTER_MODULES
    from landscaping_spreadsheet.config import Config
    cfg = Config()
    return {module.NAME: module.spec(cfg) for module in REGISTER_MODULES}


def check_chain(wb, problems: list, notes: list) -> None:
    """The five workflows only hold while every one of these does.

    Every one of them is a formula in row 5 of a register, so the check
    is cheap: find the column by its key in the specification, read the
    formula, and make sure it still mentions the thing it depends on.
    """
    specs = _specs()
    checked = 0
    for sheet_name, key, expect in CHAIN_CHECKS:
        spec = specs.get(sheet_name)
        if spec is None or sheet_name not in wb.sheetnames:
            problems.append(f"missing register {sheet_name}")
            continue
        try:
            column = spec.index(key) + 1
        except Exception:
            problems.append(f"{sheet_name}: column '{key}' has gone")
            continue
        formula = wb[sheet_name].cell(row=5, column=column).value
        if not isinstance(formula, str) or not formula.startswith("="):
            problems.append(f"{sheet_name}.{key} is no longer a formula")
            continue
        if expect[0] == "sheet":
            found = expect[1] in formula
            label = f"the {expect[1]} tab"
        elif expect[0] == "text":
            found = expect[1] in formula
            label = expect[1]
        else:
            letter = specs[expect[1]].letter(expect[2])
            label = f"{expect[1]}.{expect[2]} (column {letter})"
            if expect[1] == sheet_name:
                # a link inside the same register is a plain A1 reference
                found = bool(re.search(r"(?<![A-Z$])\$?" + letter +
                                       r"\$?5(?![0-9])", formula))
            else:
                found = f"${letter}$" in formula
        if not found:
            problems.append(
                f"{sheet_name}.{key} no longer points at {label} - "
                f"the automation chain is broken")
        checked += 1
    notes.append(f"automation chain: {checked} links verified")


def check_id_joins(wb, problems: list, notes: list) -> None:
    """Four IDs hold the workbook together. Verify them both ways.

    Customer ID, Property ID, Job ID and Invoice ID are the join keys.
    Each one is minted by exactly one register, as a formula nobody can
    overtype, and every other register that refers to it picks it from
    a drop-down.  Break that and the twenty-three tabs stop being a
    business manager and become twenty-three unrelated lists.
    """
    specs = _specs()
    hubs = [
        ("Customer CRM", "id", "CustomerPrefix", "customer",
         "CustomerList"),
        ("Property Database", "id", "PropertyPrefix", "property",
         "PropertyList"),
        ("Job & Work Orders", "id", "JobPrefix", "job", "JobIDList"),
        ("Invoice & Payments", "id", "InvoicePrefix", "invoice",
         "InvoiceIDList"),
    ]
    joined_total = 0
    for home, key, prefix, ref_key, list_name in hubs:
        spec = specs.get(home)
        if spec is None:
            problems.append(f"the {home} register has gone")
            continue
        column = next((c for c in spec.columns if c.key == key), None)
        if column is None or not column.formula:
            problems.append(
                f"{home}.{key} is not a formula - the ID has to be "
                f"minted by the workbook, not typed, or two rows end "
                f"up sharing one key")
            continue
        if prefix not in column.formula:
            problems.append(
                f"{home}.{key} no longer reads {prefix}, so the ID "
                f"prefix on Settings & Lists does nothing")
        # every register that refers to this hub must do it by ID
        joined = []
        for name, other in specs.items():
            if name == home:
                continue
            col = next((c for c in other.columns if c.key == ref_key),
                       None)
            if col is None or col.formula:
                continue                 # derived, not chosen
            joined.append(name)
            source = (col.validate or {}).get("source")
            if not (isinstance(source, str)
                    and source.lstrip("=") == list_name):
                problems.append(
                    f"{name}.{ref_key} is not a {list_name} "
                    f"drop-down, so a buyer can mistype the ID and "
                    f"silently disconnect the row from "
                    f"{home}")
        joined_total += len(joined)
        if ref_key == "customer" and len(joined) < 5:
            problems.append(
                f"only {len(joined)} registers join to the customer "
                f"by ID; the design calls for at least 5")
        if ref_key == "job" and len(joined) < 4:
            problems.append(
                f"only {len(joined)} registers join to the job by ID; "
                f"the design calls for at least 4")
    notes.append(f"ID joins: {joined_total} register columns joined "
                 f"through Customer, Property, Job and Invoice IDs")


def check_decisions(wb, problems: list, notes: list) -> None:
    """The decisions this product is not allowed to get wrong.

    Every one of them is a way a landscaping spreadsheet quietly starts
    lying to its owner, and every one is checked against the real
    formulas rather than against a promise in the documentation.
    """
    specs = _specs()
    held = []

    def column(sheet, key):
        spec = specs.get(sheet)
        if spec is None:
            problems.append(f"the {sheet} register has gone")
            return None
        col = next((c for c in spec.columns if c.key == key), None)
        if col is None:
            problems.append(f"{sheet}.{key} has gone")
        return col

    def needs(sheet, key, terms, why):
        col = column(sheet, key)
        if col is None or not col.formula:
            problems.append(f"{sheet}.{key} is no longer a formula "
                            f"- {why}")
            return False
        missing = [t for t in terms if t not in col.formula]
        if missing:
            problems.append(
                f"{sheet}.{key} no longer uses {', '.join(missing)} "
                f"- {why}")
            return False
        return True

    # 1. the quote is built from the job, not guessed
    ok = needs("Quote Calculator", "total_cost",
               ("{labour_cost}", "{materials_used}",
                "{equipment_cost}", "{travel_cost}",
                "{overhead_cost}"),
               "a quote has to cost labour, materials, equipment, "
               "travel and overhead or it is a guess")
    ok &= needs("Quote Calculator", "calc_price",
                ("{margin_used}", "MinimumVisit"),
                "the price has to come off the cost at the margin you "
                "asked for, and never go under your minimum visit")
    ok &= needs("Quote Calculator", "area",
                ("Property Database.lawn_area",),
                "lawn size has to come from the property record")
    if ok:
        held.append("quote priced from size, labour, materials, "
                    "equipment, travel and margin")

    # 2. estimated against actual, on every job
    ok = needs("Job & Work Orders", "hours_actual",
               ("{hours_logged}", "{hours_typed}"),
               "actual hours must prefer the crew's timesheets")
    ok &= needs("Job & Work Orders", "cost",
                ("{labour_cost}", "{expense_cost}"),
                "the real cost of a job is its timesheets and its "
                "expenses")
    ok &= needs("Job & Work Orders", "cost_var",
                ("{cost_est}", "{cost}"),
                "estimated against actual is the whole point of "
                "job-level profitability")
    ok &= needs("Job & Work Orders", "hours_var",
                ("{hours_est}", "{hours_actual}"),
                "estimated hours against actual hours")
    if ok:
        held.append("job profit compares estimated with actual")

    # 3. the recurring schedule works itself out
    ok = needs("Recurring Contracts", "next_num",
               ("{gap_days}", "{last_num}"),
               "the next visit date has to follow from the frequency "
               "and the last visit")
    ok &= needs("Recurring Contracts", "is_due",
                ("UpcomingDays", "{booked_next}"),
                "a contract is only due if nothing is booked yet")
    if ok:
        held.append("recurring contracts schedule themselves")

    # 4. equipment warns on hours AND on months
    ok = needs("Equipment Inventory", "service_due",
               ("{hours_left}", "{days_to_service}"),
               "a service falls due on engine hours or on the "
               "calendar, whichever comes first")
    ok &= needs("Equipment Inventory", "service_soon",
                ("ServiceWarnHours", "ServiceWarnDays"),
                "the warning window has to work both ways too")
    if ok:
        held.append("equipment warns on hours and on months")

    # 5. stock and cost are derived, never typed over
    materials = specs.get("Materials Inventory")
    if materials is not None:
        for key in ("purchased", "used", "on_hand"):
            col = next((c for c in materials.columns if c.key == key),
                       None)
            if col is None or not col.formula:
                problems.append(
                    f"Materials Inventory.{key} is typed, not derived "
                    f"- material stock has to come from the purchases "
                    f"and usage logged in the Expense Tracker")
                break
        else:
            held.append("material stock derived from expenses")
    jobs = specs.get("Job & Work Orders")
    if jobs is not None:
        for key in ("labour_cost", "expense_cost", "cost", "profit",
                    "margin_pct", "charge"):
            col = next((c for c in jobs.columns if c.key == key), None)
            if col is None or not col.formula:
                problems.append(
                    f"Job & Work Orders.{key} is typed, not derived - "
                    f"job money has to follow from the timesheets, "
                    f"the expenses and the agreed price")
                break
        else:
            held.append("job money derived, not typed")

    # 6. the route planner is a view of the jobs, not a second diary
    route = [c for c in (jobs.columns if jobs else [])
             if c.key in ("route_key", "route_seq", "route_match")]
    if len(route) < 3:
        problems.append(
            "Job & Work Orders has lost its route_key/route_seq/"
            "route_match columns, so the Route Planner would need its "
            "own copy of the day's work")
    elif not all(c.formula for c in route):
        problems.append("the route columns are typed, not derived")
    else:
        match = next(c for c in route if c.key == "route_match")
        if "RouteDate" not in (match.formula or "") or \
                "RouteCrew" not in (match.formula or ""):
            problems.append(
                "Job & Work Orders.route_match no longer reads "
                "RouteDate and RouteCrew, so the Route Planner cannot "
                "pick a day and a crew")
        else:
            held.append("route planner reads the job register")

    notes.append(f"decisions held: {len(held)} of 7 - " +
                 "; ".join(held))


def check_promises(wb, problems: list, notes: list) -> None:
    """Four promises this product makes to the buyer in writing.

    1. The route planner organises stops by hand.  It is not GPS
       navigation and it does not optimise anything, and saying
       otherwise is the single most common lie in this category.
    2. It is a workbook, not an integration: it never syncs with
       accounting, payroll or booking software.
    3. Payroll totals are gross figures for pricing, not a filing.
    4. The tax and accounting figures point at a real accountant.
    """
    banned_route = ("gps navigation", "gps route", "turn-by-turn",
                    "turn by turn", "optimises your route",
                    "optimizes your route", "optimised route",
                    "optimized route", "route optimisation",
                    "route optimization", "best route",
                    "shortest route", "live traffic", "satnav",
                    "sat nav", "automatic routing")
    banned_sync = ("syncs with", "synchronise with", "synchronize with",
                   "live feed", "api connection",
                   "automatically imports", "real-time", "real time "
                   "update", "connects to quickbooks",
                   "connects to xero", "connects to your bank",
                   "two-way sync", "automatic sync", "files your tax",
                   "files your payroll", "runs your payroll")
    banned_magic = ("updates itself", "updates automatically from",
                    "pulls your bookings", "imports your bookings",
                    "downloads your jobs", "knows the weather",
                    "weather feed")
    route_hits = sync_hits = magic_hits = 0
    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        for row in ws.iter_rows():
            for cell in row:
                value = cell.value
                if not isinstance(value, str) or value.startswith("="):
                    continue
                low = value.lower()
                negated = ("not " in low or "never " in low
                           or "cannot" in low or "does not" in low
                           or "no " in low or "nothing " in low
                           or "without " in low or "doesn't" in low
                           or "there is no" in low)
                for phrase in banned_route:
                    if phrase in low and not negated:
                        route_hits += 1
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: claims "
                            f"navigation or route optimisation -> "
                            f"{value[:70]}")
                for phrase in banned_sync:
                    if phrase in low and not negated:
                        sync_hits += 1
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: claims a "
                            f"live connection to other software -> "
                            f"{value[:70]}")
                for phrase in banned_magic:
                    if phrase in low and not negated:
                        magic_hits += 1
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: claims "
                            f"the workbook knows something nobody "
                            f"told it -> {value[:70]}")

    wanted = {
        "entered by you": ("you enter", "you type", "typed in",
                           "enter them", "you record", "you give"),
        "no GPS, no optimisation": ("not gps", "no gps",
                                    "not optimise", "not optimize",
                                    "does not optimise",
                                    "does not optimize"),
        "no accounting or payroll sync": ("does not sync",
                                          "not sync with",
                                          "no connection",
                                          "does not connect",
                                          "not connect"),
        "payroll is not a filing": ("not a payroll", "payroll filing",
                                    "whoever runs your payroll",
                                    "not payroll software"),
        "accountant": ("accountant",),
    }
    found = {key: False for key in wanted}
    for title in ("Getting Started", "Settings & Lists",
                  "Route Planner", "Timesheets & Payroll",
                  "Profit Analysis", "Dashboard"):
        if title not in wb.sheetnames:
            continue
        for row in wb[title].iter_rows():
            for cell in row:
                value = cell.value
                if not isinstance(value, str):
                    continue
                low = value.lower()
                for key, needles in wanted.items():
                    if any(n in low for n in needles):
                        found[key] = True
    for key, ok in found.items():
        if not ok:
            problems.append(f"the workbook never states the "
                            f"'{key}' honesty note")
    notes.append(f"product promises: {route_hits} navigation claims, "
                 f"{sync_hits} false sync claims, {magic_hits} "
                 f"self-updating claims, honesty notes "
                 f"{sum(found.values())}/{len(found)} present")


def check(path: str) -> int:
    problems: list = list(check_raw_xml(path))
    notes: list = []
    if problems:
        print(f"--- {path}")
        for problem in problems[:40]:
            print(f"    - {problem}")
        return 1
    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=False)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- structure
    names = wb.sheetnames
    if names != EXPECTED_SHEETS:
        problems.append(f"sheet order/={names}")
    hidden = [ws.title for ws in wb.worksheets if ws.sheet_state != "visible"]
    if hidden:
        problems.append(f"no tab should be hidden, got {hidden}")

    check_chain(wb, problems, notes)
    check_pages(wb, problems, notes)
    check_id_joins(wb, problems, notes)
    check_decisions(wb, problems, notes)
    check_promises(wb, problems, notes)

    # --------------------------------------------------------- properties
    props = wb.properties
    if props.creator != "Novality Store":
        problems.append(f"creator is {props.creator!r}")
    if props.lastModifiedBy != "Novality Store":
        problems.append(f"lastModifiedBy is {props.lastModifiedBy!r}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------- protection
    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        prot = ws.protection
        if not prot.sheet:
            problems.append(f"{ws.title}: sheet protection off")
        elif not prot.password:
            problems.append(f"{ws.title}: protected without a password")

    # -------------------------------------------------------- definednames
    defined = set(wb.defined_names)
    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        defined |= set(ws.defined_names)
    for name in REQUIRED_LIST_NAMES:
        if name not in defined:
            problems.append(f"drop down list {name} is missing")
    notes.append(f"{len(defined)} defined names")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ formulas
    used_names: Counter = Counter()
    unknown_functions: Counter = Counter()
    bare_refs: dict = {}
    bad_functions: Counter = Counter()
    formula_cells = 0
    locked_formula_problem = 0
    unlocked_input_cells = 0
    per_sheet = defaultdict(int)

    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        for row in ws.iter_rows():
            for cell in row:
                value = cell.value
                if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("="):
                    formula_cells += 1
                    per_sheet[ws.title] += 1
                    if "[[" in value or "{" in value or "}" in value:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: unresolved "
                            f"template token -> {value[:90]}")
                    if value != value.strip():
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: formula has "
                            f"stray whitespace around it, which some "
                            f"calculation engines refuse to parse -> "
                            f"{value[:70]!r}")
                    if "#REF" in value:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: #REF -> "
                            f"{value[:90]}")
                    where = f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}"
                    if (cell.protection and not cell.protection.locked
                            and where not in SEEDED_INPUTS):
                        locked_formula_problem += 1
                        if locked_formula_problem < 25:
                            problems.append(
                                f"{where}: formula cell is NOT locked")
                    stripped = re.sub(r'"[^"]*"', '""', value)
                    stripped = re.sub(r"'[^']*'!", "SHEET!", stripped)
                    if value.count('"') % 2:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: odd number of "
                            f"quotes -> {value[:80]}")
                    depth = paren_depth(value)
                    if depth:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: unbalanced "
                            f"brackets ({depth:+d}) -> {value[:80]}")
                    slip = precedence_slip(value)
                    if slip:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: a+b/c where "
                            f"(a+b)/c was meant -> {slip[:90]}")
                    collapsed = collapsed_criteria(value)
                    if collapsed:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: a criteria "
                            f"range and its test were written as one "
                            f"argument, Excel answers #VALUE! -> "
                            f"{collapsed[:90]}")
                    ragged = ragged_criteria(value)
                    if ragged:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: criteria "
                            f"ranges are different sizes, Excel answers "
                            f"#VALUE! -> {ragged[:90]}")
                    if ",)" in stripped or "(," in stripped or \
                            ",," in stripped:
                        problems.append(
                            f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate}: empty argument "
                            f"-> {value[:80]}")
                    for func in FUNCTION_RE.findall(stripped):
                        if func in MODERN_ONLY:
                            bad_functions[func] += 1
                        elif func not in KNOWN_FUNCTIONS:
                            unknown_functions[func] += 1
                    for token in NAME_RE.findall(stripped):
                        if token in KNOWN_FUNCTIONS:
                            continue
                        used_names[token] += 1
                    for token in BARE_COL_RE.findall(stripped):
                        if token in KNOWN_FUNCTIONS:
                            continue
                        bare_refs[f"{ws.title}!{cell.coordinate} {token}"] = \
                            value[:90]
                elif value is not None and cell.protection and \
                        not cell.protection.locked:
                    unlocked_input_cells += 1

    # sheet names used inside formulas must exist
    sheet_refs = Counter()
    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        for row in ws.iter_rows():
            for cell in row:
                value = cell.value
                if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("="):
                    body = re.sub(r'"[^"]*"', '""', value)
                    for ref in re.findall(r"'([^']+)'!", body):
                        sheet_refs[ref] += 1
                    for ref in re.findall(r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9_'])"
                                          r"([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*)!", body):
                        sheet_refs[ref] += 1
    for ref, count in sheet_refs.items():
        if ref not in names:
            problems.append(f"formula points at missing sheet "
                            f"'{ref}' ({count}x)")
    notes.append("cross sheet references: " + ", ".join(
        f"{k} {v}" for k, v in sorted(sheet_refs.items(),
                                      key=lambda kv: -kv[1])[:6]))

    for where, formula in list(bare_refs.items())[:15]:
        if where.rsplit(" ", 1)[-1] not in defined:
            problems.append(f"{where}: column letter with no row "
                            f"number -> {formula}")
    for func, count in unknown_functions.items():
        problems.append(f"unknown function {func}() used {count}x")
    for func, count in bad_functions.items():
        problems.append(f"{func}() needs a modern Excel - used {count}x")

    missing = {}
    for token, count in used_names.items():
        if token in defined:
            continue
        if token in KNOWN_FUNCTIONS or token.upper() in KNOWN_FUNCTIONS:
            continue
        if re.match(r"^[A-Z]{1,3}\d+$", token):
            continue
        missing[token] = count
    for token, count in sorted(missing.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])[:25]:
        problems.append(f"undefined name used {count}x: {token}")

    # ----------------------------------------------------- data validation
    for ws in wb.worksheets:
        for dv in ws.data_validations.dataValidation:
            formula = (dv.formula1 or "").lstrip("=")
            if dv.type == "list" and formula and \
                    re.match(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*$", formula):
                if formula not in defined:
                    problems.append(
                        f"{ws.title}: drop down uses missing name "
                        f"{formula}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- report
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as zf:
        size = sum(i.file_size for i in zf.infolist())
    notes.append(f"{formula_cells:,} formula cells, "
                 f"{unlocked_input_cells:,} unlocked value cells")
    notes.append(f"uncompressed xml {size / 1_000_000:.1f} MB")

    print(f"--- {path}")
    for note in notes:
        print(f"  . {note}")
    print("  . formulas per sheet: " + ", ".join(
        f"{k} {v}" for k, v in sorted(per_sheet.items(),
                                      key=lambda kv: -kv[1])[:10]))
    if problems:
        print(f"  ! {len(problems)} problem(s):")
        for problem in problems[:60]:
            print(f"    - {problem}")
        return 1
    print("  OK - no problems found")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    target = (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1
              else "output/landscaping.xlsx")
    raise SystemExit(check(target))
````

### `tools/check_parens.py`

*121 lines · 4 functions*

> Parenthesis and quote balance check for every formula in a workbook.
>
> Concatenated nested IFs are the one mistake a generator makes that no
> amount of reading catches, so this walks the saved .xlsx and counts
> brackets and quotes on every formula cell.
>
>     python3 tools/check_parens.py output/Workbook.xlsx

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `sheet_names(book)` | target path -> sheet name, in workbook order |
| `balanced(formula)` | Is the formula balanced for brackets and double quotes? |
| `suspicious(formula)` | Cheap smells that are always bugs in a generated workbook |
| `check(path)` |  |

**Module constants:** `NS` ('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/ma...).

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Parenthesis and quote balance check for every formula in a workbook.

Concatenated nested IFs are the one mistake a generator makes that no
amount of reading catches, so this walks the saved .xlsx and counts
brackets and quotes on every formula cell.

    python3 tools/check_parens.py output/Workbook.xlsx
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
import zipfile
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Tuple
from xml.etree import ElementTree as ET

NS = "{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"


def sheet_names(book: zipfile.ZipFile) -> Dict[str, str]:
    """target path -> sheet name, in workbook order."""
    rels = ET.fromstring(book.read("xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels"))
    by_id = {
        rel.get("Id"): rel.get("Target").lstrip("/")
        for rel in rels
    }
    root = ET.fromstring(book.read("xl/workbook.xml"))
    names: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for sheet in root.iter(f"{NS}sheet"):
        rid = sheet.get(
            "{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/"
            "2006/relationships}id")
        target = by_id.get(rid, "")
        if not target.startswith("xl/"):
            target = "xl/" + target
        names[target] = sheet.get("name", "?")
    return names


def balanced(formula: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """Is the formula balanced for brackets and double quotes?"""
    depth = 0
    in_string = False
    index = 0
    while index < len(formula):
        char = formula[index]
        if char == '"':
            if in_string and index + 1 < len(formula) \
                    and formula[index + 1] == '"':
                index += 2
                continue
            in_string = not in_string
        elif not in_string:
            if char == "(":
                depth += 1
            elif char == ")":
                depth -= 1
                if depth < 0:
                    return False, "closes a bracket that never opened"
        index += 1
    if in_string:
        return False, "odd number of double quotes"
    if depth:
        return False, f"{depth} bracket(s) left open"
    return True, ""


def suspicious(formula: str) -> str:
    """Cheap smells that are always bugs in a generated workbook."""
    if "{" in formula or "}" in formula:
        return "unrendered template placeholder"
    if "[[" in formula or "]]" in formula:
        return "unrendered sheet reference"
    if re.search(r"#(REF|NAME|VALUE|DIV/0|N/A)", formula):
        return "hard coded error value"
    if ",," in formula.replace('",,"', ""):
        return "empty argument"
    return ""


def check(path: str) -> int:
    problems: List[str] = []
    total = 0
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as book:
        names = sheet_names(book)
        targets: Iterable[str] = [n for n in book.namelist()
                                  if n.startswith("xl/worksheets/sheet")]
        for target in sorted(targets):
            sheet = names.get(target, target)
            root = ET.fromstring(book.read(target))
            for cell in root.iter(f"{NS}c"):
                node = cell.find(f"{NS}f")
                if node is None or not node.text:
                    continue
                total += 1
                formula = node.text
                ok, why = balanced(formula)
                smell = suspicious(formula)
                if not ok or smell:
                    ref = cell.get("r", "?")
                    problems.append(
                        f"{sheet}!{ref}: {why or smell}\n    ={formula[:160]}")
    print(f"{path}: {total:,} formulas checked")
    if problems:
        print(f"  {len(problems)} problem(s):")
        for line in problems[:40]:
            print("  - " + line)
        if len(problems) > 40:
            print(f"  ... and {len(problems) - 40} more")
        return 1
    print("  all balanced, no placeholders left behind")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print(__doc__)
        raise SystemExit(2)
    raise SystemExit(max(check(arg) for arg in sys.argv[1:]))
````

### `tools/check_landscaping_specs.py`

*206 lines · 5 functions*

> Fast structural sweep over the landscaping product's register specs.
>
> Runs in well under a second and catches, before a workbook is ever
> built, the four mistakes that are expensive to find later:
>
> 1. a formula whose brackets do not balance (concatenated nested IFs
>    lose a closing paren silently);
> 2. a `{token}` that names no column on that sheet;
> 3. `a+b/c` where `(a+b)/c` was meant;
> 4. a COUNTIFS/SUMIFS criteria range that carries its own test.
>
>     python3 tools/check_landscaping_specs.py

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `_strings_out(text)` |  |
| `_args(text, start)` | Split the argument list of a call whose '(' is at `start` |
| `collapsed_criteria(formula)` |  |
| `precedence_slip(formula)` | `a+b/c` at the top level of a division - almost always a bug |
| `main()` |  |

**Module constants:** `TOKEN` (re.compile(...)), `CALL` (re.compile(...)), `XREF` (re.compile(...)), `ENGINE_TOKENS` ({'r', 'r_prev', 'first', 'last'}), `TIME_DIVISORS` (8 items), `COMPLEMENT` (re.compile(...)), `DIVISOR` (re.compile(...)).

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Fast structural sweep over the landscaping product's register specs.

Runs in well under a second and catches, before a workbook is ever
built, the four mistakes that are expensive to find later:

1. a formula whose brackets do not balance (concatenated nested IFs
   lose a closing paren silently);
2. a `{token}` that names no column on that sheet;
3. `a+b/c` where `(a+b)/c` was meant;
4. a COUNTIFS/SUMIFS criteria range that carries its own test.

    python3 tools/check_landscaping_specs.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from landscaping_spreadsheet.config import Config     # noqa: E402

TOKEN = re.compile(r"\{([a-z_][a-z0-9_]*)\}")
CALL = re.compile(r"\b(COUNTIFS|SUMIFS|AVERAGEIFS)\s*\(")
XREF = re.compile(r"\[\[([^\].#]+)\.([a-z_][a-z0-9_]*)(#\d*)?\]\]")

#: tokens the engine substitutes that are not column keys
ENGINE_TOKENS = {"r", "r_prev", "first", "last"}


def _strings_out(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r'"[^"]*"', '""', text)


def _args(text: str, start: int):
    """Split the argument list of a call whose '(' is at `start`."""
    depth, out, cur, i = 0, [], "", start
    while i < len(text):
        ch = text[i]
        if ch == "(":
            depth += 1
            if depth == 1:
                i += 1
                continue
        elif ch == ")":
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                out.append(cur)
                return out, i
        if depth == 1 and ch == ",":
            out.append(cur)
            cur = ""
        else:
            cur += ch
        i += 1
    return out, i


def collapsed_criteria(formula: str) -> str:
    body = _strings_out(formula)
    for match in CALL.finditer(body):
        name = match.group(1)
        args, _ = _args(body, match.end() - 1)
        first = 1 if name in ("SUMIFS", "AVERAGEIFS") else 0
        if (len(args) - first) % 2:
            return f"{name}: odd number of range/criteria arguments"
        for text in args[first::2]:
            if re.search(r"[<>=]", text):
                return f"{name}: criteria range carries a test -> {text[:40]}"
    return ""


#: Dividing by one of these is a unit conversion, not a mis-bracket:
#: a date serial plus a fraction of a day, or a rate spread over a
#: fixed number of days. Bracketing those the other way would be the
#: actual bug.
TIME_DIVISORS = ("1440", "86400", "24", "60", "365", "30", "12", "7")


#: `1-x/y` is the complement of a ratio - an on-time rate, an accuracy
#: score, a discount depth. The left operand is a bare number, and
#: `(1-x)/y` is not a thing anybody means.
COMPLEMENT = re.compile(r"(?:^|[(,=<>&*+-])\s*\d+(?:\.\d+)?$")

#: the divisor that follows a `/`, so `a/n + b/n` - two terms each
#: averaged over the same window - can be told apart from `(a+b)/n`
DIVISOR = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*|\d+(?:\.\d+)?")


def precedence_slip(formula: str) -> str:
    """`a+b/c` at the top level of a division - almost always a bug."""
    body = _strings_out(formula)
    for match in re.finditer(r"/", body):
        tail = body[match.start() + 1:match.start() + 8]
        if re.match(r"(%s)\b" % "|".join(TIME_DIVISORS), tail):
            continue
        divisor = DIVISOR.match(body[match.start() + 1:])
        divisor = divisor.group(0) if divisor else ""
        # walk left over the numerator at depth 0
        i, depth = match.start() - 1, 0
        while i >= 0:
            ch = body[i]
            if ch == ")":
                depth += 1
            elif ch == "(":
                if depth == 0:
                    break
                depth -= 1
            elif depth == 0 and ch in "+-" and i > 0 and \
                    body[i - 1] not in "(,*/+-<>=&":
                left = body[:i]
                if ch == "-" and COMPLEMENT.search(left):
                    break           # 1-x/y, a proportion complement
                if ch == "+" and divisor and \
                        left.rstrip().endswith("/" + divisor):
                    break           # a/n+b/n, two terms, one window
                return body[max(0, i - 25):match.start() + 12]
            i -= 1
    return ""


def main() -> int:
    from landscaping_spreadsheet.build import REGISTER_MODULES

    cfg = Config()
    problems = 0
    total_cols = 0
    total_formulas = 0
    total_xrefs = 0

    # every register's column keys, so a [[Sheet.key]] pointing at a
    # column that does not exist is caught here and not in Excel
    catalogue = {m.spec(cfg).name: {c.key for c in m.spec(cfg).columns}
                 for m in REGISTER_MODULES}

    for module in REGISTER_MODULES:
        spec = module.spec(cfg)
        keys = {c.key for c in spec.columns}
        formulas = [c for c in spec.columns if c.formula]
        total_cols += len(spec.columns)
        total_formulas += len(formulas)

        for column in formulas:
            text = column.formula
            if not isinstance(text, str):
                problems += 1
                print(f"{spec.name}.{column.key}: formula is "
                      f"{type(text).__name__}, not a string")
                continue
            where = f"{spec.name}.{column.key}"

            body = _strings_out(text)
            if body.count("(") != body.count(")"):
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: brackets {body.count('(')} open vs "
                      f"{body.count(')')} close")
            if text.count('"') % 2:
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: odd number of quotes")
            if text != text.strip():
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: stray whitespace around the formula")

            for token in set(TOKEN.findall(text)):
                if token in ENGINE_TOKENS or token in keys:
                    continue
                if token.endswith("_first") and token[:-6] in keys:
                    continue
                if token.endswith("_last") and token[:-5] in keys:
                    continue
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: {{{token}}} is not a column on this sheet")

            for sheet, key, _n in XREF.findall(text):
                total_xrefs += 1
                if sheet not in catalogue:
                    problems += 1
                    print(f"{where}: [[{sheet}.{key}]] - no such sheet")
                elif key not in catalogue[sheet]:
                    problems += 1
                    print(f"{where}: [[{sheet}.{key}]] - "
                          f"{sheet} has no column '{key}'")

            slip = collapsed_criteria(text)
            if slip:
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: {slip}")
            slip = precedence_slip(text)
            if slip:
                problems += 1
                print(f"{where}: a+b/c where (a+b)/c was meant -> {slip}")

        print(f"  {spec.name:26s} {len(spec.columns):3d} cols, "
              f"{len(formulas):3d} formulas, {spec.rows:5d} rows")

    print(f"\n{len(REGISTER_MODULES)} registers, {total_cols} columns, "
          f"{total_formulas} formula columns, {total_xrefs} cross-sheet "
          f"references, {problems} problems")
    return 1 if problems else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
````

### `tools/check_landscaping_sample.py`

*388 lines · 5 functions*

> Check the worked example before it is ever written to a workbook.
>
> Four things go wrong with sample data, and all four are silent:
>
> 1. a key that is not an input column on that register - the value is
>    dropped and the formula it landed on goes with it;
> 2. a value that is not a member of the drop-down list the column
>    validates against - Excel accepts it, then refuses to let the user
>    re-pick it;
> 3. more sample rows than the register has room for;
> 4. an ID pointing at a record that does not exist, or a narrative
>    that does not add up - stock used that was never bought, an
>    odometer that goes backwards, an invoice paid for more than it was
>    raised for.
>
>     python3 tools/check_landscaping_sample.py

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `_module_name(module)` |  |
| `check_rows(cfg)` | Keys, drop-down membership and capacity, register by register |
| `check_joins(cfg, rows_by_module, specs)` | Every ID must point at a row that will actually exist |
| `check_narrative(cfg, rows_by_module)` | Recompute the story in plain Python: stock, odometers, money |
| `main()` |  |

**Module constants:** `LIST_SOURCES` (43 entries), `ID_SOURCES` (6 entries), `FOREIGN_KEYS` (11 items).

````python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check the worked example before it is ever written to a workbook.

Four things go wrong with sample data, and all four are silent:

1. a key that is not an input column on that register - the value is
   dropped and the formula it landed on goes with it;
2. a value that is not a member of the drop-down list the column
   validates against - Excel accepts it, then refuses to let the user
   re-pick it;
3. more sample rows than the register has room for;
4. an ID pointing at a record that does not exist, or a narrative
   that does not add up - stock used that was never bought, an
   odometer that goes backwards, an invoice paid for more than it was
   raised for.

    python3 tools/check_landscaping_sample.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as _dt
import os
import re
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(
    os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from landscaping_spreadsheet import build as B           # noqa: E402
from landscaping_spreadsheet import config as C          # noqa: E402
from landscaping_spreadsheet import sample_data as S     # noqa: E402

Problem = Tuple[str, str]

#: a drop-down whose source is one of these names is checked against
#: the matching list in config.py
LIST_SOURCES: Dict[str, List[str]] = {
    "CustomerTypeList": C.CUSTOMER_TYPES,
    "LeadSourceList": C.LEAD_SOURCES,
    "PaymentTermsList": [row[0] for row in C.PAYMENT_TERMS],
    "PaymentMethodList": C.PAYMENT_METHODS,
    "CustomerStatusList": C.CUSTOMER_STATUSES,
    "PropertyTypeList": C.PROPERTY_TYPES,
    "TerrainList": C.TERRAIN_TYPES,
    "AccessList": C.ACCESS_TYPES,
    "PropertyStatusList": C.PROPERTY_STATUSES,
    "ServiceCategoryList": C.SERVICE_CATEGORIES,
    "PricingUnitList": C.PRICING_UNITS,
    "EquipmentCategoryList": C.EQUIPMENT_CATEGORIES,
    "SeasonList": C.SEASONS,
    "FrequencyList": [row[0] for row in C.FREQUENCIES],
    "CrewRoleList": C.CREW_ROLES,
    "ServiceStatusList": C.SERVICE_STATUSES,
    "QuoteStatusList": C.QUOTE_STATUSES,
    "JobStatusList": C.JOB_STATUSES,
    "PriorityList": C.PRIORITIES,
    "WeatherList": C.WEATHER_CONDITIONS,
    "ContractStatusList": C.CONTRACT_STATUSES,
    "WeekdayList": C.WEEKDAYS,
    "EmploymentTypeList": C.EMPLOYMENT_TYPES,
    "CrewStatusList": C.CREW_STATUSES,
    "CrewNameList": C.CREW_NAMES,
    "AbsenceList": C.ABSENCE_REASONS,
    "TaskTypeList": C.TASK_TYPES,
    "EquipmentStatusList": C.EQUIPMENT_STATUSES,
    "EquipmentConditionList": C.EQUIPMENT_CONDITIONS,
    "VehicleList": C.DEFAULT_VEHICLES,
    "VehicleCostList": C.VEHICLE_COST_TYPES,
    "MaterialCategoryList": C.MATERIAL_CATEGORIES,
    "MaterialUnitList": C.MATERIAL_UNITS,
    "MaterialStatusList": C.MATERIAL_STATUSES,
    "InvoiceStatusList": C.INVOICE_STATUSES,
    "ExpenseCategoryList": C.EXPENSE_CATEGORIES,
    "StockMoveList": C.STOCK_MOVES,
    "TaxCategoryList": C.TAX_CATEGORIES,
    "FeedbackSourceList": C.FEEDBACK_SOURCES,
    "FeedbackTypeList": C.FEEDBACK_TYPES,
    "FeedbackStatusList": C.FEEDBACK_STATUSES,
    "FeedbackCauseList": C.FEEDBACK_CAUSES,
    "CurrencyList": [row[0] for row in C.CURRENCIES]
    if C.CURRENCIES and isinstance(C.CURRENCIES[0], tuple)
    else C.CURRENCIES,
}

#: lists built from the sample data itself rather than from config
ID_SOURCES = {
    "CustomerList": ("customers", "CUS-", 4),
    "PropertyList": ("properties", "PRP-", 4),
    "CrewIDList": ("crew", "EMP-", 3),
    "MaterialIDList": ("materials", "MAT-", 4),
    "EquipmentIDList": ("equipment", "EQP-", 3),
    "ServiceList": ("services", "", 0),
}

#: free text ID columns, checked against the register they point at
FOREIGN_KEYS: List[Tuple[str, str, str, str, int]] = [
    ("jobs", "contract", "contracts", "CON-", 4),
    ("jobs", "quote", "quotes", "QTE-", 4),
    ("jobs", "invoice", "invoices", "INV-", 5),
    ("quotes", "job", "jobs", "JOB-", 5),
    ("quotes", "contract", "contracts", "CON-", 4),
    ("invoices", "job", "jobs", "JOB-", 5),
    ("invoices", "contract", "contracts", "CON-", 4),
    ("timesheets", "job", "jobs", "JOB-", 5),
    ("expenses", "job", "jobs", "JOB-", 5),
    ("feedback", "job", "jobs", "JOB-", 5),
    ("feedback", "property", "properties", "PRP-", 4),
]


def _module_name(module) -> str:
    return module.__name__.rsplit(".", 1)[1]


def check_rows(cfg) -> Tuple[List[Problem], Dict[str, Any],
                              Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Keys, drop-down membership and capacity, register by register."""
    problems: List[Problem] = []
    rows_by_module: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    specs: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    for module in B.REGISTER_MODULES:
        short = _module_name(module)
        spec = module.spec(cfg)
        specs[short] = spec
        rows = S.get(short, cfg)
        rows_by_module[short] = rows
        if not rows:
            problems.append((short, "no sample rows at all"))
            continue
        if len(rows) > spec.rows:
            problems.append((
                short,
                f"{len(rows)} sample rows but the register only has "
                f"room for {spec.rows} - the tail is dropped "
                f"silently"))

        inputs = {col.key: col for col in spec.columns
                  if not col.formula}
        formulas = {col.key for col in spec.columns if col.formula}
        for number, row in enumerate(rows, start=1):
            for key, value in row.items():
                if key in formulas:
                    problems.append((
                        short,
                        f"row {number}: '{key}' is a formula column"))
                    continue
                col = inputs.get(key)
                if col is None:
                    problems.append((
                        short, f"row {number}: '{key}' is not a "
                               f"column on this register"))
                    continue
                if value in (None, ""):
                    continue
                if col.style == "date" and not isinstance(
                        value, _dt.date):
                    problems.append((
                        short, f"row {number}: '{key}' should be a "
                               f"date, got {value!r}"))
                if col.style == "time" and not isinstance(
                        value, _dt.time):
                    problems.append((
                        short, f"row {number}: '{key}' should be a "
                               f"time, got {value!r}"))
                if col.style in ("money", "number", "number1", "int",
                                 "percent", "percent0", "rating") \
                        and isinstance(value, str):
                    problems.append((
                        short, f"row {number}: '{key}' should be a "
                               f"number, got {value!r}"))
                validate = col.validate or {}
                source = validate.get("source")
                if isinstance(source, list):
                    if value not in source:
                        problems.append((
                            short, f"row {number}: '{key}' = "
                                   f"{value!r} is not in {source}"))
                elif isinstance(source, str):
                    name = source.lstrip("=")
                    allowed = LIST_SOURCES.get(name)
                    if allowed is not None and value not in allowed:
                        problems.append((
                            short, f"row {number}: '{key}' = "
                                   f"{value!r} is not in {name}"))
    return problems, rows_by_module, specs


def check_joins(cfg, rows_by_module, specs) -> List[Problem]:
    """Every ID must point at a row that will actually exist."""
    problems: List[Problem] = []
    counts = {name: len(rows) for name, rows in rows_by_module.items()}

    def _check(module: str, key: str, target: str, prefix: str,
               digits: int) -> None:
        limit = counts.get(target, 0)
        for number, row in enumerate(rows_by_module.get(module, []),
                                     start=1):
            value = row.get(key)
            if not value:
                continue
            match = re.fullmatch(prefix + r"(\d{%d})" % digits,
                                 str(value))
            if match is None:
                problems.append((
                    module, f"row {number}: '{key}' = {value!r} is "
                            f"not a {prefix}... ID"))
                continue
            if not 1 <= int(match.group(1)) <= limit:
                problems.append((
                    module, f"row {number}: '{key}' = {value!r} "
                            f"points past the end of {target} "
                            f"({limit} rows)"))

    for name, (target, prefix, digits) in ID_SOURCES.items():
        if not prefix:
            continue
        for module in rows_by_module:
            spec_keys = {
                "CustomerList": "customer",
                "PropertyList": "property",
                "CrewIDList": "employee",
                "MaterialIDList": "material",
                "EquipmentIDList": "equipment",
            }.get(name)
            if spec_keys is None:
                continue
            # only check the column when it really is an ID column on
            # that register - `services.equipment` is a category
            column = next((col for col in specs[module].columns
                           if col.key == spec_keys), None)
            if column is None or column.style != "id":
                continue
            _check(module, spec_keys, target, prefix, digits)

    for module, key, target, prefix, digits in FOREIGN_KEYS:
        _check(module, key, target, prefix, digits)

    # a service name has to be in the catalogue
    catalogue = {row["name"] for row in rows_by_module["services"]}
    for module in ("jobs", "quotes", "contracts"):
        for number, row in enumerate(rows_by_module[module], start=1):
            service = row.get("service")
            if service and service not in catalogue:
                problems.append((
                    module, f"row {number}: service {service!r} is "
                            f"not in the Service Catalog"))

    # a property has to belong to the customer the job names
    owner = {f"PRP-{number:04d}": row["customer"]
             for number, row in enumerate(
                 rows_by_module["properties"], start=1)}
    for module in ("jobs", "quotes", "contracts"):
        for number, row in enumerate(rows_by_module[module], start=1):
            prop, customer = row.get("property"), row.get("customer")
            if prop and customer and owner.get(prop) != customer:
                problems.append((
                    module, f"row {number}: {prop} belongs to "
                            f"{owner.get(prop)}, not {customer}"))
    return problems


def check_narrative(cfg, rows_by_module) -> List[Problem]:
    """Recompute the story in plain Python: stock, odometers, money."""
    problems: List[Problem] = []

    # --- stock: you cannot use what you never bought -----------------
    materials = rows_by_module["materials"]
    balance = {f"MAT-{number:04d}": float(row.get("opening") or 0)
               for number, row in enumerate(materials, start=1)}
    moves = sorted(
        (row for row in rows_by_module["expenses"] if row.get("material")),
        key=lambda row: row["date"])
    for row in moves:
        key = row["material"]
        quantity = float(row.get("quantity") or 0)
        if row.get("movement") == "Purchase":
            balance[key] = balance.get(key, 0) + quantity
        else:
            balance[key] = balance.get(key, 0) - quantity
            if balance[key] < -0.001:
                problems.append((
                    "expenses",
                    f"{row['date']}: {key} goes to "
                    f"{balance[key]:.1f} - more used than bought"))

    # --- odometers only go forwards ----------------------------------
    last: Dict[str, float] = {}
    for row in sorted(rows_by_module["vehicles"],
                      key=lambda entry: entry["date"]):
        odometer = row.get("odometer")
        if not odometer:
            continue
        name = row["vehicle"]
        if name in last and odometer < last[name]:
            problems.append((
                "vehicles",
                f"{row['date']}: {name} odometer {odometer} is "
                f"below the previous {last[name]}"))
        last[name] = odometer

    # --- invoices: never paid more than they were raised for ---------
    for number, row in enumerate(rows_by_module["invoices"], start=1):
        subtotal = float(row.get("subtotal") or 0)
        paid = float(row.get("paid") or 0)
        if paid - subtotal > 0.01:
            problems.append((
                "invoices",
                f"row {number}: paid {paid} against a subtotal of "
                f"{subtotal}"))
        if row.get("status") == "Paid" and abs(paid - subtotal) > 0.01:
            problems.append((
                "invoices",
                f"row {number}: marked Paid but {paid} of "
                f"{subtotal} has arrived"))
        if row.get("paid_date") and not row.get("paid"):
            problems.append((
                "invoices",
                f"row {number}: has a payment date but no payment"))

    # --- jobs: completed work has hours somewhere --------------------
    hours_by_job: Dict[str, float] = {}
    for row in rows_by_module["timesheets"]:
        job = row.get("job")
        if job:
            hours_by_job[job] = hours_by_job.get(job, 0) + 1
    missing = 0
    for number, row in enumerate(rows_by_module["jobs"], start=1):
        if row.get("status") != "Completed":
            continue
        job_id = f"JOB-{number:05d}"
        if not row.get("hours_typed") and job_id not in hours_by_job:
            missing += 1
    if missing:
        problems.append((
            "jobs",
            f"{missing} completed visits have neither typed hours "
            f"nor a timesheet - they will look free"))

    # --- today has a round to print ----------------------------------
    today = cfg.today
    todays = [row for row in rows_by_module["jobs"]
              if row["date"] == today]
    if len(todays) < 4:
        problems.append((
            "jobs", f"only {len(todays)} jobs dated today - the "
                    f"Route Planner will print an empty sheet"))

    # --- the alerts the dashboard promises have something to show ----
    overdue = [row for row in rows_by_module["invoices"]
               if row.get("status") == "Overdue"]
    if len(overdue) < 2:
        problems.append(("invoices", "no overdue invoices to show"))
    if not any(row.get("status") == "Needs service"
               for row in rows_by_module["equipment"]):
        problems.append(("equipment", "nothing due a service"))
    if not any(row.get("status") == "Dormant"
               for row in rows_by_module["customers"]):
        problems.append(("customers", "nobody has gone quiet"))
    return problems


def main() -> int:
    cfg = C.Config()
    problems, rows, specs = check_rows(cfg)
    if rows:
        problems += check_joins(cfg, rows, specs)
        problems += check_narrative(cfg, rows)

    print(f"Sample data: {sum(len(r) for r in rows.values())} rows "
          f"across {len(rows)} registers")
    for name in sorted(rows):
        print(f"  {name:12s} {len(rows[name]):5d}")
    if not problems:
        print("\nNo problems found.")
        return 0
    print(f"\n{len(problems)} problem(s):")
    for where, what in problems[:80]:
        print(f"  [{where}] {what}")
    if len(problems) > 80:
        print(f"  ... and {len(problems) - 80} more")
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
````

### `tools/recalc_check.py`

*216 lines · 4 functions*

> Really calculate every formula in a generated workbook and report any
> cell that ends up as an Excel error (#REF!, #DIV/0!, #VALUE! ...).
>
> `validate_workbook.py` reads the file; this one *runs* it.  It loads the
> workbook into the `formulas` calculation engine, evaluates the whole
> dependency graph and then looks at the answer in every formula cell, so a
> formula that parses perfectly but divides by zero on an empty row is still
> caught.
>
>     pip install formulas openpyxl
>     python tools/recalc_check.py "output/Pet Sitting Business Spreadsheet.xlsx"
>
> Notes
> -----
> * The engine cannot resolve `OFFSET()` based defined names, so a working
>   copy of the file is made first with those names flattened to plain
>   ranges.  The copy is a test artefact - the real workbook keeps its
>   dynamic names and is never touched.
> * Only cells that actually hold a formula are judged, so help text that
>   merely mentions "#N/A" is not mistaken for a broken formula.
> * The engine is hungry: a full-size 400-row build is around 50,000
>   formulas and wants several GB of memory.  Check a smaller build
>   instead - the formulas are identical, there are just fewer rows of
>   them:
>
>       python build_pet_sitting_workbook.py --rows 120 -o /tmp/small.xlsx
>       python tools/recalc_check.py /tmp/small.xlsx
>
>   Pass --force to run it on a full-size file anyway.

| Defines | What it does |
|---------|--------------|
| `flatten_dynamic_names(src, dst)` | Save a copy of *src* at *dst* with OFFSET names turned into ranges |
| `formula_cells(path)` | `{(SHEET NAME upper, coordinate)}` for every cell holding a formula |
| `cell_text(value)` |  |
| `main(argv)` |  |

**Module constants:** `BIG` (25000), `ERROR_TEXT` (re.compile(...)), `DYNAMIC_NAME` (re.compile(...)).

````python
"""Really calculate every formula in a generated workbook and report any
cell that ends up as an Excel error (#REF!, #DIV/0!, #VALUE! ...).

`validate_workbook.py` reads the file; this one *runs* it.  It loads the
workbook into the `formulas` calculation engine, evaluates the whole
dependency graph and then looks at the answer in every formula cell, so a
formula that parses perfectly but divides by zero on an empty row is still
caught.

    pip install formulas openpyxl
    python tools/recalc_check.py "output/Pet Sitting Business Spreadsheet.xlsx"

Notes
-----
* The engine cannot resolve `OFFSET()` based defined names, so a working
  copy of the file is made first with those names flattened to plain
  ranges.  The copy is a test artefact - the real workbook keeps its
  dynamic names and is never touched.
* Only cells that actually hold a formula are judged, so help text that
  merely mentions "#N/A" is not mistaken for a broken formula.
* The engine is hungry: a full-size 400-row build is around 50,000
  formulas and wants several GB of memory.  Check a smaller build
  instead - the formulas are identical, there are just fewer rows of
  them:

      python build_pet_sitting_workbook.py --rows 120 -o /tmp/small.xlsx
      python tools/recalc_check.py /tmp/small.xlsx

  Pass --force to run it on a full-size file anyway.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import collections
import os
import re
import sys
import tempfile
import time

#: Formula count above which the engine needs more memory than a laptop
#: is likely to enjoy handing over.
BIG = 25000

ERROR_TEXT = re.compile(r"#(REF!|VALUE!|NAME\?|DIV/0!|NUM!|NULL!|N/A)")

# OFFSET(Sheet!$A$5,0,0,MAX(1,COUNTIF(Sheet!$A$5:$A$254,"?*")),1)
DYNAMIC_NAME = re.compile(
    r"^OFFSET\((?P<sheet>'[^']+'|[A-Za-z0-9_.]+)!"
    r"\$(?P<col>[A-Z]+)\$(?P<row>\d+),0,0,"
    r"MAX\(1,COUNTIF\([^,]+,\"\?\*\"\)\),1\)$")


def flatten_dynamic_names(src: str, dst: str) -> int:
    """Save a copy of *src* at *dst* with OFFSET names turned into ranges."""
    import warnings
    import openpyxl

    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
        book = openpyxl.load_workbook(src)
    changed = 0
    for _name, defined in list(book.defined_names.items()):
        match = DYNAMIC_NAME.match(defined.value or "")
        if not match:
            continue
        last = re.search(r":\$[A-Z]+\$(\d+)", defined.value)
        if not last:
            continue
        col = match.group("col")
        defined.value = (f"{match.group('sheet')}!"
                         f"${col}${match.group('row')}:${col}${last.group(1)}")
        changed += 1
    book.save(dst)
    return changed


def formula_cells(path: str) -> set:
    """`{(SHEET NAME upper, coordinate)}` for every cell holding a formula."""
    import warnings
    import openpyxl

    with warnings.catch_warnings():
        warnings.simplefilter("ignore")
        book = openpyxl.load_workbook(path)
    cells = set()
    for sheet in book.worksheets:
        upper = sheet.title.upper()
        for row in sheet.iter_rows():
            for cell in row:
                value = cell.value
                if isinstance(value, str) and value.startswith("="):
                    cells.add((upper, cell.coordinate))
    return cells


def cell_text(value) -> str:
    try:
        return str(value.value[0, 0])
    except Exception:
        try:
            return str(value.value)
        except Exception:
            return ""


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("workbook")
    parser.add_argument("--list", type=int, default=12, metavar="N",
                        help="how many example cells to print per error type")
    parser.add_argument("--keep", action="store_true",
                        help="keep the flattened working copy")
    parser.add_argument("--show", default="", metavar="CELLS",
                        help="also print the calculated value of these "
                             "cells, e.g. \"Dashboard!A10,Reports!B12\"")
    parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true",
                        help="check a big workbook even though the engine "
                             "will need several GB of memory")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    path = args.workbook
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        print(f"no such file: {path}")
        return 2
    try:
        import formulas
    except ImportError:
        print("this check needs the calculation engine: pip install formulas")
        return 2

    print(path, flush=True)
    started = time.time()
    folder = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="recalc_")
    working = os.path.join(folder, os.path.basename(path))
    count = flatten_dynamic_names(path, working)
    print(f"  . flattened {count} dynamic names into a working copy",
          flush=True)

    wanted = formula_cells(path)
    print(f"  . {len(wanted):,} formula cells to check", flush=True)
    if len(wanted) > BIG and not args.force:
        print("  ! that is a lot - the engine would need several GB of "
              "memory.\n"
              "    Check a smaller build instead (the formulas are the "
              "same, there\n"
              "    are just fewer rows of them):\n"
              "      python build_pet_sitting_workbook.py --rows 120 "
              "-o /tmp/small.xlsx\n"
              "      python tools/recalc_check.py /tmp/small.xlsx\n"
              "    or pass --force to go ahead anyway.")
        return 2

    model = formulas.ExcelModel().loads(working).finish()
    print(f"  . loaded in {time.time() - started:.0f}s", flush=True)
    clock = time.time()
    solution = model.calculate()
    print(f"  . calculated {len(solution):,} nodes in "
          f"{time.time() - clock:.0f}s", flush=True)

    book = os.path.basename(working).upper()
    counts = collections.Counter()
    examples = collections.defaultdict(list)
    seen = 0
    for key, value in solution.items():
        if "!" not in key or "]" not in key:
            continue
        sheet, _, coord = key.partition("!")
        sheet = sheet.split("]")[-1].rstrip("'").upper()
        if book and sheet.endswith(".XLSX"):
            continue
        coord = coord.replace("$", "")
        if (sheet, coord) not in wanted:
            continue
        seen += 1
        found = ERROR_TEXT.search(cell_text(value))
        if not found:
            continue
        token = f"{sheet} {found.group(0)}"
        counts[token] += 1
        if len(examples[token]) < args.list:
            examples[token].append(coord)

    if args.show:
        wanted_show = {}
        for item in args.show.split(","):
            item = item.strip()
            if not item:
                continue
            sheet, _, coord = item.rpartition("!")
            wanted_show[(sheet.strip("'").upper(),
                         coord.replace("$", "").upper())] = item
        print("  . values asked for:")
        for key, value in solution.items():
            if "!" not in key or "]" not in key:
                continue
            sheet, _, coord = key.partition("!")
            sheet = sheet.split("]")[-1].rstrip("'").upper()
            coord = coord.replace("$", "")
            label = wanted_show.get((sheet, coord))
            if label:
                print(f"    {label:28s} = {cell_text(value)[:70]}")

    print(f"  . {seen:,} of them evaluated by the engine", flush=True)
    if not counts:
        print("  OK - every formula worked out to a real answer")
        return 0
    print(f"  ! {sum(counts.values())} cell(s) ended up as an error:")
    for token, number in counts.most_common():
        print(f"    - {token} x{number}: {', '.join(examples[token])}")
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
````

---

## Appendix A. Data dictionary

Every column of every register tab, straight from the specs that build them: the Excel column letter, the header a buyer sees, the key formulas use, the number format and whether the cell is typed in, chosen from a drop down or calculated. Pages (dashboards, reports, printables) are not listed - they have no fixed grid.
